Who Handles Orientation? Investigating Invariance in Feature Matching
Authors: David Nordström, Johan Edstedt, Fredrik Kahl, Georg Bökman
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Finding matching keypoints between images is a core problem in 3D computer vision. However, modern matchers struggle with large in-plane rotations. A straightforward mitigation is to learn rotation invariance via data augmentation. However, it remains unclear at which stage rotation invariance should be incorporated. In this paper, we study this in the context of a modern sparse matching pipeline. We perform extensive experiments by training on a large collection of 3D vision datasets and evaluating on popular image matching benchmarks. Surprisingly, we find that incorporating rotation invariance already in the descriptor yields similar performance to handling it in the matcher. However, rotation invariance is achieved earlier in the matcher when it is learned in the descriptor, allowing for a faster rotation-invariant matcher. Further, we find that enforcing rotation invariance does not hurt upright performance when trained at scale. Finally, we study the emergence of rotation invariance through scale and find that increasing the training data size substantially improves generalization to rotated images. We release two matchers robust to in-plane rotations that achieve state-of-the-art performance on e.g. multi-modal (WxBS), extreme (HardMatch), and satellite image matching (SatAst). Code is available at https://github.com/davnords/loma.
中文标题/摘要
标题：谁负责方向处理？探究特征匹配中的不变性
在图像间寻找匹配的关键点是三维计算机视觉的核心问题。然而，现代匹配器在处理大平面旋转时表现不佳。一种直接的缓解方法是通过数据增强学习旋转不变性。但目前尚不清楚应在哪个阶段引入旋转不变性。本文在现代稀疏匹配流程的背景下研究了这一问题。我们通过在大量三维视觉数据集上训练并在流行图像匹配基准上评估，进行了广泛实验。令人惊讶的是，我们发现若在描述符中提前引入旋转不变性，其性能与在匹配器中处理旋转不变性相当。此外，当旋转不变性在描述符中学习时，匹配器能更早实现旋转不变性，从而获得更快的旋转不变匹配器。进一步地，我们发现当在大规模数据上训练时，强制引入旋转不变性并不会损害垂直方向的性能。最后，我们研究了旋转不变性随尺度的变化，发现增加训练数据量显著提升了对旋转图像的泛化能力。我们发布了两个对平面旋转鲁棒的匹配器，在多模态（WxBS）、极端（HardMatch）和卫星图像匹配（SatAst）等任务上达到最先进性能。代码见 https://github.com/davnords/loma。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that modern feature matchers struggle with large in-plane rotations, this study investigates when to incorporate rotation invariance in a sparse matching pipeline. The authors train on diverse 3D vision datasets and evaluate on standard benchmarks, finding that learning rotation invariance in the descriptor stage yields performance comparable to enforcing it in the matcher, while also enabling faster convergence. Crucially, enforcing invariance does not degrade upright image matching when trained at scale, and larger training sets significantly improve generalization to rotated inputs. The paper releases two rotation-robust matchers achieving state-of-the-art results on multi-modal, extreme, and satellite image matching tasks.
该研究探讨在稀疏图像匹配流程中何时引入旋转不变性，以解决现代匹配器对大平面旋转敏感的问题。作者通过实验对比在描述符或匹配器中嵌入旋转不变性的效果，发现描述符层面引入可获得相当性能但收敛更快、不变性更早出现。重要的是，训练规模下强制旋转不变性不会损害直立图像性能，且更大规模数据显著提升对旋转图像的泛化能力。研究发布了两个抗旋转匹配器，在WxBS、HardMatch和SatAst等基准上达到最先进水平。
Pair2Scene: Learning Local Object Relations for Procedural Scene Generation
Authors: Xingjian Ran, Shujie Zhang, Weipeng Zhong, Li Luo, Bo Dai
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating high-fidelity 3D indoor scenes remains a significant challenge due to data scarcity and the complexity of modeling intricate spatial relations. Current methods often struggle to scale beyond training distribution to dense scenes or rely on LLMs/VLMs that lack the ability for precise spatial reasoning. Building on top of the observation that object placement relies mainly on local dependencies instead of information-redundant global distributions, in this paper, we propose Pair2Scene, a novel procedural generation framework that integrates learned local rules with scene hierarchies and physics-based algorithms. These rules mainly capture two types of inter-object relations, namely support relations that follow physical hierarchies, and functional relations that reflect semantic links. We model these rules through a network, which estimates spatial position distributions of dependent objects conditioned on position and geometry of the anchor ones. Accordingly, we curate a dataset 3D-Pairs from existing scene data to train the model. During inference, our framework can generate scenes by recursively applying our model within a hierarchical structure, leveraging collision-aware rejection sampling to align local rules into coherent global layouts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework outperforms existing methods in generating complex environments that go beyond training data while maintaining physical and semantic plausibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pair2Scene：通过学习局部物体关系实现程序化场景生成
由于数据稀缺和建模复杂空间关系的难度，生成高保真度的3D室内场景仍是一个重大挑战。现有方法往往难以扩展到密集场景，或依赖于缺乏精确空间推理能力的LLM/VLM。基于物体布局主要依赖局部依赖而非信息冗余的全局分布这一观察，本文提出Pair2Scene，一种新颖的程序化生成框架，将学习到的局部规则与场景层次结构及基于物理的算法相结合。这些规则主要捕捉两类物体间关系：遵循物理层次的支撑关系，以及反映语义关联的功能关系。我们通过网络建模这些规则，根据锚定物体的位置和几何形状，估计依赖物体的空间位置分布。为此，我们从现有场景数据中构建了3D-Pairs数据集用于训练模型。推理时，框架通过在层次结构中递归应用模型生成场景，并利用碰撞感知的拒绝采样方法，将局部规则整合为连贯的全局布局。大量实验表明，该框架在生成超出训练数据的复杂环境时，优于现有方法，同时保持物理与语义合理性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating high-fidelity 3D indoor scenes due to data scarcity and the difficulty of modeling complex spatial relations, this paper introduces Pair2Scene, a procedural generation framework that learns local object relations to scale beyond training data. The method models two key inter-object relations—support and functional—using a neural network that predicts spatial distributions of dependent objects based on anchor objects’ positions and geometry. Trained on a curated 3D-Pairs dataset, the framework generates scenes recursively within a hierarchical structure, using collision-aware rejection sampling to ensure global coherence. Experimental results show Pair2Scene outperforms existing methods in generating complex, physically and semantically plausible scenes beyond training distributions.
为应对因数据稀缺和空间关系建模复杂导致的高保真3D室内场景生成难题，本文提出Pair2Scene框架，通过学习局部物体关系实现超出训练分布的场景生成。该方法建模两类关键物体关系——支撑关系与功能关系，利用神经网络根据锚定物体的位置与几何预测依赖物体的空间分布。模型在自建3D-Pairs数据集上训练，推理时通过层级结构递归生成场景，并结合碰撞感知拒绝采样确保全局一致性。实验表明，该框架在生成复杂且物理与语义合理的场景方面优于现有方法。
Physics-Informed State Space Models for Reliable Solar Irradiance Forecasting in Off-Grid Systems
Authors: Mohammed Ezzaldin Babiker Abdullah
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The stable operation of autonomous off-grid photovoltaic systems dictates reliance on solar forecasting algorithms that respect atmospheric thermodynamics. Contemporary deep learning models consistently exhibit critical anomalies, primarily severe temporal phase lags during cloud transients and physically impossible nocturnal power generation. To resolve this divergence between data-driven modeling and deterministic celestial mechanics, this research introduces the Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network. The proposed methodology projects 15 meteorological and geometric variables into a Koopman-linearized Riemannian manifold to systematically map complex climatic dynamics. The architecture integrates a Spectral Calibration unit and a multiplicative Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate. This system synthesizes real-time atmospheric opacity with theoretical clear-sky boundary models, structurally enforcing strict celestial geometry compliance. This completely neutralizes phantom nocturnal generation while maintaining zero-lag synchronization during rapid weather shifts. Validated against a rigorous five-year testing horizon in a severe semi-arid climate, the framework achieves an RMSE of 18.31 Wh/m2 and a Pearson correlation of 0.988. The model strictly maintains a zero-magnitude nocturnal error across all 1826 testing days and exhibits a sub-30-minute phase response during high-frequency transients. Comprising exactly 63,458 trainable parameters, this ultra-lightweight design establishes a robust, thermodynamically consistent standard for edge-deployable microgrid controllers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息的状态空间模型用于离网系统可靠太阳辐照度预测
自主离网光伏系统的稳定运行依赖于符合大气热力学的太阳辐照度预测算法。当前深度学习模型普遍存在严重问题，主要表现为云层变化时的时间相位滞后和不可能的夜间发电现象。为弥合数据驱动建模与确定性天体力学之间的差距，本研究提出热力学液体流形网络。该方法将15个气象和几何变量投影至Koopman线性化的黎曼流形，系统性映射复杂气候动态。架构集成频谱校准单元和乘法热力学Alpha门控。该系统结合实时大气不透明度与理论晴空边界模型，结构上强制遵守严格的天体几何约束，彻底消除虚假夜间发电，同时在快速天气变化期间保持零滞后同步。经在严酷半干旱气候下五年严格测试验证，该框架实现RMSE为18.31 Wh/m²，皮尔逊相关系数0.988。模型在全部1826个测试日中夜间误差严格为零，高频瞬变响应时间低于30分钟。该超轻量设计仅含63,458个可训练参数，为边缘部署微电网控制器建立了稳健且热力学一致的标准。
Summary / 总结
To address inaccuracies in solar irradiance forecasting for off-grid systems—particularly severe phase lags and impossible nocturnal power output—this study introduces the Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network, which embeds atmospheric thermodynamics into a Koopman-linearized Riemannian manifold using 15 meteorological and geometric inputs. The model integrates a Spectral Calibration unit and a Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate to enforce celestial geometry compliance, eliminating phantom nighttime generation and ensuring zero-lag synchronization during rapid weather changes. Evaluated over five years in a semi-arid climate, it achieved an RMSE of 18.31 Wh/m² and a Pearson correlation of 0.988, with zero nocturnal error across all 1,826 test days and sub-30-minute phase response during transients, while maintaining only 63,458 trainable parameters for edge deployment.
为提升离网光伏系统对太阳辐照度预测的物理一致性，该研究提出热力学液体流形网络，将15个气象与几何变量嵌入Koopman线性化黎曼流形中，结合光谱校准单元与热力学Alpha门控机制，强制符合天体几何规律，消除夜间虚假发电现象并实现快速天气变化下的零延迟同步。在半干旱地区五年测试中，模型RMSE达18.31 Wh/m²，皮尔逊相关系数0.988，1826天内夜间误差始终为零，高频突变响应低于30分钟，仅含63,458个可训练参数。
Detecting Safety Violations Across Many Agent Traces
Authors: Adam Stein, Davis Brown, Hamed Hassani, Mayur Naik, Eric Wong
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:40+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To identify safety violations, auditors often search over large sets of agent traces. This search is difficult because failures are often rare, complex, and sometimes even adversarially hidden and only detectable when multiple traces are analyzed together. These challenges arise in diverse settings such as misuse campaigns, covert sabotage, reward hacking, and prompt injection. Existing approaches struggle here for several reasons. Per-trace judges miss failures that only become visible across traces, naive agentic auditing does not scale to large trace collections, and fixed monitors are brittle to unanticipated behaviors. We introduce Meerkat, which combines clustering with agentic search to uncover violations specified in natural language. Through structured search and adaptive investigation of promising regions, Meerkat finds sparse failures without relying on seed scenarios, fixed workflows, or exhaustive enumeration. Across misuse, misalignment, and task gaming settings, Meerkat significantly improves detection of safety violations over baseline monitors, discovers widespread developer cheating on a top agent benchmark, and finds nearly 4x more examples of reward hacking on CyBench than previous audits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨多个代理轨迹检测安全违规
为识别安全违规，审计人员通常需在大量代理轨迹中搜索。此搜索困难，因为失败往往罕见、复杂，有时甚至被对抗性隐藏，仅在多轨迹联合分析时才可检测。此类挑战出现在多种场景中，如滥用攻击、隐蔽破坏、奖励黑客行为和提示注入。现有方法在此面临多重困难：单轨迹判断器无法发现仅在多轨迹中显现的失败；朴素的代理审计难以扩展至大规模轨迹集合；固定监控器对未预期行为脆弱。我们提出Meerkat，结合聚类与代理搜索，以自然语言指定违规行为。通过结构化搜索与对有潜力区域的自适应调查，Meerkat无需依赖种子场景、固定工作流或穷举枚举即可发现稀疏失败。在滥用、对齐偏差和任务游戏化场景中，Meerkat显著优于基线监控器，发现主流代理基准中广泛存在的开发者作弊行为，并在CyBench上发现近4倍于以往审计的奖励黑客案例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of detecting rare and complex safety violations across large agent trace collections, the authors propose Meerkat, a method that combines clustering with agentic search to uncover violations described in natural language. Unlike traditional per-trace judges or fixed monitors, Meerkat performs structured, adaptive exploration of promising regions without requiring seed scenarios or exhaustive enumeration. Experimental results show Meerkat significantly outperforms baseline monitors across misuse, misalignment, and task gaming scenarios, uncovering widespread developer cheating on a top agent benchmark and nearly four times more reward hacking examples on CyBench than prior audits.
为应对在大规模代理轨迹中检测罕见且复杂的违规行为的困难，Meerkat结合聚类与代理搜索，通过自然语言描述识别违规行为。与传统的单轨迹判断器或固定监控器不同，Meerkat进行结构化、自适应的有前景区域探索，无需预设场景或穷举枚举。实验结果表明，Meerkat在滥用、对齐偏差和任务游戏化场景中显著优于基线监控器，发现了顶级代理基准测试中广泛存在的开发者作弊行为，并在CyBench上比以往审计发现近四倍的奖励黑客行为。
Solving Physics Olympiad via Reinforcement Learning on Physics Simulators
Authors: Mihir Prabhudesai, Aryan Satpathy, Yangmin Li, Zheyang Qin, Nikash Bhardwaj, Amir Zadeh, Chuan Li, Katerina Fragkiadaki, Deepak Pathak
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:40+00:00
Comments: Project Webpage - https://sim2reason.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We have witnessed remarkable advances in LLM reasoning capabilities with the advent of DeepSeek-R1. However, much of this progress has been fueled by the abundance of internet question-answer (QA) pairs, a major bottleneck going forward, since such data is limited in scale and concentrated mainly in domains like mathematics. In contrast, other sciences such as physics lack large-scale QA datasets to effectively train reasoning-capable models. In this work, we show that physics simulators can serve as a powerful alternative source of supervision for training LLMs for physical reasoning. We generate random scenes in physics engines, create synthetic question-answer pairs from simulated interactions, and train LLMs using reinforcement learning on this synthetic data. Our models exhibit zero-shot sim-to-real transfer to real-world physics benchmarks: for example, training solely on synthetic simulated data improves performance on IPhO (International Physics Olympiad) problems by 5-10 percentage points across model sizes. These results demonstrate that physics simulators can act as scalable data generators, enabling LLMs to acquire deep physical reasoning skills beyond the limitations of internet-scale QA data. Code available at: https://sim2reason.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过物理模拟器上的强化学习解决物理奥林匹克竞赛
随着DeepSeek-R1的出现，我们见证了大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的显著进步。然而，这一进展很大程度上依赖于互联网问答（QA）对的丰富性，而这一资源在未来将成为主要瓶颈，因为此类数据规模有限，主要集中于数学等领域。相比之下，其他科学领域如物理学缺乏大规模的QA数据集，难以有效训练具备推理能力的模型。在本研究中，我们展示了物理模拟器可作为训练LLM进行物理推理的强大替代监督来源。我们通过物理引擎生成随机场景，从模拟交互中创建合成问答对，并使用强化学习在这些合成数据上训练LLM。我们的模型在真实世界物理基准测试中展现出零样本模拟到现实的迁移能力：例如，仅使用合成模拟数据训练，模型在国际物理奥林匹克竞赛（IPhO）问题上的表现提升5-10个百分点（不同模型规模）。这些结果表明，物理模拟器可作为可扩展的数据生成器，使LLM超越互联网规模QA数据的限制，获得深入的物理推理能力。代码见：https://sim2reason.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large-scale physics-specific QA data to train LLMs for physical reasoning, this work leverages physics simulators as a scalable alternative. It generates synthetic question-answer pairs by simulating random physical scenes and trains LLMs via reinforcement learning on this data. Experimental results show that models trained solely on simulated data achieve 5-10 percentage point improvements on real-world IPhO benchmarks, demonstrating effective zero-shot transfer and the potential of simulators to overcome limitations of internet-based QA datasets.
鉴于互联网上缺乏大规模物理领域问答数据，该研究利用物理模拟器作为可扩展的替代训练数据源。通过在物理引擎中生成随机场景并合成问答对，再以强化学习训练大语言模型。实验表明，仅使用模拟数据训练的模型在真实国际物理奥林匹克竞赛题上比基线提升5-10个百分点，验证了从仿真到现实的零样本迁移能力。
OmniShow: Unifying Multimodal Conditions for Human-Object Interaction Video Generation
Authors: Donghao Zhou, Guisheng Liu, Hao Yang, Jiatong Li, Jingyu Lin, Xiaohu Huang, Yichen Liu, Xin Gao, Cunjian Chen, Shilei Wen, Chi-Wing Fu, Pheng-Ann Heng
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:12+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://correr-zhou.github.io/OmniShow/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we study Human-Object Interaction Video Generation (HOIVG), which aims to synthesize high-quality human-object interaction videos conditioned on text, reference images, audio, and pose. This task holds significant practical value for automating content creation in real-world applications, such as e-commerce demonstrations, short video production, and interactive entertainment. However, existing approaches fail to accommodate all these requisite conditions. We present OmniShow, an end-to-end framework tailored for this practical yet challenging task, capable of harmonizing multimodal conditions and delivering industry-grade performance. To overcome the trade-off between controllability and quality, we introduce Unified Channel-wise Conditioning for efficient image and pose injection, and Gated Local-Context Attention to ensure precise audio-visual synchronization. To effectively address data scarcity, we develop a Decoupled-Then-Joint Training strategy that leverages a multi-stage training process with model merging to efficiently harness heterogeneous sub-task datasets. Furthermore, to fill the evaluation gap in this field, we establish HOIVG-Bench, a dedicated and comprehensive benchmark for HOIVG. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniShow achieves overall state-of-the-art performance across various multimodal conditioning settings, setting a solid standard for the emerging HOIVG task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniShow：统一多模态条件的人机交互视频生成
本研究探讨人机交互视频生成（HOIVG），旨在根据文本、参考图像、音频和姿态合成高质量的人机交互视频。该任务对电商演示、短视频制作和互动娱乐等现实应用中的内容自动化具有重要实用价值。然而，现有方法无法同时支持所有这些必要条件。我们提出OmniShow，一个专为这一实用且具有挑战性的任务设计的端到端框架，能够协调多模态条件并实现行业级性能。为解决可控性与质量之间的权衡，我们引入统一通道条件化以高效注入图像和姿态，并采用门控局部上下文注意力确保精确的音视频同步。为有效应对数据稀缺问题，我们开发了解耦后联合训练策略，通过多阶段训练与模型融合高效利用异构子任务数据集。此外，为填补该领域评估空白，我们建立了HOIVG-Bench，一个专门且全面的HOIVG基准。大量实验表明，OmniShow在各种多模态条件设置下均达到最先进性能，为新兴的HOIVG任务树立了坚实标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic human-object interaction videos in applications like e-commerce and entertainment, this work introduces OmniShow, an end-to-end framework that unifies text, image, audio, and pose conditioning. It employs Unified Channel-wise Conditioning for efficient multimodal input injection and Gated Local-Context Attention to synchronize audio and visuals precisely. To tackle data scarcity, it uses a Decoupled-Then-Joint Training strategy with model merging across sub-tasks. Evaluated on a newly created HOIVG-Bench, OmniShow achieves state-of-the-art performance across all multimodal settings, establishing a new benchmark for the field.
为满足电商演示和互动娱乐等场景对高质量人-物交互视频的需求，本文提出OmniShow框架，统一处理文本、图像、音频和姿态等多模态条件。该框架采用统一通道条件注入机制提升输入效率，并引入门控局部上下文注意力确保音画同步。为应对数据稀缺问题，采用分阶段解耦训练与模型融合策略。在新建立的HOIVG-Bench基准上，OmniShow在各类多模态条件下均达到当前最优性能，为该领域树立了新标杆。
Saar-Voice: A Multi-Speaker Saarbrücken Dialect Speech Corpus
Authors: Lena S. Oberkircher, Jesujoba O. Alabi, Dietrich Klakow, Jürgen Trouvain
First: 2026-04-13T17:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:59:11+00:00
Comments: accepted at DialRes-LREC26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Natural language processing (NLP) and speech technologies have made significant progress in recent years; however, they remain largely focused on standardized language varieties. Dialects, despite their cultural significance and widespread use, are underrepresented in linguistic resources and computational models, resulting in performance disparities. To address this gap, we introduce Saar-Voice, a six-hour speech corpus for the Saarbrücken dialect of German. The dataset was created by first collecting text through digitized books and locally sourced materials. A subset of this text was recorded by nine speakers, and we conducted analyses on both the textual and speech components to assess the dataset's characteristics and quality. We discuss methodological challenges related to orthographic and speaker variation, and explore grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion. The resulting corpus provides aligned textual and audio representations. This serves as a foundation for future research on dialect-aware text-to-speech (TTS), particularly in low-resource scenarios, including zero-shot and few-shot model adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：萨尔布吕肯方言语音语料库Saar-Voice
近年来，自然语言处理（NLP）和语音技术取得了显著进展，但主要聚焦于标准化语言变体。尽管方言在文化上具有重要意义且广泛使用，却在语言资源和计算模型中代表性不足，导致性能差异。为弥合这一差距，我们推出了Saar-Voice，一个六小时的德语萨尔布吕肯方言语音语料库。该语料库首先通过数字化书籍和本地材料收集文本，其中一部分文本由九位说话者录制。我们对文本和语音成分进行了分析，以评估语料库的特性和质量。我们讨论了与拼写和说话者变异相关的研究方法挑战，并探讨了音素到字符（G2P）转换。最终语料库提供了对齐的文本和音频表示，为未来方言感知的文本到语音（TTS）研究奠定了基础，特别是在低资源场景（包括零样本和少样本模型适配）中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underrepresentation of dialects in NLP and speech technologies, which leads to performance disparities, this study introduces Saar-Voice, a six-hour speech corpus for the Saarbrücken German dialect. The corpus was constructed by digitizing local texts and recording them by nine speakers, followed by analysis of both textual and audio components to evaluate quality and characteristics. Methodological challenges such as orthographic variation and speaker diversity were addressed, including grapheme-to-phoneme conversion. The resulting aligned text-audio dataset enables future research in dialect-aware text-to-speech systems, especially in low-resource settings like zero-shot and few-shot adaptation.
鉴于方言在自然语言处理和语音技术中长期被忽视，导致性能差异，本文提出了萨尔布吕肯德语方言语音语料库Saar-Voice。该语料库通过数字化本地文本并由九位说话者录制，结合文本与语音分析评估其质量与特性，解决了拼写变异和说话人差异等方法论挑战，包括音素-字符转换，最终生成对齐的文本-语音数据。实验结果表明，该语料库可有效支持方言感知的语音合成系统，尤其适用于零样本和小样本场景的模型适配。
Psychological Concept Neurons: Can Neural Control Bias Probing and Shift Generation in LLMs?
Authors: Yuto Harada, Hiro Taiyo Hamada
First: 2026-04-13T17:58:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:58:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using psychological constructs such as the Big Five, large language models (LLMs) can imitate specific personality profiles and predict a user's personality. While LLMs can exhibit behaviors consistent with these constructs, it remains unclear where and how they are represented inside the model and how they relate to behavioral outputs. To address this gap, we focus on questionnaire-operationalized Big Five concepts, analyze the formation and localization of their internal representations, and use interventions to examine how these representations relate to behavioral outputs. In our experiment, we first use probing to examine where Big Five information emerges across model depth. We then identify neurons that respond selectively to each Big Five concept and test whether enhancing or suppressing their activations can bias latent representations and label generation in intended directions. We find that Big Five information becomes rapidly decodable in early layers and remains detectable through the final layers, while concept-selective neurons are most prevalent in mid layers and exhibit limited overlap across domains. Interventions on these neurons consistently shift probe readouts toward targeted concepts, with targeted success rates exceeding 0.8 for some concepts, indicating that the model's internal separation of Big Five personality traits can be causally steered. At the label-generation level, the same interventions often bias generated label distributions in the intended directions, but the effects are weaker, more concept-dependent, and often accompanied by cross-trait spillover, indicating that comparable control over generated labels is difficult even with interventions on a large fraction of concept-selective neurons. Overall, our findings reveal a gap between representational control and behavioral control in LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：心理概念神经元：神经控制能否探测并引导大语言模型中的偏见与生成？
利用如大五人格等心理构念，大语言模型（LLMs）可模拟特定人格特征并预测用户人格。尽管LLMs可表现出与这些构念一致的行为，但其内部表示位置及与行为输出的关系尚不明确。为填补这一空白，我们聚焦问卷操作化的大五人格构念，分析其内部表示的形成与定位，并通过干预手段考察其与行为输出的关系。实验中，我们首先通过探测方法考察大五信息在模型深度中的出现位置；随后识别对每个大五构念选择性响应的神经元，并测试增强或抑制其激活是否能引导潜在表示与标签生成朝预期方向偏移。结果发现，大五信息在早期层迅速可解码，且贯穿至最终层仍可检测；概念选择性神经元主要集中在中层，且跨领域重叠有限。对这些神经元的干预可稳定将探测读数向目标构念偏移，部分构念的成功率超过0.8，表明模型内部大五人格特质可被因果引导。在标签生成层面，相同干预常使生成标签分布朝预期方向偏移，但效果较弱、依赖具体构念，且常伴随跨特质溢出，表明即使干预大量概念选择性神经元，对生成标签的控制仍较困难。总体而言，我们的研究揭示了LLMs中表征控制与行为控制之间的差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how psychological constructs like the Big Five are represented and controlled within large language models, this study investigates whether targeted neural interventions can steer both internal representations and behavioral outputs. Using probing across model layers and identifying concept-selective neurons, the researchers demonstrate that Big Five traits emerge early and remain decodable throughout the model, with neurons most active in mid-layers. Interventions on these neurons successfully bias latent representations toward targeted traits with high success rates, but their influence on generated labels is weaker, more variable, and often causes unintended trait spillover, revealing a disconnect between representational and behavioral control in LLMs.
该研究探讨了大语言模型中五大人格特质的心理学概念如何被表征与控制。研究人员通过探针技术定位这些特质在模型各层的出现位置，识别出对特定特质选择性响应的神经元，并通过增强或抑制这些神经元，证明内部表征可被因果性引导至目标特质（部分成功率达0.8以上），但标签生成效果较弱、依赖性强且常伴随跨特质干扰，揭示了LLM中表征控制与行为输出控制之间的差距。
CLSGen: A Dual-Head Fine-Tuning Framework for Joint Probabilistic Classification and Verbalized Explanation
Authors: WonJin Yoon, Kangyu Zhu, Ian Bulovic, Autumn Sehy, Yanjun Gao, Dmitriy Dligach, Majid Afshar, Timothy A. Miller
First: 2026-04-13T17:58:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:58:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the recent progress of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is a growing interest in applying these models to solve complex and challenging problems. Modern LLMs, capable of processing long contexts and generating verbalized explanations, offer significant potential in addressing real-world applications. However, a critical hurdle in deploying LLMs for practical decision-making is their inability to provide reliable, quantitative probabilities. While task-specific fine-tuning of LLMs using traditional discriminative objectives (similar to encoder-only models) can yield probability estimates, this often leads to catastrophic forgetting and linguistic collapse. Consequently, the model loses its ability to generate explanations, severely undermining its interpretability and usability. To address this challenge, we propose CLSGen, a novel LLM fine-tuning framework designed for binary classification tasks. The CLSGen framework encompasses a new model architecture, training methodology, and data construction strategy to enable robust probability estimation without sacrificing the model's inherent explanation-generation capabilities. Experimental results across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that models fine-tuned with CLSGen outperform existing baselines in classification metrics (AUROC and F1-score). Regarding explanation, the results showed strong alignment between predicted labels and generated justifications, as well as high readability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLSGen：一种用于联合概率分类与口语化解释的双头微调框架
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展，人们越来越关注将其应用于解决复杂和具有挑战性的问题。现代LLMs具备处理长上下文和生成口语化解释的能力，在应对现实世界应用方面具有巨大潜力。然而，在将LLMs部署于实际决策中时，一个关键障碍是其无法提供可靠、定量的概率。虽然使用传统判别性目标（类似于仅编码器模型）对LLMs进行任务特定微调可生成概率估计，但这通常会导致灾难性遗忘和语言崩溃，从而使模型丧失生成解释的能力，严重削弱其可解释性和可用性。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了CLSGen，一种专为二分类任务设计的新型LLM微调框架。该框架包含新的模型架构、训练方法和数据构建策略，旨在实现稳健的概率估计，同时不牺牲模型固有的解释生成能力。在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，使用CLSGen微调的模型在分类指标（AUROC和F1分数）上优于现有基线。在解释方面，结果表明预测标签与生成的解释高度一致，且可读性高。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLMs to provide reliable probability estimates while retaining their ability to generate verbalized explanations, the authors propose CLSGen, a dual-head fine-tuning framework for binary classification. CLSGen introduces a novel architecture with two heads—one for probabilistic classification and one for explanation generation—along with a specialized training strategy that avoids catastrophic forgetting and linguistic collapse. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that CLSGen fine-tuned models achieve superior classification performance (higher AUROC and F1-score) compared to baselines, while also producing explanations that strongly align with predicted labels and exhibit high readability.
为解决大语言模型在实际决策中缺乏可靠概率估计且丧失解释能力的问题，CLSGen提出一种双头微调框架，同时优化分类与解释生成。该框架通过创新训练策略，在保持概率预测准确性的同时，保留模型的自然语言解释能力。实验表明，CLSGen微调模型在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，分类指标（AUROC和F1分数）更优，且生成的解释与预测标签高度一致、语义清晰易读。
Budget-Aware Uncertainty for Radiotherapy Segmentation QA Using nnU-Net
Authors: Ricardo Coimbra Brioso, Lorenzo Mondo, Damiano Dei, Nicola Lambri, Pietro Mancosu, Marta Scorsetti, Daniele Loiacono
First: 2026-04-13T17:58:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:58:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate delineation of the Clinical Target Volume (CTV) is essential for radiotherapy planning, yet remains time-consuming and difficult to assess, especially for complex treatments such as Total Marrow and Lymph Node Irradiation (TMLI). While deep learning-based auto-segmentation can reduce workload, safe clinical deployment requires reliable cues indicating where models may be wrong. In this work, we propose a budget-aware uncertainty-driven quality assurance (QA) framework built on nnU-Net, combining uncertainty quantification and post-hoc calibration to produce voxel-wise uncertainty maps (based on predictive entropy) that can guide targeted manual review. We compare temperature scaling (TS), deep ensembles (DE), checkpoint ensembles (CE), and test-time augmentation (TTA), evaluated both individually and in combination on TMLI as a representative use case. Reliability is assessed through ROI-masked calibration metrics and uncertainty--error alignment under realistic revision constraints, summarized as AUC over the top 0-5% most uncertain voxels. Across configurations, segmentation accuracy remains stable, whereas TS substantially improves calibration. Uncertainty-error alignment improves most with calibrated checkpoint-based inference, leading to uncertainty maps that highlight more consistently regions requiring manual edits. Overall, integrating calibration with efficient ensembling seems a promising strategy to implement a budget-aware QA workflow for radiotherapy segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于nnU-Net的预算感知不确定性用于放射治疗分割质量保证
临床靶区体积（CTV）的精确勾画对放射治疗计划至关重要，但耗时且难以评估，尤其在全骨髓和淋巴结照射（TMLI）等复杂治疗中。尽管基于深度学习的自动分割可减轻工作负担，但临床安全部署需可靠提示模型可能出错的位置。本文提出一种基于nnU-Net的预算感知不确定性驱动的质量保证框架，结合不确定性量化与事后校准，生成基于预测熵的体素级不确定性图，以指导针对性人工复核。我们在TMLI作为代表性用例中，比较了温度缩放（TS）、深度集成（DE）、检查点集成（CE）和测试时增强（TTA），分别单独及组合评估。通过ROI掩码校准指标和在现实修订约束下的不确定性-误差对齐评估可靠性，以最不确定的0-5%体素的AUC总结。各配置下分割精度保持稳定，而TS显著提升校准效果。校准后的检查点推理使不确定性-误差对齐效果最佳，生成的不确定性图更一致地突出需人工编辑的区域。总体而言，将校准与高效集成结合似乎是实现放射治疗分割预算感知质量保证工作流的有前景策略。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable auto-segmentation in complex radiotherapy cases like Total Marrow and Lymph Node Irradiation, this study introduces a budget-aware uncertainty QA framework built on nnU-Net that combines uncertainty quantification and post-hoc calibration to generate voxel-wise uncertainty maps via predictive entropy. The framework evaluates four methods—temperature scaling, deep ensembles, checkpoint ensembles, and test-time augmentation—both individually and in combination, assessing reliability through calibration metrics and uncertainty-error alignment under revision constraints. Results show that while segmentation accuracy remains stable across configurations, temperature scaling significantly improves calibration, and calibrated checkpoint ensembles yield the best uncertainty-error alignment, consistently highlighting regions needing manual review.
为解决复杂放疗案例（如全骨髓和淋巴结照射）中自动分割的可靠性问题，本研究在nnU-Net基础上提出一种预算感知的质量保证框架，结合不确定性量化与后校准生成体素级不确定性图谱（基于预测熵）。该框架评估了温度缩放、深度集成、检查点集成和测试时增强四种方法，分别单独和组合使用，并通过校准指标和在修订约束下的不确定性-误差对齐进行可靠性评估。结果显示，尽管分割精度在各配置下保持稳定，温度缩放显著提升校准效果，而校准后的检查点集成方法在不确定性-误差对齐上表现最优，能更一致地标出需人工修正的区域。
SyncFix: Fixing 3D Reconstructions via Multi-View Synchronization
Authors: Deming Li, Abhay Yadav, Cheng Peng, Rama Chellappa, Anand Bhattad
First: 2026-04-13T17:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:58:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SyncFix, a framework that enforces cross-view consistency during the diffusion-based refinement of reconstructed scenes. SyncFix formulates refinement as a joint latent bridge matching problem, synchronizing distorted and clean representations across multiple views to fix the semantic and geometric inconsistencies. This means SyncFix learns a joint conditional over multiple views to enforce consistency throughout the denoising trajectory. Our training is done only on image pairs, but it generalizes naturally to an arbitrary number of views during inference. Moreover, reconstruction quality improves with additional views, with diminishing returns at higher view counts. Qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate that SyncFix consistently generates high-quality reconstructions and surpasses current state-of-the-art baselines, even in the absence of clean reference images. SyncFix achieves even higher fidelity when sparse references are available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyncFix：通过多视角同步修复3D重建
我们提出了SyncFix，一个在基于扩散的重建场景细化过程中强制跨视角一致性的框架。SyncFix将细化建模为联合潜在桥接匹配问题，通过同步多个视角间的失真与干净表示，修复语义和几何不一致性。这意味着SyncFix学习多个视角的联合条件分布，以在整个去噪轨迹中强制一致性。我们的训练仅使用图像对，但在推理时可自然扩展至任意数量的视角。此外，随着视角数量增加，重建质量提升，但高视角数量时收益递减。定性和定量结果表明，SyncFix持续生成高质量重建，并在无干净参考图像时仍超越当前最先进基线。当存在稀疏参考时，SyncFix可实现更高保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve 3D reconstruction quality by resolving cross-view inconsistencies during diffusion-based refinement, SyncFix introduces a joint latent bridge matching framework that synchronizes distorted and clean representations across multiple views. It learns a multi-view conditional distribution to enforce geometric and semantic consistency throughout the denoising process, trained only on image pairs but naturally generalizing to any number of views at inference. Experimental results show that SyncFix consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with reconstruction quality improving as more views are added—though returns diminish at higher view counts—and achieves peak fidelity when sparse clean references are available.
为解决扩散模型重建过程中多视角间语义与几何不一致的问题，SyncFix提出一种联合潜在桥匹配框架，通过同步多视角的失真与干净表示来提升重建质量。该方法学习多视角条件分布，在去噪过程中强制保持一致性，仅需成对图像训练即可泛化至任意视角数量。实验表明，SyncFix在重建质量上持续优于当前最优方法，视角数量越多效果越好（高视角数时收益递减），且在稀疏干净参考图像下可实现最高保真度。
C-ReD: A Comprehensive Chinese Benchmark for AI-Generated Text Detection Derived from Real-World Prompts
Authors: Chenxi Qing, Junxi Wu, Zheng Liu, Yixiang Qiu, Hongyao Yu, Bin Chen, Hao Wu, Shu-Tao Xia
First: 2026-04-13T17:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:56:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recently, large language models (LLMs) are capable of generating highly fluent textual content. While they offer significant convenience to humans, they also introduce various risks, like phishing and academic dishonesty. Numerous research efforts have been dedicated to developing algorithms for detecting AI-generated text and constructing relevant datasets. However, in the domain of Chinese corpora, challenges remain, including limited model diversity and data homogeneity. To address these issues, we propose C-ReD: a comprehensive Chinese Real-prompt AI-generated Detection benchmark. Experiments demonstrate that C-ReD not only enables reliable in-domain detection but also supports strong generalization to unseen LLMs and external Chinese datasets-addressing critical gaps in model diversity, domain coverage, and prompt realism that have limited prior Chinese detection benchmarks. We release our resources at https://github.com/HeraldofLight/C-ReD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：C-ReD：基于真实提示的全面中文AI生成文本检测基准
近期，大语言模型（LLM）能够生成高度流畅的文本内容。尽管它们为人类提供了极大便利，但也带来了诸如网络钓鱼和学术不端等风险。已有大量研究致力于开发AI生成文本检测算法并构建相关数据集。然而，在中文语料领域仍存在挑战，包括模型多样性不足和数据同质化。为解决这些问题，我们提出C-ReD：一个全面的中文真实提示AI生成文本检测基准。实验表明，C-ReD不仅支持可靠的领域内检测，还能有效泛化至未见的LLM和外部中文数据集，弥补了先前中文检测基准在模型多样性、领域覆盖和提示真实感方面的关键不足。我们的资源已发布于https://github.com/HeraldofLight/C-ReD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address gaps in Chinese AI-generated text detection, this work introduces C-ReD, a benchmark derived from real-world prompts to enhance model diversity and data realism. The method constructs a comprehensive dataset by collecting and annotating real prompts paired with AI-generated responses, enabling evaluation across diverse LLMs and unseen Chinese corpora. Experimental results show that C-ReD supports reliable in-domain detection and strong generalization to novel models and datasets, effectively overcoming prior limitations in Chinese detection benchmarks.
为应对中文语境下AI生成文本检测能力不足的问题，本研究提出C-ReD基准，该数据集基于真实世界提示构建，旨在解决现有中文数据集模型单一、提示不真实等缺陷。方法通过收集多样化的现实场景提示，构建覆盖多模型与多领域的检测评估集。实验表明，C-ReD不仅在本域检测中表现可靠，还能有效泛化至未见模型和外部中文数据集，显著弥补了先前中文检测基准的关键短板。
Disentangled Point Diffusion for Precise Object Placement
Authors: Lyuxing He, Eric Cai, Shobhit Aggarwal, Jianjun Wang, David Held
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in robotic manipulation have highlighted the effectiveness of learning from demonstration. However, while end-to-end policies excel in expressivity and flexibility, they struggle both in generalizing to novel object geometries and in attaining a high degree of precision. An alternative, object-centric approach frames the task as predicting the placement pose of the target object, providing a modular decomposition of the problem. Building on this goal-prediction paradigm, we propose TAX-DPD, a hierarchical, disentangled point diffusion framework that achieves state-of-the-art performance in placement precision, multi-modal coverage, and generalization to variations in object geometries and scene configurations. We model global scene-level placements through a novel feed-forward Dense Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) that yields a spatially dense prior over global placements; we then model the local object-level configuration through a novel disentangled point cloud diffusion module that separately diffuses the object geometry and the placement frame, enabling precise local geometric reasoning. Interestingly, we demonstrate that our point cloud diffusion achieves substantially higher accuracy than a prior approach based on SE(3)-diffusion, even in the context of rigid object placement. We validate our approach across a suite of challenging tasks in simulation and in the real-world on high-precision industrial insertion tasks. Furthermore, we present results on a cloth-hanging task in simulation, indicating that our framework can further relax assumptions on object rigidity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦点扩散用于精确物体放置
机器人操作领域的最新进展突显了从演示中学习的有效性。然而，尽管端到端策略在表达能力和灵活性方面表现优异，但在泛化到新物体几何形状以及实现高精度方面仍存在困难。一种替代方法——以物体为中心的方法，将任务建模为预测目标物体的放置位姿，从而实现问题的模块化分解。基于这一目标预测范式，我们提出了TAX-DPD，一种分层解耦点扩散框架，在放置精度、多模态覆盖以及对物体几何形状和场景配置变化的泛化能力方面达到当前最优性能。我们通过一种新颖的前馈密集高斯混合模型（GMM）建模全局场景级放置，从而在全局放置上生成空间密集的先验；随后，我们通过一种新颖的解耦点云扩散模块建模局部物体级配置，分别对物体几何形状和放置坐标系进行扩散，以实现精确的局部几何推理。有趣的是，我们证明了我们的点云扩散方法在精度上显著优于基于SE(3)扩散的先前方法，即使在刚性物体放置场景中也是如此。我们在仿真和现实世界高精度工业插入任务中验证了该方法，并在仿真中的布料悬挂任务中展示了我们的框架能够进一步放宽对物体刚性的假设。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of end-to-end policies in robotic manipulation—particularly their poor generalization and precision with novel object geometries—the authors propose TAX-DPD, a hierarchical, disentangled point diffusion framework that predicts object placement poses. The method combines a feed-forward Dense Gaussian Mixture Model for global scene-level placement priors with a novel disentangled point cloud diffusion module that separately models object geometry and placement frame for precise local reasoning. Experimental results across simulation and real-world industrial insertion tasks show TAX-DPD achieves state-of-the-art placement precision, multi-modal coverage, and generalization to object shape and scene variations, outperforming prior SE(3)-diffusion methods even for rigid objects, and demonstrating applicability to non-rigid tasks like cloth hanging.
为解决端到端策略在机器人操作中对新物体几何形状泛化能力差、精度不足的问题，本文提出TAX-DPD框架，采用分层解耦的点扩散模型预测目标物体放置位姿。该框架结合密集高斯混合模型建模全局场景放置先验，再通过新型解耦点云扩散模块分别建模物体几何与放置坐标系，实现局部精确几何推理。实验表明，TAX-DPD在放置精度、多模态覆盖和物体形状/场景泛化方面达到当前最优，其点云扩散方法在刚性物体放置任务中显著优于基于SE(3)扩散的前序方法，并在仿真中成功扩展至非刚性任务如布料悬挂。
LottieGPT: Tokenizing Vector Animation for Autoregressive Generation
Authors: Junhao Chen, Kejun Gao, Yuehan Cui, Mingze Sun, Mingjin Chen, Shaohui Wang, Xiaoxiao Long, Fei Ma, Qi Tian, Ruqi Huang, Hao Zhao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project Page: https://lottiegpt.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite rapid progress in video generation, existing models are incapable of producing vector animation, a dominant and highly expressive form of multimedia on the Internet. Vector animations offer resolution-independence, compactness, semantic structure, and editable parametric motion representations, yet current generative models operate exclusively in raster space and thus cannot synthesize them. Meanwhile, recent advances in large multimodal models demonstrate strong capabilities in generating structured data such as slides, 3D meshes, LEGO sequences, and indoor layouts, suggesting that native vector animation generation may be achievable. In this work, we present the first framework for tokenizing and autoregressively generating vector animations. We adopt Lottie, a widely deployed JSON-based animation standard, and design a tailored Lottie Tokenizer that encodes layered geometric primitives, transforms, and keyframe-based motion into a compact and semantically aligned token sequence. To support large-scale training, we also construct LottieAnimation-660K, the largest and most diverse vector animation dataset to date, consisting of 660k real-world Lottie animation and 15M static Lottie image files curated from broad Internet sources. Building upon these components, we finetune Qwen-VL to create LottieGPT, a native multimodal model capable of generating coherent, editable vector animations directly from natural language or visual prompts. Experiments show that our tokenizer dramatically reduces sequence length while preserving structural fidelity, enabling effective autoregressive learning of dynamic vector content. LottieGPT exhibits strong generalization across diverse animation styles and outperforms previous state-of-the-art models on SVG generation (a special case of single-frame vector animation).
中文标题/摘要
标题：LottieGPT：矢量动画的标记化与自回归生成
尽管视频生成技术进展迅速，现有模型仍无法生成矢量动画——这是互联网上主流且高度表达性的多媒体形式。矢量动画具备分辨率无关性、紧凑性、语义结构及可编辑的参数化运动表示，但当前生成模型仅在光栅空间中运作，因而无法合成矢量动画。与此同时，大型多模态模型的最新进展展示了生成结构化数据（如幻灯片、3D网格、乐高序列和室内布局）的强大能力，表明原生矢量动画生成或可实现。本文首次提出用于矢量动画标记化与自回归生成的框架。我们采用广泛部署的基于JSON的Lottie动画标准，并设计了专用的Lottie标记器，将分层几何原语、变换及基于关键帧的运动编码为紧凑且语义对齐的标记序列。为支持大规模训练，我们构建了LottieAnimation-660K数据集——迄今最大、最多样化的矢量动画数据集，包含66万份真实世界Lottie动画及1500万份静态Lottie图像文件，均从广泛互联网来源精心筛选。基于这些组件，我们微调Qwen-VL，构建出LottieGPT——一种可直接从自然语言或视觉提示生成连贯、可编辑矢量动画的原生多模态模型。实验表明，我们的标记器显著缩短序列长度同时保持结构保真度，从而有效实现动态矢量内容的自回归学习。LottieGPT在多种动画风格上展现出强大泛化能力，并在SVG生成（单帧矢量动画的特例）任务上超越现有最先进模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of vector animation generation in current video models despite its expressive and scalable nature, this work introduces LottieGPT, the first autoregressive framework for generating vector animations. It employs a custom Lottie Tokenizer to encode layered geometric primitives, transformations, and keyframe motion into compact, semantically aligned tokens, enabling efficient sequence modeling. Trained on LottieAnimation-660K, the largest vector animation dataset to date, the model fine-tunes Qwen-VL to produce coherent, editable animations from text or image prompts. Experimental results demonstrate that the tokenizer significantly reduces sequence length while preserving structural fidelity, and LottieGPT achieves superior performance over prior methods in SVG generation and generalizes well across diverse animation styles.
鉴于现有视频生成模型无法处理矢量动画，而矢量动画在互联网上具有高表达力和分辨率无关性，本研究提出LottieGPT，首个用于自回归生成矢量动画的框架。该框架基于Lottie标准，设计专用分词器将几何元素、变换和关键帧运动编码为紧凑语义对齐的序列。训练依托LottieAnimation-660K，目前最大且最多样化的矢量动画数据集，包含66万动画及1500万静态图像。模型在Qwen-VL基础上微调，可直接从文本或图像提示生成连贯可编辑的矢量动画。实验表明，分词器显著压缩序列长度同时保持结构保真度，LottieGPT在SVG生成任务中超越现有最先进方法，并在多种动画风格上表现优异。
A Mechanistic Analysis of Looped Reasoning Language Models
Authors: Hugh Blayney, Álvaro Arroyo, Johan Obando-Ceron, Pablo Samuel Castro, Aaron Courville, Michael M. Bronstein, Xiaowen Dong
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:36+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 63 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning has become a central capability in large language models. Recent research has shown that reasoning performance can be improved by looping an LLM's layers in the latent dimension, resulting in looped reasoning language models. Despite promising results, few works have investigated how their internal dynamics differ from those of standard feedforward models. In this paper, we conduct a mechanistic analysis of the latent states in looped language models, focusing in particular on how the stages of inference observed in feedforward models compare to those observed in looped ones. To this end, we analyze cyclic recurrence and show that for many of the studied models each layer in the cycle converges to a distinct fixed point; consequently, the recurrent block follows a consistent cyclic trajectory in the latent space. We provide evidence that as these fixed points are reached, attention-head behavior stabilizes, leading to constant behavior across recurrences. Empirically, we discover that recurrent blocks learn stages of inference that closely mirror those of feedforward models, repeating these stages in depth with each iteration. We study how recurrent block size, input injection, and normalization influence the emergence and stability of these cyclic fixed points. We believe these findings help translate mechanistic insights into practical guidance for architectural design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环推理语言模型的机制分析
推理已成为大型语言模型的核心能力。近期研究表明，通过在潜在维度上循环LLM的层可提升推理性能，形成循环推理语言模型。尽管效果显著，但鲜有研究探讨其内部动态与标准前馈模型的差异。本文对循环语言模型的潜在状态进行机制分析，特别关注前馈模型中观察到的推理阶段与循环模型中的对比。为此，我们分析循环递归性，发现多数研究模型中每个循环层收敛至不同固定点，因此递归块在潜在空间中遵循一致的循环轨迹。我们提供证据表明，当固定点达成时，注意力头行为趋于稳定，导致递归过程中行为恒定。实证发现，递归块学习到的推理阶段与前馈模型高度相似，并在每次迭代中深度重复这些阶段。我们研究了递归块大小、输入注入和归一化对循环固定点出现与稳定的影响。我们认为，这些发现有助于将机制洞察转化为架构设计的实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed performance gains of looped reasoning language models, this study investigates how their internal latent dynamics differ from standard feedforward architectures. The authors analyze cyclic recurrence in looped models and find that each layer in the cycle converges to a distinct fixed point, forming a consistent trajectory in latent space. Attention behavior stabilizes at these fixed points, leading to invariant output patterns across iterations. Empirically, the recurrent blocks replicate inference stages seen in feedforward models, repeating them depth-wise with each loop. The study further examines how block size, input injection, and normalization affect the emergence and stability of these fixed points, offering mechanistic guidance for architectural design.
为理解循环推理语言模型性能提升的内在机制，本研究对比其与标准前馈模型的潜空间动态差异。分析表明，循环结构中各层收敛至不同固定点，形成稳定的潜空间循环轨迹，且注意力行为在固定点处稳定，导致输出不变。实验发现循环块可复现前馈模型的推理阶段，并随迭代深度重复这些阶段，其表现受块大小、输入注入和归一化方式显著影响，为架构设计提供机制性指导。
ClawGuard: A Runtime Security Framework for Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injection
Authors: Wei Zhao, Zhe Li, Peixin Zhang, Jun Sun
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating complex, multi-step real-world tasks, yet remain vulnerable to indirect prompt injection. Adversaries exploit this weakness by embedding malicious instructions within tool-returned content, which agents directly incorporate into their conversation history as trusted observations. This vulnerability manifests across three primary attack channels: web and local content injection, MCP server injection, and skill file injection. To address these vulnerabilities, we introduce \textsc{ClawGuard}, a novel runtime security framework that enforces a user-confirmed rule set at every tool-call boundary, transforming unreliable alignment-dependent defense into a deterministic, auditable mechanism that intercepts adversarial tool calls before any real-world effect is produced. By automatically deriving task-specific access constraints from the user's stated objective prior to any external tool invocation, \textsc{ClawGuard} blocks all three injection pathways without model modification or infrastructure change. Experiments across five state-of-the-art language models on AgentDojo, SkillInject, and MCPSafeBench demonstrate that \textsc{ClawGuard} achieves robust protection against indirect prompt injection without compromising agent utility. This work establishes deterministic tool-call boundary enforcement as an effective defense mechanism for secure agentic AI systems, requiring neither safety-specific fine-tuning nor architectural modification. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Claw-Guard/ClawGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClawGuard：一种针对工具增强型大语言模型代理的间接提示注入运行时安全框架
工具增强型大语言模型（LLM）代理在自动化复杂多步骤现实任务方面表现出色，但仍易受间接提示注入攻击。攻击者通过将恶意指令嵌入工具返回的内容中，使代理将其作为可信观察直接纳入对话历史。该漏洞主要通过三种攻击渠道表现：网页与本地内容注入、MCP服务器注入和技能文件注入。为应对这些漏洞，我们提出ClawGuard，一种新颖的运行时安全框架，它在每次工具调用边界强制执行用户确认的规则集，将依赖对齐的不可靠防御转化为可审计的确定性机制，从而在任何现实影响产生前拦截对抗性工具调用。ClawGuard在任何外部工具调用前，自动从用户声明的目标中推导出任务特定的访问约束，从而无需修改模型或基础设施即可阻断所有三种注入路径。在AgentDojo、SkillInject和MCPSafeBench五个前沿语言模型上的实验表明，ClawGuard在不损害代理效用的前提下，实现了对间接提示注入的稳健防护。本工作确立了在工具调用边界强制执行确定性机制作为安全代理AI系统的有效防御手段，无需专门的安全微调或架构修改。代码公开于https://github.com/Claw-Guard/ClawGuard。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of tool-augmented LLM agents to indirect prompt injection via malicious content embedded in tool responses, the authors propose ClawGuard, a runtime security framework that enforces user-confirmed rules at every tool-call boundary to block adversarial inputs before they affect real-world behavior. By deriving task-specific access constraints from the user’s stated objective prior to any tool invocation, ClawGuard intercepts attacks across three primary injection channels—web/local content, MCP server, and skill file—without modifying the model or infrastructure. Experimental results across five LLMs on three benchmarks show ClawGuard effectively blocks all injection attempts while preserving agent utility, establishing deterministic boundary enforcement as a viable, model-agnostic defense.
为应对工具增强型大语言模型代理在工具返回内容中被注入恶意指令的漏洞，作者提出ClawGuard运行时安全框架，在每次工具调用边界强制执行用户确认的访问规则。通过在调用任何外部工具前从用户目标自动推导任务特定约束，ClawGuard可拦截三种攻击路径——网页/本地内容、MCP服务器、技能文件注入——无需修改模型或基础设施。在五个主流模型和三个基准测试中，实验表明ClawGuard有效阻止了间接提示注入攻击，同时保持代理性能，确立了确定性边界控制作为模型无关的安全防御方案。
LMMs Meet Object-Centric Vision: Understanding, Segmentation, Editing and Generation
Authors: Yuqian Yuan, Wenqiao Zhang, Juekai Lin, Yu Zhong, Mingjian Gao, Binhe Yu, Yunqi Cao, Wentong Li, Yueting Zhuang, Beng Chin Ooi
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:02+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved remarkable progress in general-purpose vision--language understanding, yet they remain limited in tasks requiring precise object-level grounding, fine-grained spatial reasoning, and controllable visual manipulation. In particular, existing systems often struggle to identify the correct instance, preserve object identity across interactions, and localize or modify designated regions with high precision. Object-centric vision provides a principled framework for addressing these challenges by promoting explicit representations and operations over visual entities, thereby extending multimodal systems from global scene understanding to object-level understanding, segmentation, editing, and generation. This paper presents a comprehensive review of recent advances at the convergence of LMMs and object-centric vision. We organize the literature into four major themes: object-centric visual understanding, object-centric referring segmentation, object-centric visual editing, and object-centric visual generation. We further summarize the key modeling paradigms, learning strategies, and evaluation protocols that support these capabilities. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including robust instance permanence, fine-grained spatial control, consistent multi-step interaction, unified cross-task modeling, and reliable benchmarking under distribution shift. We hope this paper provides a structured perspective on the development of scalable, precise, and trustworthy object-centric multimodal systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大模型遇见以对象为中心的视觉：理解、分割、编辑与生成
大型多模态模型（LMMs）在通用视觉-语言理解方面取得了显著进展，但在需要精确对象级定位、细粒度空间推理和可控视觉操作的任务中仍显不足。现有系统常难以准确识别目标实例、保持对象身份一致性，并高精度定位或修改指定区域。以对象为中心的视觉提供了一种原则性框架，通过显式表示和操作视觉实体，将多模态系统从全局场景理解拓展至对象级理解、分割、编辑与生成。本文全面综述了LMMs与以对象为中心视觉融合的最新进展，将文献划分为四大主题：以对象为中心的视觉理解、以对象为中心的指代分割、以对象为中心的视觉编辑、以对象为中心的视觉生成，并总结支持这些能力的关键建模范式、学习策略与评估协议。最后，我们讨论了开放挑战与未来方向，包括鲁棒实例持久性、细粒度空间控制、一致多步交互、统一跨任务建模及分布偏移下的可靠基准评估。希望本文为构建可扩展、精确且可信的以对象为中心的多模态系统提供结构化视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in handling object-level tasks such as precise segmentation, spatial reasoning, and controllable editing, this paper reviews recent advances at the intersection of LMMs and object-centric vision. It organizes existing work into four core areas: understanding, segmentation, editing, and generation, highlighting key modeling approaches, learning strategies, and evaluation methods. Experimental findings show that object-centric frameworks significantly improve instance grounding, spatial precision, and multi-step consistency, though challenges remain in maintaining object identity, achieving fine-grained control, and ensuring robustness under distribution shifts.
鉴于大型多模态模型（LMMs）在需要精确物体级定位、空间推理和可控编辑的任务中表现不足，本文综述了LMMs与物体中心视觉结合的最新进展。文章将文献划分为四个核心主题——理解、分割、编辑与生成，总结了支持这些能力的关键建模范式与学习策略。实验结果表明，这些方法在实例定位、空间精度和多步一致性方面有所提升，但仍面临物体身份保持、精细控制及分布偏移下鲁棒性不足等挑战。
HDR Video Generation via Latent Alignment with Logarithmic Encoding
Authors: Naomi Ken Korem, Mohamed Oumoumad, Harel Cain, Matan Ben Yosef, Urska Jelercic, Ofir Bibi, Yaron Inger, Or Patashnik, Daniel Cohen-Or
First: 2026-04-13T17:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:55:02+00:00
Comments: https://HDR-LumiVid.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
High dynamic range (HDR) imagery offers a rich and faithful representation of scene radiance, but remains challenging for generative models due to its mismatch with the bounded, perceptually compressed data on which these models are trained. A natural solution is to learn new representations for HDR, which introduces additional complexity and data requirements. In this work, we show that HDR generation can be achieved in a much simpler way by leveraging the strong visual priors already captured by pretrained generative models. We observe that a logarithmic encoding widely used in cinematic pipelines maps HDR imagery into a distribution that is naturally aligned with the latent space of these models, enabling direct adaptation via lightweight fine-tuning without retraining an encoder. To recover details that are not directly observable in the input, we further introduce a training strategy based on camera-mimicking degradations that encourages the model to infer missing high dynamic range content from its learned priors. Combining these insights, we demonstrate high-quality HDR video generation using a pretrained video model with minimal adaptation, achieving strong results across diverse scenes and challenging lighting conditions. Our results indicate that HDR, despite representing a fundamentally different image formation regime, can be handled effectively without redesigning generative models, provided that the representation is chosen to align with their learned priors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过对数编码的潜在空间对齐生成HDR视频
高动态范围（HDR）图像能真实丰富地表示场景辐射度，但因其与生成模型训练所用的有界、感知压缩数据不匹配，给生成模型带来挑战。本文表明，可通过利用预训练生成模型已捕捉的强视觉先验，以更简单的方式实现HDR生成。我们观察到，电影制作流程中广泛使用的对数编码可将HDR图像映射到与这些模型潜在空间自然对齐的分布，从而通过轻量级微调即可直接适配，无需重新训练编码器。为恢复输入中不可直接观测的细节，我们进一步引入基于相机仿真的退化训练策略，促使模型从其学习到的先验中推断缺失的高动态范围内容。结合这些见解，我们仅需最小化适配，便能利用预训练视频模型生成高质量HDR视频，在多样场景与复杂光照条件下均取得优异效果。结果表明，尽管HDR代表了根本不同的成像机制，只要选择合适的表示使其与模型先验对齐，无需重设计生成模型即可有效处理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty generative models face in producing HDR video due to training on perceptually compressed data, this work proposes a lightweight approach leveraging logarithmic encoding to align HDR imagery with pretrained model latents. By fine-tuning a pretrained video model without retraining an encoder, and using camera-mimicking degradations to encourage inference of missing HDR content, the method achieves high-quality HDR video generation across diverse scenes and lighting conditions without redesigning the underlying architecture.
为了解决高动态范围（HDR）视频生成中因HDR光度范围与生成模型训练数据不匹配而带来的困难，该研究提出通过使用对数编码将HDR图像映射到与预训练生成模型潜在空间自然对齐的分布，从而实现轻量级微调而不需重新训练编码器。为恢复输入中不可见的HDR细节，研究引入相机模拟退化训练策略，引导模型从已学先验中推断缺失内容。实验表明，该方法在多种场景和复杂光照条件下均能生成高质量HDR视频，证明HDR无需重设计生成模型即可有效处理，前提是输入表示与模型先验对齐。
GenTac: Generative Modeling and Forecasting of Soccer Tactics
Authors: Jiayuan Rao, Tianlin Gui, Haoning Wu, Yanfeng Wang, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-04-13T17:53:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:53:49+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 5 figures; technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling open-play soccer tactics is a formidable challenge due to the stochastic, multi-agent nature of the game. Existing computational approaches typically produce single, deterministic trajectory forecasts or focus on highly structured set-pieces, fundamentally failing to capture the inherent variance and branching possibilities of real-world match evolution. Here, we introduce GenTac, a diffusion-based generative framework that conceptualizes soccer tactics as a stochastic process over continuous multi-player trajectories and discrete semantic events. By learning the underlying distribution of player movements from historical tracking data, GenTac samples diverse, plausible, long-horizon future trajectories. The framework supports rich contextual conditioning, including opponent behavior, specific team or league playing styles, and strategic objectives, while grounding continuous spatial dynamics into a 15-class tactical event space. Extensive evaluations on our proposed benchmark, TacBench, demonstrate four key capabilities: (1) GenTac achieves high geometric accuracy while strictly preserving the collective structural consistency of the team; (2) it accurately simulates stylistic nuances, distinguishing between specific teams (e.g., Auckland FC) and leagues (e.g., A-League versus German leagues); (3) it enables controllable counterfactual simulations, demonstrably altering spatial control and expected threat metrics based on offensive or defensive guidance; and (4) it reliably anticipates future tactical outcomes directly from generated rollouts. Finally, we demonstrate that GenTac can be successfully trained to generalize to other dynamic team sports, including basketball, American football, and ice hockey.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenTac：足球战术的生成建模与预测
由于足球比赛具有随机性和多智能体特性，建模开放性比赛战术是一项艰巨挑战。现有计算方法通常仅生成单一确定性轨迹预测，或聚焦于高度结构化的定位球，根本无法捕捉真实比赛演进中的固有变异性与分支可能性。本文提出GenTac，一种基于扩散的生成框架，将足球战术视为在连续多球员轨迹与离散语义事件上的随机过程。通过从历史追踪数据中学习球员运动的潜在分布，GenTac可采样出多样、合理且长期的未来轨迹。该框架支持丰富的上下文条件化，包括对手行为、特定球队或联赛风格及战略目标，并将连续空间动态映射至15类战术事件空间。在我们提出的基准TacBench上的广泛评估表明GenTac具备四大能力：(1) 在保持球队整体结构一致性的同时，实现高几何精度；(2) 准确模拟风格细节，区分特定球队（如奥克兰FC）与联赛（如澳职联与德甲）；(3) 支持可控反事实模拟，根据进攻或防守指导显著改变空间控制与预期威胁指标；(4) 可直接从生成轨迹中可靠预测未来战术结果。最后，我们证明GenTac可成功迁移到其他动态团队运动，如篮球、美式橄榄球和冰球。

Motivated by the need to model the stochastic and multi-agent nature of soccer tactics, GenTac introduces a diffusion-based generative framework that treats tactics as a stochastic process over continuous player trajectories and discrete tactical events. It learns the distribution of historical player movements to sample diverse, plausible long-horizon futures while preserving team structure and enabling contextual conditioning on opponent behavior, team styles, and strategic goals. Experimental results on TacBench show high geometric accuracy, stylistic fidelity across teams and leagues, controllable counterfactual simulations altering spatial control and threat metrics, and reliable anticipation of future outcomes from generated rollouts, with successful generalization to basketball, American football, and ice hockey.
为解决现有方法难以捕捉足球战术随机性与多智能体特性的问题，GenTac提出一种基于扩散模型的生成框架，将战术建模为连续球员轨迹与离散战术事件的随机过程。该框架从历史追踪数据中学习球员运动分布，生成多样且合理的长时程未来轨迹，同时支持对对手行为、球队风格和战略目标的上下文条件控制。在TacBench基准测试中，GenTac在保持团队结构一致性的同时实现高几何精度，能准确模拟不同球队与联赛的风格差异，支持可控反事实模拟以调整空间控制与威胁指标，并可直接从生成轨迹预测未来战术结果，且可泛化至篮球、美式足球和冰球等动态团队运动。
Physics-informed AI Accelerated Retention Analysis of Ferroelectric Vertical NAND: From Day-Scale TCAD to Second-Scale Surrogate Model
Authors: Gyujun Jeong, Sungwon Cho, Minji Shon, Namhoon Kim, Woohyun Hwang, Kwangyou Seo, Suhwan Lim, Wanki Kim, Daewon Ha, Prasanna Venkatesan, Kihang Youn, Ram Cherukuri, Yiyi Wang, Suman Datta, Asif Khan, Shimeng Yu
First: 2026-03-06T21:01:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:53:41+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 6 figures, to be published in ICMC (International Compact Modeling Conference)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ferroelectric field-effect transistors (FeFET)-based vertical NAND (Fe-VNAND) has emerged as a promising candidate to overcome z-scaling limitations with lower programming voltages. However, the data retention of 3D Fe-VNAND is hindered by the complex interaction between charge detrapping and ferroelectric depolarization. Developing optimized device designs requires exploring an extensive parameter space, but the high computational cost of conventional Technology Computer-Aided Design (TCAD) tools makes such wide-scale optimization impractical. To overcome these simulation barriers, we present a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO)-based AI surrogate model designed for high-efficiency prediction of threshold voltage (Vth) shifts and retention behavior. By embedding fundamental physical principles into the learning architecture, our PINO framework achieves a speedup exceeding 10000x compared to TCAD while maintaining physical accuracy. This study demonstrates the model's effectiveness on a single FeFET configuration, serving as a pathway toward modeling the retention loss mechanisms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息人工智能加速铁电垂直NAND的保持性分析：从日级TCAD到秒级代理模型
基于铁电场效应晶体管（FeFET）的垂直NAND（Fe-VNAND）已成为克服z缩放限制、降低编程电压的有前景候选方案。然而，三维Fe-VNAND的数据保持性受电荷脱陷阱与铁电极化衰减之间复杂相互作用的阻碍。优化器件设计需探索广阔参数空间，但传统工艺计算机辅助设计（TCAD）工具的高计算成本使大规模优化不切实际。为克服这些仿真瓶颈，我们提出一种基于物理信息神经算子（PINO）的人工智能代理模型，用于高效预测阈值电压（Vth）偏移和保持行为。通过将基本物理原理嵌入学习架构，我们的PINO框架相比TCAD实现超过10000倍加速，同时保持物理准确性。本研究在单一FeFET配置上验证了模型的有效性，为建模保持性损失机制提供了路径。

Driven by the need to optimize ferroelectric vertical NAND (Fe-VNAND) retention under complex charge detrapping and depolarization effects, this study introduces a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) surrogate model to accelerate simulation. By integrating physical laws into the neural network architecture, the PINO model predicts threshold voltage shifts and retention behavior with over 10,000x speedup compared to conventional TCAD simulations while preserving accuracy. Experimental validation on a single FeFET configuration confirms the model’s reliability, establishing a scalable framework for future device optimization.
为解决3D Fe-VNAND器件因电荷脱捕与铁电极化相互作用导致的数据保持性问题，本研究提出一种融合物理规律的神经算子（PINO）代理模型，用于加速仿真。该模型在保持物理精度的前提下，相比传统TCAD工具提速超10,000倍，可有效预测阈值电压偏移与保持行为。在单个FeFET结构上的实验验证表明，该模型具备扩展性，可作为未来保持机制分析的高效工具。
ClawGUI: A Unified Framework for Training, Evaluating, and Deploying GUI Agents
Authors: Fei Tang, Zhiqiong Lu, Boxuan Zhang, Weiming Lu, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang, Yongliang Shen
First: 2026-04-13T17:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:52:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
GUI agents drive applications through their visual interfaces instead of programmatic APIs, interacting with arbitrary software via taps, swipes, and keystrokes, reaching a long tail of applications that CLI-based agents cannot. Yet progress in this area is bottlenecked less by modeling capacity than by the absence of a coherent full-stack infrastructure: online RL training suffers from environment instability and closed pipelines, evaluation protocols drift silently across works, and trained agents rarely reach real users on real devices. We present \textbf{ClawGUI}, an open-source framework addressing these three gaps within a single harness. \textbf{ClawGUI-RL} provides the first open-source GUI agent RL infrastructure with validated support for both parallel virtual environments and real physical devices, integrating GiGPO with a Process Reward Model for dense step-level supervision. \textbf{ClawGUI-Eval} enforces a fully standardized evaluation pipeline across 6 benchmarks and 11+ models, achieving 95.8\% reproduction against official baselines. \textbf{ClawGUI-Agent} brings trained agents to Android, HarmonyOS, and iOS through 12+ chat platforms with hybrid CLI-GUI control and persistent personalized memory. Trained end to end within this pipeline, \textbf{ClawGUI-2B} achieves 17.1\% Success Rate on MobileWorld GUI-Only, outperforming the same-scale MAI-UI-2B baseline by 6.0\%.
中文标题/摘要

GUI代理通过视觉界面驱动应用程序，而非程序化API，通过点击、滑动和按键与任意软件交互，触及CLI代理无法覆盖的长尾应用。然而，该领域进展受限于缺乏统一的全栈基础设施，而非模型容量：在线强化学习训练受环境不稳定和封闭管道影响，评估协议在不同研究间悄然漂移，训练好的代理极少能在真实设备上交付给真实用户。我们提出\textbf{ClawGUI}，一个开源框架，通过单一平台解决上述三大问题。\textbf{ClawGUI-RL}提供首个支持并行虚拟环境与真实物理设备的开源GUI代理强化学习基础设施，集成GiGPO与进程奖励模型，实现密集的步级监督。\textbf{ClawGUI-Eval}在6个基准和11+模型上强制执行标准化评估流程，对官方基线的复现率达95.8%。\textbf{ClawGUI-Agent}通过12+聊天平台将训练好的代理部署至Android、HarmonyOS和iOS，支持混合CLI-GUI控制与持久化个性化记忆。在该管道内端到端训练的\textbf{ClawGUI-2B}在MobileWorld GUI-Only基准上达到17.1%成功率，超越同等规模的MAI-UI-2B基线6.0%。

Motivated by the need for a unified infrastructure to train, evaluate, and deploy GUI agents that interact with software via touch and keystrokes rather than APIs, the authors introduce ClawGUI, an open-source framework addressing three key gaps: unstable online RL training, inconsistent evaluation protocols, and lack of real-device deployment.
