Automatic Uncertainty-Aware Synthetic Data Bootstrapping for Historical Map Segmentation
Authors: Lukas Arzoumanidis, Julius Knechtel, Jan-Henrik Haunert, Youness Dehbi
First: 2025-11-19T21:02:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:51:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The automated analysis of historical documents, particularly maps, has drastically benefited from advances in deep learning and its success across various computer vision applications. However, most deep learning-based methods heavily rely on large amounts of annotated training data, which are typically unavailable for historical maps, especially for those belonging to specific, homogeneous cartographic domains, also known as corpora. Creating high-quality training data suitable for machine learning often takes a significant amount of time and involves extensive manual effort. While synthetic training data can alleviate the scarcity of real-world samples, it often lacks the affinity (realism) and diversity (variation) necessary for effective learning. By transferring the cartographic style of a historical map corpus onto modern vector data, we bootstrap an effectively unlimited number of synthetic historical maps suitable for tasks such as land-cover interpretation of a homogeneous historical map corpus. We propose an automatic deep generative approach and an alternative manual stochastic degradation technique to emulate the visual uncertainty and noise, also known as aleatoric uncertainty, commonly observed in historical map scans. To quantitatively evaluate the effectiveness and applicability of our approach, the bootstrapped training datasets were employed for domain-adaptive semantic segmentation on a homogeneous map corpus using a Self-Constructing Graph Convolutional Network, enabling a comprehensive assessment of the impact of our data bootstrapping methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向历史地图分割的自动不确定性感知合成数据自举方法
深度学习在历史文档（特别是地图）的自动化分析中取得了显著进展，但多数深度学习方法依赖大量标注训练数据，而历史地图，尤其是特定同质制图领域的地图（即语料库），通常缺乏此类数据。生成高质量训练数据往往耗时且需大量人工。尽管合成数据可缓解真实样本稀缺问题，但常缺乏真实感与多样性。本文通过将历史地图语料库的制图风格迁移至现代矢量数据，自举出大量适用于同质历史地图语料库任务（如地表覆盖识别）的合成历史地图。我们提出一种自动深度生成方法及替代的手动随机退化技术，以模拟历史地图扫描中常见的视觉不确定性（即随机不确定性）。为定量评估方法有效性，我们使用自举训练数据集，在同质地图语料库上采用自构建图卷积网络进行领域自适应语义分割，全面评估数据自举方法的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of annotated historical map data for training deep learning models, this work proposes an automatic method to generate synthetic training samples by transferring modern vector data into the cartographic style of historical map corpora. The approach combines a deep generative model with a manual stochastic degradation technique to simulate visual uncertainty and noise, enhancing realism and diversity. Experimental results demonstrate that the bootstrapped datasets significantly improve domain-adaptive semantic segmentation performance on homogeneous historical maps when used with a Self-Constructing Graph Convolutional Network, validating the effectiveness of the synthetic data generation strategy.
为解决历史地图标注数据稀缺的问题，该研究提出通过将现代矢量数据转换为历史地图风格，自动生成合成训练样本，以支持领域自适应语义分割。采用深度生成模型与手动随机退化两种方法引入视觉不确定性与噪声，模拟真实历史地图扫描的特性。实验表明，所生成的合成数据集在同质历史地图语料上显著提升分割性能，验证了该数据生成方法的有效性。
ELT: Elastic Looped Transformers for Visual Generation
Authors: Sahil Goyal, Swayam Agrawal, Gautham Govind Anil, Prateek Jain, Sujoy Paul, Aditya Kusupati
First: 2026-04-10T09:53:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:50:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Elastic Looped Transformers (ELT), a highly parameter-efficient class of visual generative models based on a recurrent transformer architecture. While conventional generative models rely on deep stacks of unique transformer layers, our approach employs iterative, weight-shared transformer blocks to drastically reduce parameter counts while maintaining high synthesis quality. To effectively train these models for image and video generation, we propose the idea of Intra-Loop Self Distillation (ILSD), where student configurations (intermediate loops) are distilled from the teacher configuration (maximum training loops) to ensure consistency across the model's depth in a single training step. Our framework yields a family of elastic models from a single training run, enabling Any-Time inference capability with dynamic trade-offs between computational cost and generation quality, with the same parameter count. ELT significantly shifts the efficiency frontier for visual synthesis. With $4\times$ reduction in parameter count under iso-inference-compute settings, ELT achieves a competitive FID of $2.0$ on class-conditional ImageNet $256 \times 256$ and FVD of $72.8$ on class-conditional UCF-101.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ELT：用于视觉生成的弹性循环变换器
我们提出了弹性循环变换器（ELT），这是一种基于循环变换器架构、参数效率极高的视觉生成模型。与传统生成模型依赖深度堆叠的独特变换器层不同，我们的方法采用迭代且权重共享的变换器模块，大幅减少参数数量的同时保持高质量合成效果。为有效训练这些模型进行图像和视频生成，我们提出“循环内自蒸馏”（ILSD）方法，即在单次训练步骤中，从教师配置（最大训练循环）蒸馏学生配置（中间循环），确保模型深度内的一致性。我们的框架从单次训练中生成一系列弹性模型，支持任意时间推理，动态平衡计算成本与生成质量，且参数量保持不变。ELT显著提升了视觉合成的效率前沿。在推理计算量相同条件下，参数量减少4倍，ELT在类条件ImageNet 256×256上实现FID 2.0，在类条件UCF-101上实现FVD 72.8。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more parameter-efficient visual generative models, ELT introduces a recurrent transformer architecture that shares weights across iterative blocks, drastically reducing parameters while preserving synthesis quality. It employs Intra-Loop Self Distillation (ILSD) to train a family of models from a single run, enabling dynamic trade-offs between computation and quality during inference. Experiments show ELT achieves competitive FID of 2.0 on ImageNet and FVD of 72.8 on UCF-101 with 4x fewer parameters under equal inference compute.
为提升视觉生成模型的参数效率，ELT提出一种循环Transformer架构，通过共享权重的迭代块大幅减少参数量而不牺牲生成质量。其采用“循环内自蒸馏”（ILSD）训练方法，将中间循环配置从最终循环蒸馏，确保各深度层表现一致。实验表明，在同等推理计算量下，ELT参数量减少4倍，于ImageNet上FID达2.0，UCF-101上FVD达72.8，支持动态调整速度与质量的权衡，无需重新训练。
Exact Structural Abstraction and Tractability Limits
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:50:36+00:00
Comments: 42 pages. 3 tables. Lean 4 formalization available https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Any rigorously specified problem determines an admissible-output relation $R$, and the only state distinctions that matter are the classes $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$. Every exact correctness claim reduces to the same quotient-recovery problem, and the no-go concerns tractability of the underlying problem, not of its presentation. Exact means agreement with $R$, not zero-error determinism or absence of approximation/randomization in the specification. The exact-semantics quotient theorem identifies admissible-output equivalence as the canonical object recovered by exact relevance certification. Decision, search, approximation, statistical, randomized, horizon, and distributional guarantees instantiate it. Tractable families have a finite primitive basis, but optimizer-quotient realizability is maximal, so quotient shape cannot characterize the frontier. We prove a meta-impossibility theorem for efficiently checkable structural predicates invariant under theorem-forced closure laws of exact certification. Zero-distortion summaries, quotient entropy bounds, and support counting explain them. Same-orbit disagreements across four obstruction families, via action-independent pair-targeted affine witnesses, force contradiction. Consequently no correct problem-tractability classifier on a closure-closed domain yields an exact characterization over these families. Restricting to a closure-closed subdomain helps only by removing orbit gaps. Uniform strict-gap control preserves the full optimizer quotient, while arbitrarily small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency. Closure-orbit agreement is forced by correctness, and the same compute-cost barrier extends to optimizer computation, payload/search, and theorem-backed external or transported outputs. The obstruction therefore appears at the level of correctness itself, not any particular output formalism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精确结构抽象与可处理性极限
任何严格定义的问题都确定一个可接受输出关系 $R$，且仅有的状态区分是满足 $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$ 的等价类。所有精确正确性断言都归结为相同的商恢复问题，而不可行性关注的是底层问题本身，而非其表述形式。精确指与 $R$ 一致，而非零错误确定性或规范中无近似/随机化。精确语义商定理将可接受输出等价性识别为精确相关性认证所恢复的典范对象。决策、搜索、近似、统计、随机化、时域和分布保证均体现于此。可处理族具有有限的原始基，但优化器商的可实现性是最大的，因此商结构无法刻画前沿。我们证明了一个关于高效可检查结构谓词的元不可能定理，这些谓词在精确认证的定理强制闭包律下保持不变。零失真摘要、商熵界和支持计数解释了它们。通过作用无关的成对目标仿射见证，四个障碍族中的不同轨道分歧导致矛盾。因此，在闭包闭合域上不存在能对这些族给出精确刻画的正确问题可处理性分类器。仅通过限制到闭包闭合子域，可帮助消除轨道间隙。统一严格间隙控制保持完整优化器商，而任意微小扰动可能翻转相关性与充分性。闭包轨道一致性由正确性强制，且相同的计算成本障碍也扩展到优化器计算、负载/搜索以及定理支持的外部或传输输出。因此，障碍出现在正确性本身层面，而非任何特定输出形式层面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the inherent limits of tractability in problems with exact semantics, the paper introduces a framework where correctness is defined by an admissible-output relation R, and state equivalence is determined by indistinguishability under R. The authors show that all exact correctness claims reduce to a quotient-recovery problem, and that tractability depends on the structure of the quotient, not the presentation. They prove a meta-impossibility result: no efficient classifier can exactly characterize tractability across four obstruction families, because correctness forces closure-orbit agreement, and any perturbation can flip relevance and sufficiency. The key finding is that the obstruction arises at the level of correctness itself, not output formalism, and even uniform strict-gap control preserves the full optimizer quotient.
为理解具有精确语义问题的可计算性本质限制，本文将正确性定义为与可接受输出关系R的一致性，将所有精确断言归约为商结构恢复问题。通过引入元不可能性定理，证明在四个障碍族中，不存在高效的分类器能精确刻画可计算性，因为定理闭包域内结构不变量与作用无关的仿射见证会导致矛盾。方法利用商熵、支撑计数和轨道分歧，表明即使微小扰动也可能翻转相关性与充分性，而均匀严格间隙控制可保持完整优化商结构。实验结果表明，正确性约束本身即构成可计算性分类的根本障碍，且该障碍扩展至优化器计算、负载搜索及外部输出。
AtlasKV: Augmenting LLMs with Billion-Scale Knowledge Graphs in 20GB VRAM
Authors: Haoyu Huang, Hong Ting Tsang, Jiaxin Bai, Xi Peng, Gong Zhang, Yangqiu Song
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-20T15:40:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:45:21+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has shown some success in augmenting large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, as a non-parametric knowledge integration paradigm for LLMs, RAG methods heavily rely on external retrieval modules and the retrieved textual context prior. Especially for very large scale knowledge augmentation, they would introduce substantial inference latency due to expensive searches and much longer relevant context. In this paper, we propose a parametric knowledge integration method, called \textbf{AtlasKV}, a scalable, effective, and general way to augment LLMs with billion-scale knowledge graphs (KGs) (e.g. 1B triples) using very little GPU memory cost (e.g. less than 20GB VRAM). In AtlasKV, we introduce KG2KV and HiKVP to integrate KG triples into LLMs at scale with sub-linear time and memory complexity. It maintains strong knowledge grounding and generalization performance using the LLMs' inherent attention mechanism, and requires no external retrievers, long context priors, or retraining when adapting to new knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AtlasKV：在20GB显存下利用十亿级知识图谱增强大语言模型
检索增强生成（RAG）在增强大语言模型（LLMs）方面已取得一定成功。然而，作为一种非参数化的知识集成范式，RAG方法严重依赖外部检索模块和检索到的文本上下文先验。尤其在进行超大规模知识增强时，会因昂贵的搜索和更长的相关上下文而引入显著的推理延迟。本文提出一种参数化知识集成方法——AtlasKV，它是一种可扩展、高效且通用的方法，可在极小的GPU内存开销（如少于20GB VRAM）下，将十亿级知识图谱（如10亿三元组）融入LLMs。在AtlasKV中，我们引入KG2KV和HiKVP，以亚线性时间与内存复杂度大规模集成知识图谱三元组。该方法利用LLMs固有的注意力机制，保持强大的知识 grounding 与泛化性能，且无需外部检索器、长上下文先验或重新训练即可适应新知识。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency and memory cost of retrieval-augmented generation for large-scale knowledge graphs, this paper introduces AtlasKV, a parametric method that integrates billion-scale knowledge graphs directly into LLMs using under 20GB VRAM. It employs KG2KV and HiKVP to convert knowledge graph triples into efficient key-value representations with sub-linear complexity, enabling scalable knowledge augmentation without external retrievers or retraining. Experiments show AtlasKV maintains strong knowledge grounding and generalization while significantly reducing inference latency compared to RAG baselines, especially for large knowledge graphs.
为了解决传统RAG方法在处理十亿级知识图谱时存在的高延迟和高显存开销问题，本文提出AtlasKV，一种参数化方法，通过少于20GB显存直接将知识图谱融入大语言模型。其采用KG2KV和HiKVP技术，以次线性复杂度高效编码三元组至模型的键值缓存，无需外部检索器或长上下文提示。实验表明，AtlasKV在不进行再训练的情况下，保持了强大的知识 grounding 和泛化能力，同时显著降低了推理延迟，优于RAG基线方法。
Semantic Segmentation Algorithm Based on Light Field and LiDAR Fusion
Authors: Jie Luo, Yuxuan Jiang, Xin Jin, Mingyu Liu, Yihui Fan
First: 2025-10-08T06:15:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:44:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semantic segmentation serves as a cornerstone of scene understanding in autonomous driving but continues to face significant challenges under complex conditions such as occlusion. Light field and LiDAR modalities provide complementary visual and spatial cues that are beneficial for robust perception; however, their effective integration is hindered by limited viewpoint diversity and inherent modality discrepancies. To address these challenges, the first multimodal semantic segmentation dataset integrating light field data and point cloud data is proposed. Based on this dataset, we proposed a multi-modal light field point-cloud fusion segmentation network(Mlpfseg), incorporating feature completion and depth perception to segment both camera images and LiDAR point clouds simultaneously. The feature completion module addresses the density mismatch between point clouds and image pixels by performing differential reconstruction of point-cloud feature maps, enhancing the fusion of these modalities. The depth perception module improves the segmentation of occluded objects by reinforcing attention scores for better occlusion awareness. Our method outperforms image-only segmentation by 1.71 Mean Intersection over Union(mIoU) and point cloud-only segmentation by 2.38 mIoU, demonstrating its effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于光场与激光雷达融合的语义分割算法
语义分割是自动驾驶场景理解的基础，但在遮挡等复杂条件下仍面临显著挑战。光场与激光雷达模态提供了互补的视觉与空间线索，有利于鲁棒感知；然而，其有效融合受限于视角多样性不足和固有模态差异。为此，我们提出了首个融合光场数据与点云数据的多模态语义分割数据集，并基于该数据集提出了一种多模态光场点云融合分割网络（Mlpfseg），通过特征补全与深度感知模块，同时对相机图像与激光雷达点云进行分割。特征补全模块通过点云特征图的差分重建，解决点云与图像像素密度不匹配问题，增强模态融合；深度感知模块通过强化注意力得分，提升遮挡物体的分割效果。实验表明，该方法相比仅图像分割提升1.71 mIoU，相比仅点云分割提升2.38 mIoU，验证了其有效性。
Summary / 总结
To improve semantic segmentation under occlusion in autonomous driving, this work proposes a novel multimodal fusion approach combining light field and LiDAR data, addressing their inherent modality discrepancies and limited viewpoint diversity. The Mlpfseg network simultaneously processes camera images and LiDAR point clouds, using a feature completion module to reconcile density mismatches via differential reconstruction of point-cloud feature maps, and a depth perception module to enhance occlusion-aware segmentation through attention refinement. Experimental results show the method achieves 1.71 mIoU higher than image-only and 2.38 mIoU higher than point cloud-only baselines, validating its robustness and effectiveness.
为提升自动驾驶中遮挡场景下的语义分割性能，该研究提出融合光场与激光雷达数据的多模态方法，解决二者模态差异与视角多样性不足的问题。Mlpfseg网络同步处理图像与点云，通过特征补全模块利用差分重建缓解点云与图像像素密度不匹配，深度感知模块通过注意力强化提升遮挡物体分割能力。实验表明，该方法相较图像单模态提升1.71 mIoU，相较点云单模态提升2.38 mIoU，显著优于现有方法。
General365: Benchmarking General Reasoning in Large Language Models Across Diverse and Challenging Tasks
Authors: Junlin Liu, Shengnan An, Shuang Zhou, Dan Ma, Shixiong Luo, Ying Xie, Yuan Zhang, Wenling Yuan, Yifan Zhou, Xiaoyu Li, Ziwen Wang, Xuezhi Cao, Xunliang Cai
First: 2026-04-13T17:44:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:44:25+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Contemporary large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning capabilities, particularly in specialized domains like mathematics and physics. However, their ability to generalize these reasoning skills to more general and broader contexts--often termed general reasoning--remains under-explored. Unlike domain-specific reasoning, general reasoning relies less on expert knowledge but still presents formidable reasoning challenges, such as complex constraints, nested logical branches, and semantic interference. To address this gap, we introduce General365, a benchmark specifically designed to assess general reasoning in LLMs. By restricting background knowledge to a K-12 level, General365 explicitly decouples reasoning from specialized expertise. The benchmark comprises 365 seed problems and 1,095 variant problems across eight categories, ensuring both high difficulty and diversity. Evaluations across 26 leading LLMs reveal that even the top-performing model achieves only 62.8% accuracy, in stark contrast to the near-perfect performances of LLMs in math and physics benchmarks. These results suggest that the reasoning abilities of current LLMs are heavily domain-dependent, leaving significant room for improvement in broader applications. We envision General365 as a catalyst for advancing LLM reasoning beyond domain-specific tasks toward robust, general-purpose real-world scenarios. Code, Dataset, and Leaderboard: https://general365.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：General365：在多样且具有挑战性的任务中评估大语言模型的通用推理能力
当前大语言模型（LLMs）在数学和物理等专业领域展现了卓越的推理能力。然而，它们将这些推理技能泛化到更通用、更广泛的情境——通常称为通用推理——的能力仍被严重忽视。与领域特定推理不同，通用推理较少依赖专家知识，但仍面临严峻的推理挑战，如复杂约束、嵌套逻辑分支和语义干扰。为填补这一空白，我们提出了General365，一个专门用于评估LLMs通用推理能力的基准测试。通过将背景知识限制在K-12水平，General365明确将推理与专业专长解耦。该基准包含8个类别的365个基础问题和1,095个变体问题，确保了高难度和多样性。对26个主流LLMs的评估显示，即使表现最佳的模型准确率也仅为62.8%，与在数学和物理基准测试中近乎完美的表现形成鲜明对比。这些结果表明，当前LLMs的推理能力高度依赖领域，其在更广泛应用场景中的提升空间巨大。我们期望General365能成为推动LLMs推理能力从领域特定任务迈向稳健、通用的现实世界场景的催化剂。代码、数据集和排行榜：https://general365.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models excel in specialized reasoning tasks like math and physics but struggle with general reasoning, the authors introduce General365, a benchmark designed to evaluate LLMs on broad, non-expert-dependent reasoning challenges. The benchmark uses K-12 level knowledge to isolate reasoning from domain expertise and includes 365 seed problems and 1,095 variants across eight categories to ensure diversity and difficulty. Evaluations of 26 leading models show that even the top-performing model achieves only 62.8% accuracy, highlighting a significant gap compared to their performance in domain-specific benchmarks and underscoring the current limitations of LLMs in general reasoning.
为评估大语言模型（LLM）在非专业领域（如数学和物理之外）的推理泛化能力，作者构建了General365基准测试，其背景知识限定在小学至高中水平，以剥离领域专有知识的影响。该基准包含365个基础问题和1095个变体，覆盖八个类别，强调复杂约束与嵌套逻辑等挑战性任务。对26个主流LLM的评估显示，表现最佳的模型准确率仅为62.8%，远低于数学和物理等专业领域中的近满分表现，揭示当前LLM推理能力高度依赖特定领域知识。
Efficient KernelSHAP Explanations for Patch-based 3D Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Ricardo Coimbra Brioso, Giulio Sichili, Damiano Dei, Nicola Lambri, Pietro Mancosu, Marta Scorsetti, Daniele Loiacono
First: 2026-04-13T17:43:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:43:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Perturbation-based explainability methods such as KernelSHAP provide model-agnostic attributions but are typically impractical for patch-based 3D medical image segmentation due to the large number of coalition evaluations and the high cost of sliding-window inference. We present an efficient KernelSHAP framework for volumetric CT segmentation that restricts computation to a user-defined region of interest and its receptive-field support, and accelerates inference via patch logit caching, reusing baseline predictions for unaffected patches while preserving nnU-Net's fusion scheme. To enable clinically meaningful attributions, we compare three automatically generated feature abstractions within the receptive-field crop: whole-organ units, regular FCC supervoxels, and hybrid organ-aware supervoxels, and we study multiple aggregation/value functions targeting stabilizing evidence (TP/Dice/Soft Dice) or false-positive behavior. Experiments on whole-body CT segmentations show that caching substantially reduces redundant computation (with computational savings ranging from 15% to 30%) and that faithfulness and interpretability exhibit clear trade-offs: regular supervoxels often maximize perturbation-based metrics but lack anatomical alignment, whereas organ-aware units yield more clinically interpretable explanations and are particularly effective for highlighting false-positive drivers under normalized metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向基于补丁的3D医学图像分割的高效KernelSHAP解释方法
基于扰动的可解释性方法（如KernelSHAP）虽提供模型无关的归因，但通常因需大量联盟评估及滑动窗口推理成本高，难以应用于基于补丁的3D医学图像分割。本文提出一种高效的KernelSHAP框架，用于体CT分割，仅在用户定义的兴趣区域及其感受野支持范围内进行计算，并通过补丁logit缓存加速推理，复用未受影响补丁的基线预测，同时保留nnU-Net的融合方案。为实现临床意义的归因，我们在感受野裁剪区域内比较三种自动生成的特征抽象：全器官单元、规则FCC超体素及混合器官感知超体素，并研究多种聚合/价值函数，目标为稳定证据（TP/Dice/Soft Dice）或降低假阳性行为。在全身体CT分割实验中，缓存显著减少冗余计算（节省15%至30%计算量），且忠实度与可解释性存在明显权衡：规则超体素常最大化扰动基指标但缺乏解剖对齐，而器官感知单元则提供更临床可解释的解释，尤其在标准化指标下有效突出假阳性驱动因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of KernelSHAP for 3D medical image segmentation, this work introduces an efficient framework that limits evaluations to a user-defined region of interest and its receptive field, while accelerating inference through patch logit caching and preserving nnU-Net’s fusion architecture. The method evaluates three feature abstractions—whole-organ units, regular FCC supervoxels, and hybrid organ-aware supervoxels—and tests multiple aggregation functions to balance faithfulness and interpretability. Experimental results on whole-body CT scans show that caching reduces redundant computation by 15–30%, and while regular supervoxels optimize perturbation-based metrics, organ-aware units deliver more anatomically aligned and clinically interpretable explanations, especially effective at identifying false-positive drivers under normalized metrics.
为解决KernelSHAP在3D医学图像分割中计算开销大的问题，该研究提出一种高效框架，仅在用户指定的兴趣区域及其感受野内进行计算，并通过补丁logit缓存加速推理，同时保留nnU-Net的融合机制。研究对比了三种特征抽象方法——全器官单元、常规FCC超体素和混合器官感知超体素，并结合多种聚合函数以优化稳定性或减少假阳性。在全身体CT分割实验中，缓存机制使计算量降低15–30%，常规超体素虽在扰动指标上表现最优，但缺乏解剖对齐；而器官感知单元则提供更符合临床解释的解释，尤其在标准化指标下更擅长识别假阳性驱动因素。
Autonomous Diffractometry Enabled by Visual Reinforcement Learning
Authors: J. Oppliger, M. Stifter, A. Rüegg, I. Biało, L. Martinelli, P. G. Freeman, D. Prabhakaran, J. Zhao, Q. Wang, J. Chang
First: 2026-04-13T17:42:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:42:19+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automation underpins progress across scientific and industrial disciplines. Yet, automating tasks requiring interpretation of abstract visual information remain challenging. For example, crystal alignment strongly relies on humans with the ability to comprehend diffraction patterns. Here we introduce an autonomous system that aligns single crystals without access to crystallography and diffraction theory. Using a model-free reinforcement learning framework, an agent learns to identify and navigate towards high-symmetry orientations directly from Laue diffraction patterns. Despite the absence of human supervision, the agent develops human-like strategies to achieve time-efficient alignment across different crystal symmetry classes. With this, we provide a computational framework for intelligent diffractometers. As such, our approach advances the development of automated experimental workflows in materials science.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉强化学习的自主衍射技术
自动化是科学与工业领域进步的基础。然而，自动化需要解释抽象视觉信息的任务仍具挑战性。例如，晶体对准高度依赖能够理解衍射图样的人类。本文介绍了一种无需晶体学和衍射理论知识的自主系统，该系统通过无模型强化学习框架，使智能体直接从劳厄衍射图样中识别并导航至高对称取向。尽管无须人类监督，该智能体仍能发展出类似人类的策略，高效完成不同晶体对称类别的对准。由此，我们提供了一种智能衍射仪的计算框架，推动材料科学中自动化实验流程的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate tasks involving abstract visual interpretation, such as crystal alignment in diffractometry, this study introduces a visual reinforcement learning system that autonomously aligns single crystals without prior knowledge of crystallography. The method employs a model-free reinforcement learning agent trained directly from Laue diffraction patterns to identify and navigate toward high-symmetry orientations. Experimental results demonstrate that the agent, despite lacking human supervision, develops efficient, human-like alignment strategies across diverse crystal symmetry classes, enabling time-saving and reliable automation of diffractometry workflows.
为实现对抽象视觉信息（如晶体衍射图）的自动化处理，本研究提出一种基于视觉强化学习的自主系统，无需晶体学知识即可完成单晶对准。该方法采用无模型强化学习代理，直接从劳厄衍射图中识别并导航至高对称取向。实验表明，该代理在不同晶体对称类中展现出类人策略，大幅缩短对准时间且无需人工监督，为材料科学中的智能自动衍射仪提供了计算框架。
Preference Learning Unlocks LLMs' Psycho-Counseling Skills
Authors: Mian Zhang, Shaun M. Eack, Zhiyu Zoey Chen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-02-27T03:50:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:41:31+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Camera-Ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Applying large language models (LLMs) to assist in psycho-counseling is an emerging and meaningful approach, driven by the significant gap between patient needs and the availability of mental health support. However, current LLMs struggle to consistently provide effective responses to client speeches, largely due to the lack of supervision from high-quality real psycho-counseling data, whose content is typically inaccessible due to client privacy concerns. Furthermore, the quality of therapists' responses in available sessions can vary significantly based on their professional training and experience. Assessing the quality of therapists' responses remains an open challenge. In this work, we address these challenges by first proposing a set of professional and comprehensive principles to evaluate therapists' responses to client speeches. Using these principles, we create a preference dataset, PsychoCounsel-Preference, which contains 36k high-quality preference comparison pairs. This dataset aligns with the preferences of professional psychotherapists, providing a robust foundation for evaluating and improving LLMs in psycho-counseling. Experiments on reward modeling and preference learning demonstrate that PsychoCounsel-Preference is an excellent resource for LLMs to acquire essential skills for responding to clients in a counseling session. Our best-aligned model, PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B, achieves an impressive win rate of 87% against GPT-4o. We release PsychoCounsel-Preference, PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B and the reward model PsychoCounsel Llama3-8B-Reward to facilitate the research of psycho-counseling with LLMs at: https://hf.co/Psychotherapy-LLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：偏好学习解锁大语言模型的心理咨询能力
将大语言模型（LLMs）应用于辅助心理咨询是一种新兴且有意义的方法，旨在弥合患者需求与心理健康支持资源之间的显著差距。然而，当前LLMs在应对客户发言时难以持续提供有效回应，主要原因是缺乏高质量真实心理咨询数据的监督，而此类数据因患者隐私问题通常难以获取。此外，现有会话中治疗师回应的质量因专业训练和经验不同而差异显著，评估治疗师回应质量仍是一个开放性挑战。本研究通过首先提出一套专业且全面的原则来评估治疗师对客户发言的回应，进而构建了一个名为PsychoCounsel-Preference的偏好数据集，包含36,000对高质量偏好比较样本。该数据集符合专业心理咨询师的偏好，为评估和提升LLMs在心理咨询中的表现提供了坚实基础。在奖励建模与偏好学习实验中，PsychoCounsel-Preference被证明是LLMs学习应对客户咨询对话所需关键技能的优质资源。我们最优对齐的模型PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B在与GPT-4o的对抗中取得了87%的胜率。我们已发布PsychoCounsel-Preference、PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B及奖励模型PsychoCounsel Llama3-8B-Reward，以促进LLMs在心理咨询领域的研究：https://hf.co/Psychotherapy-LLM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the shortage of mental health professionals and the need for LLMs to deliver effective psycho-counseling, this work addresses the lack of high-quality supervision data by creating PsychoCounsel-Preference, a 36k-pair preference dataset aligned with professional therapist standards. The method involves defining comprehensive evaluation principles to curate and label preference comparisons from real counseling sessions, enabling reward modeling and preference learning for LLMs. Experimental results show that the best-aligned model, PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B, achieves an 87% win rate against GPT-4o in preference-based evaluations, demonstrating strong capability in counseling response generation.
为应对心理健康服务供需失衡及高质量辅导数据稀缺的问题，该研究构建了包含3.6万对偏好样本的PsychoCounsel-Preference数据集，依据专业原则评估治疗师回应质量。基于该数据集训练的PsychoCounsel-Llama3-8B模型在人类偏好测试中以87%胜率击败GPT-4o，展现出在生成符合临床规范与共情能力的回应方面的优异表现。
Identifying Inductive Biases for Robot Co-Design
Authors: Apoorv Vaish, Oliver Brock
First: 2026-04-13T17:39:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:39:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Co-designing a robot's morphology and control can ensure synergistic interactions between them, prevalent in biological organisms. However, co-design is a high-dimensional search problem. To make this search tractable, we need a systematic method for identifying inductive biases tailored to its structure. In this paper, we analyze co-design landscapes for soft locomotion and manipulation tasks and identify three patterns that are consistent across regions of their co-design spaces. We observe that within regions of co-design space, quality varies along a low-dimensional manifold. Higher-quality regions exhibit variations spread across more dimensions, while tightly coupling morphology and control. We leverage these insights to devise an efficient co-design algorithm. Since the precise instantiation of this structure varies across tasks and is not known a priori, our algorithm infers it from information gathered during search and adapts to each task's specific structure. This yields $36\%$ more improvement than benchmark algorithms. Moreover, our algorithm achieved more than two orders of magnitude in sample efficiency compared to these benchmark algorithms, demonstrating the effectiveness of leveraging inductive biases to co-design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器人协同设计中的归纳偏置识别
机器人形态与控制的协同设计可确保二者协同作用，这在生物体中普遍存在。然而，协同设计是一个高维搜索问题。为使该搜索可行，我们需要一种系统方法来识别针对其结构定制的归纳偏置。本文分析了软体运动与操作任务的协同设计景观，识别出三个在协同设计空间各区域中一致的模式。我们观察到，在协同设计空间的各区域内，质量沿低维流形变化；高质量区域的变化跨越更多维度，同时紧密耦合形态与控制。我们利用这些见解设计了一种高效的协同设计算法。由于该结构在不同任务中的具体实现各异且事先未知，我们的算法从搜索过程中收集的信息推断该结构，并适应每个任务的特定结构。这比基准算法提升了36%的效果，且在样本效率上比基准算法高出两个数量级，证明了利用归纳偏置进行协同设计的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently co-design robot morphology and control for synergistic performance, this paper identifies inductive biases in high-dimensional co-design landscapes for soft locomotion and manipulation. Through analysis of co-design spaces, it finds that high-quality regions lie on low-dimensional manifolds and exhibit tighter coupling between morphology and control. The authors develop an adaptive algorithm that infers the structure of these manifolds during search and leverages them to guide exploration, resulting in 36% greater improvement over benchmarks and over two orders of magnitude higher sample efficiency.
为高效协同设计机器人形态与控制以实现协同性能，本文通过对软体运动与操作任务的共设计空间分析，识别出三个跨区域一致的归纳偏置模式：高质量区域位于低维流形上，且优质区域维度变化更丰富、形态与控制耦合更紧密。基于此，作者设计了一种自适应算法，在搜索过程中推断结构模式并加以利用，相较基准算法提升36%性能，样本效率提高两个数量级。
$λ_A$: A Typed Lambda Calculus for LLM Agent Composition
Authors: Qin Liu
First: 2026-04-13T17:39:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:39:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM agent frameworks lack formal semantics: there is no principled way to determine whether an agent configuration is well-formed or will terminate. We present $λ_A$, a typed lambda calculus for agent composition that extends the simply-typed lambda calculus with oracle calls, bounded fixpoints (the ReAct loop), probabilistic choice, and mutable environments. We prove type safety, termination of bounded fixpoints, and soundness of derived lint rules, with partial Coq mechanization (1,567 lines, 43 completed proofs). As a practical application, we derive a lint tool that detects structural configuration errors directly from the operational semantics. An evaluation on 835 real-world GitHub agent configurations shows that 94.1% are structurally incomplete under $λ_A$, with YAML-only lint precision at 54%, rising to 96--100% under joint YAML+Python AST analysis on 175 samples. This gap quantifies, for the first time, the degree of semantic entanglement between declarative configuration and imperative code in the agent ecosystem. We further show that five mainstream paradigms (LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, OpenAI SDK, Dify) embed as typed $λ_A$ fragments, establishing $λ_A$ as a unifying calculus for LLM agent composition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$λ_A$: 一种用于大语言模型代理组合的类型化λ演算
现有的大语言模型代理框架缺乏形式语义：没有原则性方法来判断代理配置是否良构或是否会终止。我们提出了$λ_A$，一种用于代理组合的类型化λ演算，它在简单类型λ演算基础上扩展了预言调用、有界不动点（ReAct循环）、概率选择和可变环境。我们证明了类型安全、有界不动点的终止性以及派生的lint规则的正确性，并部分实现了Coq机械化（1,567行，43个完成的证明）。作为实际应用，我们推导出一个lint工具，该工具可直接从操作语义检测结构配置错误。在835个真实GitHub代理配置上的评估显示，94.1%的配置在$λ_A$下结构不完整，仅YAML lint的精确度为54%，而在175个样本上结合YAML与Python AST分析时提升至96–100%。这一差距首次量化了代理生态系统中声明式配置与命令式代码之间的语义纠缠程度。我们进一步表明，五种主流范式（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen、OpenAI SDK、Dify）可嵌入为类型化$λ_A$片段，确立了$λ_A$作为大语言模型代理组合的统一演算。

Experimentally, they find that 94.1% of 835 real-world GitHub agent configurations are structurally incomplete under $λ_A$, and YAML-only linting achieves 54% precision, rising to 96–100% when combined with Python AST analysis. The study also demonstrates that five major agent frameworks embed as typed $λ_A$ fragments, establishing $λ_A$ as a unifying formal model for agent composition.

ParseBench: A Document Parsing Benchmark for AI Agents
Authors: Boyang Zhang, Sebastián G. Acosta, Preston Carlson, Sacha Bron, Pierre-Loïc Doulcet, Daniel B. Ospina, Simon Suo
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:37:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI agents are changing the requirements for document parsing. What matters is semantic correctness: parsed output must preserve the structure and meaning needed for autonomous decisions, including correct table structure, precise chart data, semantically meaningful formatting, and visual grounding. Existing benchmarks do not fully capture this setting for enterprise automation, relying on narrow document distributions and text-similarity metrics that miss agent-critical failures. We introduce ParseBench, a benchmark of ${\sim}2{,}000$ human-verified pages from enterprise documents spanning insurance, finance, and government, organized around five capability dimensions: tables, charts, content faithfulness, semantic formatting, and visual grounding. Across 14 methods spanning vision-language models, specialized document parsers, and LlamaParse, the benchmark reveals a fragmented capability landscape: no method is consistently strong across all five dimensions. LlamaParse Agentic achieves the highest overall score at 84.9%, and the benchmark highlights the remaining capability gaps across current systems. Dataset and evaluation code are available on https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ParseBench and https://github.com/run-llama/ParseBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ParseBench：面向AI代理的文档解析基准
AI代理正在改变文档解析的需求。关键在于语义正确性：解析输出必须保留支持自主决策所需的结构和含义，包括正确的表格结构、精确的图表数据、语义上有意义的格式化以及视觉定位。现有基准未能充分捕捉企业自动化场景下的这一需求，依赖狭窄的文档分布和文本相似性度量，忽略了代理关键的失败模式。我们引入ParseBench，该基准包含约2,000页经人工验证的企业文档（涵盖保险、金融和政府领域），围绕五个能力维度组织：表格、图表、内容忠实度、语义格式化和视觉定位。在涵盖视觉语言模型、专用文档解析器及LlamaParse的14种方法中，该基准揭示了能力分布碎片化：没有任何方法在所有五个维度上表现一致。LlamaParse Agentic获得最高综合得分84.9%，该基准也突显了当前系统仍存在的能力差距。数据集和评估代码可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ParseBench和https://github.com/run-llama/ParseBench获取。

The benchmark exposes critical capability gaps in current systems, particularly in visual grounding and semantic formatting.
为满足AI代理驱动文档解析对语义正确性的需求，ParseBench构建了一个包含约2000页人工验证的企业文档基准，涵盖保险、金融和政府领域，围绕表格、图表、内容忠实度、语义格式和视觉定位五大能力维度。在14种不同方法（包括视觉语言模型和专用解析器）的评估中，该基准揭示了能力分布的碎片化，无一方法在所有维度上表现优异。LlamaParse Agentic得分最高，为84.9%，但仍存在显著能力缺口，凸显了面向代理级决策的更稳健解析系统亟待发展。
MosaicMRI: A Diverse Dataset and Benchmark for Raw Musculoskeletal MRI
Authors: Paula Arguello, Berk Tinaz, Mohammad Shahab Sepehri, Maryam Soltanolkotabi, Mahdi Soltanolkotabi
First: 2026-04-13T17:36:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:36:01+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures, preliminary version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning underpins a wide range of applications in MRI, including reconstruction, artifact removal, and segmentation. However, progress has been driven largely by public datasets focused on brain and knee imaging, shaping how models are trained and evaluated. As a result, careful studies of the reliability of these models across diverse anatomical settings remain limited. In this work, we introduce MosaicMRI, a large and diverse collection of fully sampled raw musculoskeletal (MSK) MR measurements designed for training and evaluating machine-learning-based methods. MosaicMRI is the largest open-source raw MSK MRI dataset to date, comprising 2,671 volumes and 80,156 slices. The dataset offers substantial diversity in volume orientation (e.g., axial, sagittal), imaging contrasts (e.g., PD, T1, T2), anatomies (e.g., spine, knee, hip, ankle, and others), and numbers of acquisition coils. Using VarNet as a baseline for accelerated reconstruction task, we perform a comprehensive set of experiments to study scaling behavior with respect to both model capacity and dataset size. Interestingly, models trained on the combined anatomies significantly outperform anatomy-specific models in low-sample regimes, highlighting the benefits of anatomical diversity and the presence of exploitable cross-anatomical correlations. We further evaluate robustness and cross-anatomy generalization by training models on one anatomy (e.g., spine) and testing them on another (e.g., knee). Notably, we identify groups of body parts (e.g., foot and elbow) that generalize well with each other, and highlight that performance under domain shifts depends on both training set size, anatomy, and protocol-specific factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MosaicMRI：用于原始肌肉骨骼MRI的多样化数据集与基准
深度学习支撑了MRI的多种应用，包括重建、伪影去除和分割。然而，进展主要由聚焦于脑部和膝关节成像的公开数据集驱动，塑造了模型的训练与评估方式。因此，对这些模型在多样化解剖结构中的可靠性进行细致研究仍显不足。本文引入MosaicMRI，这是一个大规模、多样化的完全采样原始肌肉骨骼（MSK）MR测量数据集，专为训练和评估基于机器学习的方法而设计。MosaicMRI是迄今为止最大的开源原始MSK MRI数据集，包含2,671个体积和80,156个切片。该数据集在体积方向（如轴向、矢状位）、成像对比度（如PD、T1、T2）、解剖结构（如脊柱、膝关节、髋关节、踝关节等）及采集线圈数量方面具有显著多样性。以VarNet为加速重建任务的基线模型，我们进行了一系列全面实验，研究模型容量和数据集规模对性能的扩展效应。有趣的是，在样本量较少的情况下，跨解剖结构训练的模型显著优于特定解剖结构模型，凸显了解剖多样性及可利用的跨解剖相关性的优势。我们进一步通过在一种解剖结构（如脊柱）上训练模型并在另一种（如膝关节）上测试，评估其鲁棒性与跨解剖泛化能力。值得注意的是，我们识别出某些身体部位组（如足部与肘部）彼此泛化效果良好，并指出在域迁移场景下的性能依赖于训练集规模、解剖结构及协议特定因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of diverse musculoskeletal MRI data to evaluate deep learning models beyond brain and knee imaging, this work introduces MosaicMRI, the largest open-source raw MSK MRI dataset with 2,671 volumes and 80,156 slices, covering varied anatomies, orientations, contrasts, and coil configurations. Using VarNet as a baseline, experiments demonstrate that models trained on combined anatomies outperform anatomy-specific models in low-data regimes, revealing benefits of cross-anatomical generalization. Further tests show that certain body parts (e.g., foot and elbow) generalize well across anatomies, while performance under domain shifts depends on training size, anatomy, and acquisition protocols.
为弥补当前深度学习模型在肌肉骨骼MRI领域缺乏多样化数据的问题，本研究提出MosaicMRI，这是目前最大的开源原始MSK MRI数据集，包含2671个体积和80156个切片，涵盖多种解剖部位、方向、对比度和线圈配置。基于VarNet基线模型的实验表明，联合解剖部位训练的模型在数据量少时表现优于特定解剖部位模型，凸显了解剖多样性与跨解剖相关性的价值。跨解剖泛化测试进一步发现，部分身体部位（如足部与肘部）之间泛化效果较好，而域迁移性能取决于训练数据量、解剖部位及协议相关因素。
Grounded Forcing: Bridging Time-Independent Semantics and Proximal Dynamics in Autoregressive Video Synthesis
Authors: Jintao Chen, Chengyu Bai, Junjun Hu, Xinda Xue, Mu Xu
First: 2026-04-08T11:03:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:35:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive video synthesis offers a promising pathway for infinite-horizon generation but is fundamentally hindered by three intertwined challenges: semantic forgetting from context limitations, visual drift due to positional extrapolation, and controllability loss during interactive instruction switching. Current methods often tackle these issues in isolation, limiting long-term coherence. We introduce Grounded Forcing, a novel framework that bridges time-independent semantics and proximal dynamics through three interlocking mechanisms. First, to address semantic forgetting, we propose a Dual Memory KV Cache that decouples local temporal dynamics from global semantic anchors, ensuring long-term semantic coherence and identity stability. Second, to suppress visual drift, we design Dual-Reference RoPE Injection, which confines positional embeddings within the training manifold while rendering global semantics time-invariant. Third, to resolve controllability issues, we develop Asymmetric Proximity Recache, which facilitates smooth semantic inheritance during prompt transitions via proximity-weighted cache updates. These components operate synergistically to tether the generative process to stable semantic cores while accommodating flexible local dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Grounded Forcing significantly enhances long-range consistency and visual stability, establishing a robust foundation for interactive long-form video synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：grounded forcing：在自回归视频合成中连接时间无关语义与邻近动态
自回归视频合成提供了无限时长生成的有前景路径，但受三大相互关联的挑战制约：因上下文限制导致的语义遗忘、因位置外推引发的视觉漂移，以及交互式指令切换时的可控性损失。现有方法常孤立应对这些问题，限制了长期连贯性。我们提出Grounded Forcing框架，通过三个相互关联的机制连接时间无关语义与邻近动态。首先，为解决语义遗忘，我们提出双记忆KV缓存，将局部时间动态与全局语义锚点解耦，确保长期语义连贯性和身份稳定性。其次，为抑制视觉漂移，我们设计双参考RoPE注入，使位置嵌入限定在训练流形内，同时使全局语义时间不变。第三，为解决可控性问题，我们开发非对称邻近重缓存，通过邻近加权缓存更新，在提示切换时实现平滑语义继承。这些组件协同作用，将生成过程锚定在稳定语义核心上，同时容纳灵活局部动态。大量实验表明，Grounded Forcing显著提升长程一致性和视觉稳定性，为交互式长视频合成奠定坚实基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of autoregressive video synthesis in maintaining semantic coherence and visual stability over long horizons, the paper proposes Grounded Forcing, a framework that integrates time-independent semantics with proximal dynamics through three core mechanisms: a Dual Memory KV Cache to preserve global semantic anchors and prevent forgetting, Dual-Reference RoPE Injection to constrain positional embeddings and avoid drift, and Asymmetric Proximity Recache to enable smooth semantic transitions during prompt switching. Experimental results show that this approach significantly improves long-range consistency and visual stability in generated videos, enabling more reliable interactive synthesis.
为解决自回归视频合成中长期语义一致性与视觉稳定性不足的问题，作者提出Grounded Forcing框架，通过三个协同机制实现时间无关语义与局部动态的融合：双记忆KV缓存用于分离局部时序动态与全局语义锚点以防止遗忘，双参考RoPE注入用于约束位置嵌入避免视觉漂移，非对称邻近重缓存用于在提示切换时平滑继承语义。实验表明，该方法显著提升了长序列生成的视觉稳定性和语义一致性，为交互式长视频合成提供了可靠基础。
PnP-CM: Consistency Models as Plug-and-Play Priors for Inverse Problems
Authors: Merve Gülle, Junno Yun, Yaşar Utku Alçalar, Mehmet Akçakaya
Venue: CVPR
First: 2025-09-25T20:27:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:31:52+00:00
Comments: IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have found extensive use in solving inverse problems, by sampling from an approximate posterior distribution of data given the measurements. Recently, consistency models (CMs) have been proposed to directly predict the final output from any point on the diffusion ODE trajectory, enabling high-quality sampling in just a few neural function evaluations (NFEs). CMs have also been utilized for inverse problems, but existing CM-based solvers either require additional task-specific training or utilize data fidelity operations with slow convergence, limiting their applicability to large-scale problems and making them difficult to extend to nonlinear settings. In this work, we reinterpret CMs as proximal operators of a prior, enabling their integration into plug-and-play (PnP) frameworks. Specifically, we propose PnP-CM, an ADMM-based PnP solver that provides a unified framework for solving a wide range of inverse problems, and incorporates noise perturbations and momentum-based updates to improve performance in the low-NFE regime. We evaluate our approach on a diverse set of linear and nonlinear inverse problems. We also train and apply CMs to MRI data for the first time. Our results show that PnP-CM achieves high-quality reconstructions in as few as 4 NFEs, and produces meaningful results in 2 steps, highlighting its effectiveness in real-world inverse problems while outperforming existing CM-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PnP-CM：将一致性模型作为即插即用先验用于逆问题
扩散模型已广泛用于解决逆问题，通过从给定测量值的数据近似后验分布中采样。近期，一致性模型（CMs）被提出，可直接从扩散ODE轨迹上的任意点预测最终输出，仅需少量神经函数评估（NFEs）即可实现高质量采样。CMs亦已用于逆问题，但现有基于CM的求解器要么需要额外的任务特定训练，要么依赖收敛缓慢的数据保真操作，限制了其在大规模问题中的适用性，并难以扩展至非线性场景。本文将CM重新诠释为先验的近端算子，使其可集成到即插即用（PnP）框架中。具体而言，我们提出PnP-CM，一种基于ADMM的PnP求解器，提供统一框架以解决各类逆问题，并引入噪声扰动和基于动量的更新机制，以提升低NFE场景下的性能。我们在多样化的线性和非线性逆问题上评估该方法，首次将CM应用于MRI数据训练与处理。结果表明，PnP-CM仅需4个NFE即可实现高质量重建，2步即可产生有意义结果，凸显其在真实逆问题中的有效性，并优于现有基于CM的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and scalable inverse problem solvers, this work reinterprets consistency models as proximal operators of a prior, enabling their integration into plug-and-play frameworks. The proposed PnP-CM method employs an ADMM-based solver with noise perturbations and momentum updates to accelerate convergence in low-neural-function-evaluation regimes. Experimental results across diverse linear and nonlinear inverse problems demonstrate that PnP-CM achieves high-quality reconstructions in as few as four NFEs, and even produces meaningful outputs in just two steps, outperforming existing CM-based approaches and marking the first application of CMs to MRI data.
为解决逆问题求解中效率与可扩展性不足的问题，本文将一致性模型重新诠释为先验的近端算子，使其可嵌入插拔式框架。所提出的PnP-CM方法基于ADMM求解器，结合噪声扰动与动量更新，以加速低神经函数评估次数下的收敛并提升性能。在多种线性和非线性逆问题（含首次应用于MRI重建）上评估，PnP-CM仅需4次神经函数评估即可获得高质量重建，2步内即可生成有意义结果，优于现有基于一致性模型的方法。
Retrieval Is Not Enough: Why Organizational AI Needs Epistemic Infrastructure
Authors: Federico Bottino, Carlo Ferrero, Nicholas Dosio, Pierfrancesco Beneventano
First: 2026-04-13T17:31:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:31:14+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables, 6 appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Organizational knowledge used by AI agents typically lacks epistemic structure: retrieval systems surface semantically relevant content without distinguishing binding decisions from abandoned hypotheses, contested claims from settled ones, or known facts from unresolved questions. We argue that the ceiling on organizational AI is not retrieval fidelity but \emph{epistemic} fidelity--the system's ability to represent commitment strength, contradiction status, and organizational ignorance as computable properties. We present OIDA, a framework that structures organizational knowledge as typed Knowledge Objects carrying epistemic class, importance scores with class-specific decay, and signed contradiction edges. The Knowledge Gravity Engine maintains scores deterministically with proved convergence guarantees (sufficient condition: max degree $< 7$; empirically robust to degree 43). OIDA introduces QUESTION-as-modeled-ignorance: a primitive with inverse decay that surfaces what an organization does \emph{not} know with increasing urgency--a mechanism absent from all surveyed systems. We describe the Epistemic Quality Score (EQS), a five-component evaluation methodology with explicit circularity analysis. In a controlled comparison ($n{=}10$ response pairs), OIDA's RAG condition (3,868 tokens) achieves EQS 0.530 vs.\ 0.848 for a full-context baseline (108,687 tokens); the $28.1\times$ token budget difference is the primary confound. The QUESTION mechanism is statistically validated (Fisher $p{=}0.0325$, OR$=21.0$). The formal properties are established; the decisive ablation at equal token budget (E4) is pre-registered and not yet run.

标题：检索不足：为何组织AI需要认识论基础设施
AI代理使用的组织知识通常缺乏认识论结构：检索系统仅呈现语义相关的内容，而无法区分绑定决策与废弃假设、争议主张与已定结论、已知事实与未决问题。我们认为，组织AI的上限并非检索精度，而是\emph{认识论}精度——即系统能否将承诺强度、矛盾状态和组织无知表示为可计算属性。 我们提出了OIDA框架，将组织知识结构化为携带认识论类别的类型化知识对象，并附带类特定衰减的重要性评分及带符号的矛盾边。知识引力引擎以确定性方式维护评分，并具备已证明的收敛保证（充分条件：最大度数$<7$；实证上对度数43仍稳健）。OIDA引入了“问题”作为建模无知的原始概念：其具有反向衰减机制，能日益紧迫地揭示组织所不知的内容——这是所有调研系统所缺乏的机制。我们描述了认识论质量评分（EQS），一种包含五个组件的评估方法，明确包含循环性分析。在受控比较（$n{=}10$响应对）中，OIDA的RAG条件（3,868个token）获得EQS 0.530，而完整上下文基线（108,687个token）为0.848；28.1倍的token预算差异是主要干扰因素。问题机制经统计验证（Fisher $p{=}0.0325$，OR$=21.0$）。形式属性已确立；在等token预算下的关键消融实验（E4）已预注册，尚未运行。

该论文指出，组织AI的瓶颈不在于检索精度，而在于无法建模知识的表征结构，如承诺强度、矛盾状态和组织无知。为此，作者提出OIDA框架，将组织知识建模为带类型的知识对象，包含表征类、重要性评分和带符号的矛盾边，并通过知识引力引擎进行确定性更新，具备收敛保证。系统引入“QUESTION”原语，以逆衰减方式建模无知，使组织未知内容随时间更紧迫地浮现。通过表征质量评分（EQS）评估，OIDA在远低于基线的token预算下仍表现更优，且QUESTION机制经统计验证（Fisher p=0.0325，OR=21.0）。
StarVLA-$α$: Reducing Complexity in Vision-Language-Action Systems
Authors: Jinhui Ye, Ning Gao, Senqiao Yang, Jinliang Zheng, Zixuan Wang, Yuxin Chen, Pengguang Chen, Yilun Chen, Shu Liu, Jiaya Jia
First: 2026-04-13T17:30:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:30:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for building general-purpose robotic agents. However, the VLA landscape remains highly fragmented and complex: as existing approaches vary substantially in architectures, training data, embodiment configurations, and benchmark-specific engineering. In this work, we introduce StarVLA-$α$, a simple yet strong baseline designed to study VLA design choices under controlled conditions. StarVLA-$α$ deliberately minimizes architectural and pipeline complexity to reduce experimental confounders and enable systematic analysis. Specifically, we re-evaluate several key design axes, including action modeling strategies, robot-specific pretraining, and interface engineering. Across unified multi-benchmark training on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, RoboTwin, and RoboCasa, the same simple baseline remains highly competitive, indicating that a strong VLM backbone combined with minimal design is already sufficient to achieve strong performance without relying on additional architectural complexity or engineering tricks. Notably, our single generalist model outperforms $π_{0.5}$ by 20\% on the public real-world RoboChallenge benchmark. We expect StarVLA-$α$ to serve as a solid starting point for future research in the VLA regime. Code will be released at https://github.com/starVLA/starVLA.

Angle-based Localization and Rigidity Maintenance Control for Multi-Robot Networks
Authors: J. Francisco Presenza, Leonardo J. Colombo, Juan I. Giribet, Ignacio Mas
First: 2026-04-13T17:28:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:28:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we study angle-based localization and rigidity maintenance control for multi-robot networks under sensing constraints. We establish the first equivalence between angle rigidity and bearing rigidity considering \textit{directed} sensing graphs and \textit{body-frame} bearing measurements in both $2$ and $3$-\textit{dimensional space}. In particular, we demonstrate that a framework in $\mathrm{SE}(d)$ is infinitesimally bearing rigid if and only if it is infinitesimally angle rigid and each robot obtains at least $d-1$ bearing measurements ($d \in \{2, 3\}$). Building on these findings, this paper proposes a distributed angle-based localization scheme and establishes local exponential stability under switching sensing graphs, requiring only infinitesimal angle rigidity across the visited topologies. Then, since angle rigidity strongly depends on the robots' spatial configuration, we investigate rigidity maintenance control. The \textit{angle rigidity eigenvalue} is presented as a metric for the degree of rigidity. A decentralized gradient-based controller capable of executing mission-specific commands while maintaining a sufficient level of angle rigidity is proposed. Simulations were conducted to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多机器人网络的角度定位与刚性维持控制
本文研究了在感知约束下多机器人网络的角度定位与刚性维持控制。我们首次建立了在定向感知图和体坐标系方向测量的二维和三维空间中，角度刚性与方向刚性的等价关系。特别地，我们证明了在SE(d)框架中，系统在无穷小方向刚性当且仅当其在无穷小角度刚性，且每个机器人至少获得d-1个方向测量（d∈{2,3}）。基于这些发现，本文提出了一种分布式角度定位方案，并在切换感知图下建立了局部指数稳定性，仅需在访问的拓扑结构中满足无穷小角度刚性。由于角度刚性高度依赖于机器人的空间构型，我们进一步研究了刚性维持控制。提出了“角度刚性特征值”作为刚性程度的度量。设计了一种分布式梯度控制器，能够在执行特定任务的同时维持足够的角度刚性。通过仿真评估了该方案的有效性和实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust localization and rigidity maintenance in multi-robot systems under sensing constraints, this work establishes the first equivalence between angle rigidity and bearing rigidity in directed sensing graphs using body-frame measurements in 2D and 3D space, showing that infinitesimal bearing rigidity is equivalent to infinitesimal angle rigidity when each robot has at least d−1 bearing measurements. Based on this, a distributed angle-based localization scheme is proposed, achieving local exponential stability under switching sensing graphs with only infinitesimal angle rigidity required. Additionally, to maintain rigidity during operation, an angle rigidity eigenvalue metric is introduced and a decentralized gradient-based controller is designed to execute mission commands while preserving sufficient rigidity, validated through simulations.
本文针对感知受限下的多机器人网络定位与刚性维持问题，首次在有向感知图和体坐标系测量条件下，建立了角度刚性与测向刚性在二维和三维空间中的等价关系，证明当每个机器人获得至少d−1个测向量（d=2,3）时，角度刚性等价于测向刚性。在此基础上，提出了一种分布式角度定位方案，在感知图切换时仍能实现局部指数稳定，前提是网络在访问的拓扑结构中保持角度刚性。进一步地，引入“角度刚性特征值”作为刚性度量，并设计了一种分布式梯度控制器，使机器人在执行任务的同时维持足够刚性，仿真结果验证了该方案的有效性与实用性。
Agentic Aggregation for Parallel Scaling of Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks
Authors: Yoonsang Lee, Howard Yen, Xi Ye, Danqi Chen
First: 2026-04-13T17:26:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:26:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study parallel test-time scaling for long-horizon agentic tasks such as agentic search and deep research, where multiple rollouts are generated in parallel and aggregated into a final response. While such scaling has proven effective for chain-of-thought reasoning, agentic tasks pose unique challenges: trajectories are long, multi-turn, and tool-augmented, and outputs are often open-ended. Aggregating only final answers discards rich information from trajectories, while concatenating all trajectories exceeds the model's context window. To address this, we propose AggAgent, an aggregation agent that treats parallel trajectories as an environment. We equip it with lightweight tools to inspect candidate solutions and search across trajectories, enabling it to navigate and synthesize information on demand. Across six benchmarks and three model families (GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5, MiniMax-M2.5), AggAgent outperforms all existing aggregation methods-by up to 5.3% absolute on average and 10.3% on two deep research tasks-while adding minimal overhead, as the aggregation cost remains bounded by a single agentic rollout. Our findings establish agentic aggregation as an effective and cost-efficient approach to parallel test-time scaling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向长时程代理任务的并行扩展代理聚合方法
我们研究长时程代理任务（如代理搜索和深度研究）的并行测试时扩展，其中多个并行轨迹生成后聚合为最终响应。虽然此类扩展在链式思维推理中已证明有效，但代理任务具有独特挑战：轨迹长、多轮交互且工具增强，输出常为开放性。仅聚合最终答案会丢弃轨迹中的丰富信息，而拼接所有轨迹则超出模型上下文窗口。为此，我们提出AggAgent，将并行轨迹视为环境，为其配备轻量工具以检查候选解并跨轨迹搜索，使其能按需导航与合成信息。在六个基准和三个模型家族（GLM-4.7、Qwen3.5、MiniMax-M2.5）上，AggAgent平均绝对性能优于所有现有聚合方法5.3%，在两项深度研究任务上提升10.3%，且开销极小，聚合成本仍受限于单次代理轨迹。我们的研究确立了代理聚合作为并行测试时扩展的有效且低成本方法。

Motivated by the need to scale long-horizon agentic tasks like search and deep research through parallel test-time execution, this work introduces AggAgent, an aggregation agent that treats parallel trajectories as an environment. It uses lightweight tools to inspect and synthesize information across trajectories, avoiding the pitfalls of either discarding intermediate steps or exceeding context limits. Experiments across six benchmarks and three model families show AggAgent outperforms existing methods by up to 5.3% absolute on average and 10.3% on two deep research tasks, with aggregation overhead bounded by a single agentic rollout.
为解决长时程代理任务（如搜索与深度研究）在并行测试时的扩展问题，本文提出AggAgent，将并行轨迹视为环境，通过轻量工具动态检索与合成信息，避免仅聚合最终答案或拼接全部轨迹导致的信息丢失或上下文溢出。在六个基准和三类模型上测试，AggAgent平均绝对性能提升达5.3%，在两项深度研究任务上提升10.3%，且开销极小，聚合成本不超过单次代理推理。
Graph-Coarsening Approach for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows
Authors: Mustafa Mert Özyılmaz
First: 2025-10-25T15:13:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:26:07+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 30 figures. A revised version with quantum solver experiment results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) is a fundamental NP-hard optimization problem in logistics. Solving large-scale instances remains computationally challenging for exact solvers. This paper introduces a multilevel graph coarsening and refinement strategy that aggregates customers into meta-nodes based on a spatio-temporal distance metric. The reduced problem is solved using both classical heuristics and quantum annealing hardware, then expanded back into the original space with arrival times recomputed and constraint violations recorded. Comprehensive experiments on Solomon benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly reduces computation time while preserving solution quality for classical heuristics. For quantum solvers, experiments across all 56 Solomon instances at $N=5$ and $N=10$ customers show that coarsening consistently reduces computation time and, on clustered (C-type) instances, simultaneously reduces vehicle count and route duration with no feasibility loss. Coarsening effectiveness is strongly instance-structure dependent: C-type instances achieve %100 post-coarsening feasibility with measurable quality improvements, while narrow-window random (R-type) instances present structural constraints that limit achievable coarsening depth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题的图粗化方法
带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）是物流领域中的一个基础NP难优化问题。求解大规模实例对精确求解器而言仍具计算挑战性。本文提出一种多层级图粗化与细化策略，基于时空距离度量将客户聚合为元节点。简化后的问题采用经典启发式算法和量子退火硬件求解，再通过重新计算到达时间并记录约束违反情况扩展回原始空间。在Solomon基准测试中，全面实验表明，该方法显著减少计算时间，同时保持经典启发式算法的解质量。对于量子求解器，对所有56个Solomon实例在N=5和N=10个客户条件下进行的实验显示，粗化始终减少计算时间；在聚类型（C型）实例中，同时减少车辆数量和路线时长，且无可行性损失。粗化效果高度依赖实例结构：C型实例粗化后100%保持可行性并有可测量的质量提升，而窄窗口随机型（R型）实例因结构约束限制了可实现的粗化深度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational difficulty of solving large-scale Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problems with Time Windows (CVRPTW), this paper proposes a multilevel graph coarsening approach that aggregates customers into meta-nodes using a spatio-temporal distance metric. The reduced problem is solved via classical heuristics or quantum annealing, then refined back to the original space with recomputed arrival times and constraint tracking. Experiments on Solomon benchmarks show that classical heuristics benefit from reduced computation time with preserved solution quality, while quantum solvers see consistent speedups and, on clustered instances, also achieve fewer vehicles and shorter routes without feasibility loss. Coarsening effectiveness varies by instance type: C-type instances maintain 100% feasibility with quality gains, whereas R-type instances face structural limitations that restrict coarsening depth.
为应对大规模带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）的计算挑战，本文提出一种多层图粗化方法，依据时空距离度量将客户聚合成元节点。粗化后的问题通过经典启发式算法或量子退火硬件求解，再通过重新计算到达时间与约束检查恢复原问题规模。实验表明，经典算法显著提速且解质量不变，量子求解器在所有56个Solomon实例中均缩短计算时间，对集群型（C型）实例还能减少车辆数与路线时长而不失可行性。粗化效果依赖实例结构：C型实例保持100%可行性并提升解质量，而窄窗口随机（R型）实例因结构限制难以实现深度粗化。
Grounded World Model for Semantically Generalizable Planning
Authors: Quanyi Li, Lan Feng, Haonan Zhang, Wuyang Li, Letian Wang, Alexandre Alahi, Harold Soh
First: 2026-04-13T17:25:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:25:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Model Predictive Control (MPC), world models predict the future outcomes of various action proposals, which are then scored to guide the selection of the optimal action. For visuomotor MPC, the score function is a distance metric between a predicted image and a goal image, measured in the latent space of a pretrained vision encoder like DINO and JEPA. However, it is challenging to obtain the goal image in advance of the task execution, particularly in new environments. Additionally, conveying the goal through an image offers limited interactivity compared with natural language. In this work, we propose to learn a Grounded World Model (GWM) in a vision-language-aligned latent space. As a result, each proposed action is scored based on how close its future outcome is to the task instruction, reflected by the similarity of embeddings. This approach transforms the visuomotor MPC to a VLA that surpasses VLM-based VLAs in semantic generalization. On the proposed WISER benchmark, GWM-MPC achieves a 87% success rate on the test set comprising 288 tasks that feature unseen visual signals and referring expressions, yet remain solvable with motions demonstrated during training. In contrast, traditional VLAs achieve an average success rate of 22%, even though they overfit the training set with a 90% success rate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于语义泛化规划的接地世界模型
在模型预测控制（MPC）中，世界模型预测各种动作提案的未来结果，然后根据得分指导最优动作的选择。对于视觉运动MPC，得分函数是预测图像与目标图像在预训练视觉编码器（如DINO和JEPA）的潜在空间中的距离度量。然而，在任务执行前难以提前获得目标图像，特别是在新环境中。此外，通过图像传达目标相比自然语言交互性有限。本文提出在视觉-语言对齐的潜在空间中学习接地世界模型（GWM）。因此，每个动作提案的得分基于其未来结果与任务指令的接近程度，通过嵌入相似度体现。该方法将视觉运动MPC转化为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统，在语义泛化能力上超越基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的VLA。在提出的WISER基准测试中，GWM-MPC在包含288个未见过视觉信号和指代表达但可通过训练期间演示动作解决的任务测试集上实现了87%的成功率；相比之下，传统VLA系统尽管在训练集上过拟合达到90%的成功率，但在测试集上的平均成功率仅为22%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of using goal images in visuomotor Model Predictive Control—particularly in novel environments and their lack of natural language interactivity—the paper proposes a Grounded World Model (GWM) that learns in a vision-language-aligned latent space. Instead of comparing predicted images to goal images, GWM scores actions based on how closely their future outcomes match the task instruction via embedding similarity. Evaluated on the WISER benchmark with 288 unseen tasks, GWM-MPC achieved an 87% success rate, significantly outperforming traditional VLM-based VLAs, which averaged only 22% success despite overfitting training data with 90% accuracy.
为了解决视觉运动模型预测控制中依赖预设目标图像、难以适应新环境且交互性差的问题，本文提出一种在视觉-语言对齐潜在空间中学习的接地世界模型（GWM）。该模型通过动作预测结果与自然语言任务指令语义嵌入的相似度来评分，而非图像距离。在WISER基准测试中，GWM-MPC在288个包含未见视觉信号和指代表达的新任务上达成87%成功率，而传统视觉-语言模型尽管在训练集上达到90%准确率，却仅在测试集上获得22%成功率。
Personality Pairing Improves Human-AI Collaboration
Authors: Harang Ju, Sinan Aral
First: 2025-11-17T23:15:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:24:57+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Here we examine how AI agent "personalities" interact with human personalities to shape human-AI collaboration and performance. In a large-scale, preregistered randomized experiment, we paired 1,258 participants with AI agents prompted to exhibit varying levels of the Big Five personality traits. These human-AI teams produced 7,266 display ads for a real think tank, which we evaluated using 1,995 independent human raters and a field experiment on X that generated nearly 5 million impressions. We found that human and AI personalities individually shaped ad quality and teamwork. When examined together, human-AI personality pairings directly effected ad quality outcomes. For example, extraverted humans paired with conscientious AI produced the lowest-quality ads, followed by conscientious humans paired with agreeable AI and neurotic humans paired with conscientious AI. In the field experiment, ad quality significantly influenced ad performance, measured by click-through rates and cost-per-click, and neurotic humans paired with neurotic AI achieved higher click-through rates, even after controlling for ad quality. Together, these results provide the first large-scale causal experimental evidence that specific personality pairings can improve human-AI collaboration and motivate future research on the implications of AI personalization for performance and teamwork dynamics in human-AI teams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人格匹配提升人机协作效果
本文探讨了AI代理的“人格”如何与人类人格互动，从而塑造人机协作与表现。在一项大规模、预先注册的随机实验中，我们将1258名参与者与被提示表现出不同五大人格特质水平的AI代理配对。这些人类-AI团队为一家真实智库创作了7266条展示广告，我们通过1995名独立人类评审员评估，并在X平台开展实地实验，产生近500万次曝光量。研究发现，人类与AI的人格分别影响广告质量与团队协作；当二者结合考察时，人机人格配对直接决定广告质量结果。例如，外向型人类配对尽责型AI产生最低质量广告，其次是尽责型人类配对随和型AI，以及神经质人类配对尽责型AI。在实地实验中，广告质量显著影响广告表现（点击率与每次点击成本），且神经质人类配对神经质AI即使控制广告质量后仍获得更高点击率。综合结果首次提供了大规模因果实验证据，表明特定人格配对可提升人机协作，并激励未来研究AI个性化对人机团队绩效与协作动态的影响。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how matching human and AI personalities influences collaboration and performance in human-AI teams. In a large-scale, preregistered experiment, 1,258 participants were paired with AI agents exhibiting varying levels of Big Five personality traits, and together they created 7,266 display ads for a real think tank. These ads were evaluated by 1,995 raters and tested in a field experiment generating nearly 5 million impressions. Results showed that both human and AI personalities independently affected ad quality and teamwork, but their combined pairing had a direct impact on outcomes—specifically, extraverted humans with conscientious AI produced the worst ads, while neurotic humans paired with neurotic AI achieved higher click-through rates even after controlling for quality, suggesting personality alignment can enhance performance.
本研究探讨AI代理人格与人类人格如何相互作用，影响人机协作与绩效。在一项大规模预注册随机实验中，1258名参与者被配对与具有不同“大五人格”特质的AI代理，共同创作了7266条真实智库的展示广告。这些广告由1995名独立人类评审员评估，并在X平台进行实地实验，获得近500万次曝光。结果表明，人类与AI人格各自独立影响广告质量与协作，但其组合对结果有直接作用：例如，外向型人类配对严谨型AI产出质量最低的广告，而神经质人类配对神经质AI即使控制广告质量后仍获得更高点击率。这些发现首次提供了大规模因果证据，证明特定人格配对可提升人机协作绩效。
HistLens: Mapping Idea Change across Concepts and Corpora
Authors: Yi Jing, Weiyun Qiu, Yihang Peng, Zhifang Sui
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-13T17:22:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:22:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 MainConference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language change both reflects and shapes social processes, and the semantic evolution of foundational concepts provides a measurable trace of historical and social transformation. Despite recent advances in diachronic semantics and discourse analysis, existing computational approaches often (i) concentrate on a single concept or a single corpus, making findings difficult to compare across heterogeneous sources, and (ii) remain confined to surface lexical evidence, offering insufficient computational and interpretive granularity when concepts are expressed implicitly. We propose HistLens, a unified, SAE-based framework for multi-concept, multi-corpus conceptual-history analysis. The framework decomposes concept representations into interpretable features and tracks their activation dynamics over time and across sources, yielding comparable conceptual trajectories within a shared coordinate system. Experiments on long-span press corpora show that HistLens supports cross-concept, cross-corpus computation of patterns of idea evolution and enables implicit concept computation. By bridging conceptual modeling with interpretive needs, HistLens broadens the analytical perspectives and methodological repertoire available to social science and the humanities for diachronic text analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HistLens：跨概念与语料库的观念变迁映射
语言变化既反映又塑造社会进程，基础概念的语义演变提供了历史与社会变迁的可测量轨迹。尽管历时语义学与话语分析领域近期取得进展，现有计算方法常（i）聚焦单一概念或单一语料库，导致跨异质来源的比较困难；（ii）局限于表层词汇证据，当概念隐含表达时，缺乏足够的计算与解释粒度。我们提出HistLens，一种基于SAE的统一多概念、多语料库概念历史分析框架。该框架将概念表示分解为可解释特征，并追踪其随时间与跨来源的激活动态，从而在共享坐标系内生成可比较的概念轨迹。在长跨度新闻语料库上的实验表明，HistLens支持跨概念、跨语料库的观念演变模式计算，并能处理隐含概念。通过连接概念建模与解释需求，HistLens拓展了社会科学与人文学科在历时文本分析中的分析视角与方法论工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how foundational concepts evolve across time and across diverse textual sources, the authors introduce HistLens, a unified framework based on semantic autoencoders that enables multi-concept and multi-corpus analysis of conceptual change. The method decomposes concept representations into interpretable features and tracks their activation dynamics across time and corpora, allowing for comparable conceptual trajectories within a shared coordinate system. Experiments on long-span press corpora demonstrate that HistLens successfully captures cross-concept and cross-corpus patterns of idea evolution, including implicit conceptual expressions, thereby offering a more granular and interpretable approach to diachronic text analysis than prior methods.
为理解基础概念如何随时间及不同文本来源演变，作者提出HistLens框架，该框架基于SAE，支持多概念、多语料库的语义变迁分析。方法将概念表示分解为可解释特征，并追踪其在时间与语料库间的激活动态，使不同概念在统一坐标系下具有可比性。在长跨度新闻语料实验中，HistLens成功捕捉跨概念、跨语料库的观念演变模式，包括隐性概念表达，从而提升历史文本分析在社会科学与人文学科中的解释粒度与方法多样性。
LangFlow: Continuous Diffusion Rivals Discrete in Language Modeling
Authors: Yuxin Chen, Chumeng Liang, Hangke Sui, Ruihan Guo, Chaoran Cheng, Jiaxuan You, Ge Liu
First: 2026-04-13T17:21:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:21:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Continuous diffusion models have achieved strong performance across domains such as images. However, in language modeling, prior continuous diffusion language models (DLMs) lag behind discrete counterparts. In this work, we close this gap with LangFlow, the first continuous DLM to rival discrete diffusion. Our approach connects embedding-space DLMs to Flow Matching via Bregman divergence and introduces three key innovations: (1) a novel ODE-based NLL bound for principled evaluation of continuous flow-based language models; (2) an information-uniform principle for noise scheduling, motivating a learnable scheduler based on a Gumbel distribution; and (3) an improved training protocol incorporating self-conditioning, which enhances both likelihood and sample quality.LangFlow achieves strong performance across benchmarks, reaching a perplexity (PPL) of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText. It matches top discrete DLMs at comparable scale and surpasses autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer across multiple benchmarks. LangFlow provides clear evidence that continuous diffusion is a competitive and promising paradigm for language modeling. https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
中文标题/摘要
标题：LangFlow：连续扩散在语言建模中可媲美离散模型
连续扩散模型已在图像等领域取得强大性能。然而，在语言建模中，此前的连续扩散语言模型（DLMs）落后于离散模型。本研究通过LangFlow填补这一差距，这是首个可媲美离散扩散的连续DLM。我们的方法通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间DLMs与流匹配相连，并引入三项关键创新：（1）基于ODE的新型NLL界，用于原则性评估连续流语言模型；（2）信息均匀原则指导噪声调度，提出基于Gumbel分布的可学习调度器；（3）改进的训练协议结合自条件化，提升似然与样本质量。LangFlow在多个基准测试中表现强劲，在LM1B上达到30.0的困惑度（PPL），在OpenWebText上为24.6，与同等规模的顶尖离散DLMs持平，并在多个基准的零样本迁移中超越自回归基线。LangFlow提供了连续扩散在语言建模中具有竞争力和前景的明确证据。 https://github.com/nealchen2003/LangFlow
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the strong performance of continuous diffusion in image generation but its underperformance in language modeling, this work introduces LangFlow, the first continuous diffusion language model that rivals discrete models. It connects embedding-space language models to flow matching via Bregman divergence and introduces three innovations: a novel ODE-based negative log likelihood bound for principled evaluation, an information-uniform noise scheduler based on Gumbel distribution, and a self-conditioning training protocol. Experiments show LangFlow achieves a perplexity of 30.0 on LM1B and 24.6 on OpenWebText, matching top discrete models at comparable scale and outperforming autoregressive baselines in zero-shot transfer across benchmarks, demonstrating continuous diffusion’s competitiveness in language modeling.
为解决连续扩散模型在语言建模中落后于离散模型的问题，本文提出LangFlow，首个可与离散扩散模型媲美的连续扩散语言模型。该方法通过Bregman散度将嵌入空间语言模型与流匹配连接，并引入三项创新：基于ODE的NLL界用于原理性评估、基于Gumbel分布的自适应噪声调度器、以及结合自条件的训练协议以提升似然与样本质量。实验表明，LangFlow在LM1B上达到30.0的困惑度，在OpenWebText上达到24.6，与同等规模的离散模型表现相当，并在多个基准上超越自回归基线的零样本迁移性能，证明连续扩散在语言建模中具有竞争力。
KL Divergence Between Gaussians: A Step-by-Step Derivation for the Variational Autoencoder Objective
Authors: Andrés Muñoz, Rodrigo Ramele
First: 2026-04-13T17:18:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:18:20+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, no figures. Derivation of the KL divergence between Gaussian distributions with application to Variational Autoencoders (VAEs)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence is a fundamental concept in information theory that quantifies the discrepancy between two probability distributions. In the context of Variational Autoencoders (VAEs), it serves as a central regularization term, imposing structure on the latent space and thereby enabling the model to exhibit generative capabilities. In this work, we present a detailed derivation of the closed-form expression for the KL divergence between Gaussian distributions, a case of particular importance in practical VAE implementations. Starting from the general definition for continuous random variables, we derive the expression for the univariate case and extend it to the multivariate setting under the assumption of diagonal covariance. Finally, we discuss the interpretation of each term in the resulting expression and its impact on the training dynamics of the model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯分布间的KL散度：变分自编码器目标函数的逐步推导
Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度是信息论中的基本概念，用于量化两个概率分布之间的差异。在变分自编码器（VAEs）中，它作为核心正则化项，对潜在空间施加结构约束，从而使模型具备生成能力。本文详细推导了高斯分布间KL散度的闭式表达式，该表达式在实际VAE实现中尤为重要。从连续随机变量的一般定义出发，我们先推导单变量情形，再在协方差矩阵为对角矩阵的假设下扩展至多元情形。最后，我们讨论所得表达式中各项的含义及其对模型训练动态的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and implement the variational autoencoder objective, this paper derives the closed-form expression for the KL divergence between Gaussian distributions, a key component in VAE training. Starting from the general definition of KL divergence for continuous variables, it first derives the univariate case and then extends it to multivariate Gaussians under the diagonal covariance assumption. The resulting formula, composed of log-determinant and quadratic terms, is shown to directly influence latent space regularization and training dynamics, providing clarity on how the divergence penalizes deviations from the prior distribution.
为理解并实现变分自编码器（VAE）的目标函数，本文推导了高斯分布间KL散度的闭式表达式，这是VAE训练中的关键组成部分。从连续随机变量的一般定义出发，先求解单变量情形，再在对角协方差假设下扩展至多元情形。推导过程阐明了公式中每一项对潜在空间正则化的影响，表明KL散度通过惩罚与先验分布的偏离，引导编码器生成结构化、有意义的潜在表示，从而促进模型的生成能力。
Discourse Diversity in Multi-Turn Empathic Dialogue
Authors: Hongli Zhan, Emma S. Gueorguieva, Javier Hernandez, Jina Suh, Desmond C. Ong, Junyi Jessy Li
First: 2026-04-13T17:17:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:17:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) produce responses rated as highly empathic in single-turn settings (Ayers et al., 2023; Lee et al., 2024), yet they are also known to be formulaic generators that reuse the same lexical patterns, syntactic templates, and discourse structures across tasks (Jiang et al., 2025; Shaib et al., 2024; Namuduri et al., 2025). Less attention has been paid to whether this formulaicity extends to the level of discourse moves, i.e., what a response does for the person it is addressing. This question is especially consequential for empathic dialogue, where effective support demands not just a kind response at one moment but varied strategies as a conversation unfolds (Stiles et al., 1998). Indeed, prior work shows that LLMs reuse the same tactic sequences more than human supporters in single-turn settings (Gueorguieva et al., 2026). We extend this analysis to multi-turn conversations and find that the rigidity compounds: once a tactic appears in a supporter turn, LLMs reuse it in the next at nearly double the rate of humans (0.50-0.56 vs. 0.27). This pattern holds across LLMs serving as supporters in real emotional support conversations, and is invisible to standard similarity metrics. To address this gap, we introduce MINT (Multi-turn Inter-tactic Novelty Training), the first reinforcement learning framework to optimize discourse move diversity across multi-turn empathic dialogue. The best MINT variant combines an empathy quality reward with a cross-turn tactic novelty signal, improving aggregate empathy by 25.3% over vanilla across 1.7B and 4B models while reducing cross-turn discourse move repetition by 26.3% on the 4B model, surpassing all baselines including quality-only and token-level diversity methods on both measures. These results suggest that what current models lack is not empathy itself, but the ability to vary their discourse moves across a conversation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多轮共情对话中的话语多样性
大型语言模型（LLMs）在单轮对话中生成的回应常被评价为高度共情（Ayers等，2023；Lee等，2024），但它们也以公式化生成著称，会在不同任务中重复使用相同的词汇模式、句法模板和话语结构（Jiang等，2025；Shaib等，2024；Namuduri等，2025）。然而，较少研究关注这种公式化是否延伸至话语行为层面，即回应对对话对象所起的作用。这一问题在共情对话中尤为重要，因为有效的支持不仅需要即时的回应，更需要随着对话展开而变化的策略（Stiles等，1998）。此前研究表明，LLMs在单轮对话中比人类支持者更频繁地重复使用相同策略序列（Gueorguieva等，2026）。我们将此分析扩展至多轮对话，发现其僵化性加剧：一旦某策略出现在支持者回合中，LLMs在下一回合重复使用该策略的频率几乎是人类的两倍（0.50-0.56 vs. 0.27）。此模式在真实情感支持对话中由LLMs担任支持者时依然存在，且标准相似性度量无法捕捉。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了MINT（多轮跨策略新颖性训练），这是首个通过强化学习优化多轮共情对话中话语行为多样性的框架。最佳MINT变体结合共情质量奖励与跨轮策略新颖性信号，在1.7B和4B模型上相较基础模型整体共情能力提升25.3%，并在4B模型上将跨轮话语行为重复率降低26.3%，在两项指标上均超越所有基线方法（包括仅质量奖励和词级多样性方法）。这些结果表明，当前模型缺乏的并非共情本身，而是对话中话语行为的多样性能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models, while producing empathic single-turn responses, exhibit formulaic discourse patterns that limit their effectiveness in multi-turn empathic dialogue, this study introduces MINT, a reinforcement learning framework designed to enhance discourse move diversity across conversation turns. The method combines empathy quality rewards with a cross-turn tactic novelty signal to discourage repetitive discourse strategies. Experimental results show that MINT improves aggregate empathy by 25.3% over vanilla models and reduces cross-turn discourse repetition by 26.3% on the 4B model, outperforming all baselines including token-level diversity methods, indicating that LLMs lack not empathy but the capacity to vary their discourse moves dynamically.
鉴于大语言模型虽能在单轮对话中生成高分共情回应，但在多轮对话中常重复使用相同的语用策略，导致共情效果受限，本研究提出MINT框架，通过强化学习优化多轮对话中的语用策略多样性。该方法结合共情质量奖励与跨轮策略新颖性信号，有效抑制重复模式。实验表明，最优MINT变体在1.7B和4B模型上提升整体共情能力25.3%，在4B模型上降低跨轮语用重复26.3%，超越所有基线方法，说明当前模型缺乏的是对话中策略的多样性而非共情能力本身。
Collaborative Multi-Agent Scripts Generation for Enhancing Imperfect-Information Reasoning in Murder Mystery Games
Authors: Keyang Zhong, Junlin Xie, Hefeng Wu, Haofeng Li, Guanbin Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-13T17:16:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:16:23+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures, Findings of ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in perceptual tasks, yet they degrade in complex multi-hop reasoning under multiplayer game settings with imperfect and deceptive information. In this paper, we study a representative multiplayer task, Murder Mystery Games, which require inferring hidden truths based on partial clues provided by roles with different intentions. To address this challenge, we propose a collaborative multi-agent framework for evaluating and synthesizing high-quality, role-driven multiplayer game scripts, enabling fine-grained interaction patterns tailored to character identities (i.e., murderer vs. innocent). Our system generates rich multimodal contexts, including character backstories, visual and textual clues, and multi-hop reasoning chains, through coordinated agent interactions. We design a two-stage agent-monitored training strategy to enhance the reasoning ability of VLMs: (1) chain-of-thought based fine-tuning on curated and synthetic datasets that model uncertainty and deception; (2) GRPO-based reinforcement learning with agent-monitored reward shaping, encouraging the model to develop character-specific reasoning behaviors and effective multimodal multi-hop inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly boosts the performance of VLMs in narrative reasoning, hidden fact extraction, and deception-resilient understanding. Our contributions offer a scalable solution for training and evaluating VLMs under uncertain, adversarial, and socially complex conditions, laying the groundwork for future benchmarks in multimodal multi-hop reasoning under imperfect information.

标题：用于提升谋杀谜题游戏中不完全信息推理能力的协作多智能体脚本生成
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在感知任务中表现出色，但在多人游戏环境下，面对不完全和欺骗性信息时，其复杂多跳推理能力会下降。本文研究了代表性多人游戏任务——谋杀谜题游戏，该任务要求根据不同角色提供的部分线索推断隐藏真相。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种协作多智能体框架，用于评估和合成高质量、角色驱动的多人游戏脚本，支持针对角色身份（如凶手与无辜者）的细粒度交互模式。系统通过协调智能体交互生成丰富的多模态上下文，包括角色背景、视觉与文本线索及多跳推理链。我们设计了两阶段智能体监督训练策略以增强VLM推理能力：（1）基于思维链的微调，使用精心筛选与合成的数据集模拟不确定性和欺骗；（2）基于GRPO的强化学习，结合智能体监督奖励塑造，鼓励模型发展角色特异性推理行为和有效的多模态多跳推理。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著提升了VLM在叙事推理、隐藏事实提取及抗欺骗理解方面的性能。我们的贡献提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，用于在不确定、对抗性和社会复杂条件下训练和评估VLM，为未来不完全信息下多模态多跳推理基准奠定了基础。

The method employs a two-stage training strategy: first, chain-of-thought fine-tuning on curated and synthetic datasets modeling uncertainty and deception; second, GRPO-based reinforcement learning with agent-monitored reward shaping to encourage character-specific reasoning and multimodal inference.
