Learning Long-term Motion Embeddings for Efficient Kinematics Generation
Authors: Nick Stracke, Kolja Bauer, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Miguel Angel Bautista, Josh Susskind, Björn Ommer
First: 2026-04-13T17:14:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:14:47+00:00
Comments: for the project page and code, view https://compvis.github.io/long-term-motion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding and predicting motion is a fundamental component of visual intelligence. Although modern video models exhibit strong comprehension of scene dynamics, exploring multiple possible futures through full video synthesis remains prohibitively inefficient. We model scene dynamics orders of magnitude more efficiently by directly operating on a long-term motion embedding that is learned from large-scale trajectories obtained from tracker models. This enables efficient generation of long, realistic motions that fulfill goals specified via text prompts or spatial pokes. To achieve this, we first learn a highly compressed motion embedding with a temporal compression factor of 64x. In this space, we train a conditional flow-matching model to generate motion latents conditioned on task descriptions. The resulting motion distributions outperform those of both state-of-the-art video models and specialized task-specific approaches.

标题：学习长期运动嵌入以高效生成运动学
理解与预测运动是视觉智能的基本组成部分。尽管现代视频模型对场景动态具有较强的感知能力，但通过完整视频合成探索多种可能的未来仍效率低下。我们通过直接操作从跟踪模型获得的大规模轨迹中学习到的长期运动嵌入，将场景动态建模效率提升数个数量级。这使得能够高效生成满足文本提示或空间触碰指定目标的长时、真实运动。为此，我们首先学习一个压缩比为64倍的高密度运动嵌入。在此空间中，我们训练一个条件流匹配模型，根据任务描述生成运动潜变量。所得到的运动分布优于当前最先进的视频模型和专门的任务特定方法。
Summary / 总结

为解决通过完整视频合成生成长期运动效率低的问题，该论文提出直接学习一种压缩的长期运动嵌入，其时间压缩比达64倍，从跟踪模型生成的大规模轨迹中学习得到。在此空间中，训练一个条件流匹配模型，根据文本提示或空间输入生成运动潜变量。实验表明，所生成的运动分布显著优于当前最先进的视频模型和专门的运动生成方法，能高效生成符合目标的长期真实运动序列。
Multi-ORFT: Stable Online Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Multi-Agent Diffusion Planning in Cooperative Driving
Authors: Haojie Bai, Aimin Li, Ruoyu Yao, Xiongwei Zhao, Tingting Zhang, Xing Zhang, Lin Gao, and Jun Ma
First: 2026-04-13T17:13:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:13:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Closed-loop cooperative driving requires planners that generate realistic multimodal multi-agent trajectories while improving safety and traffic efficiency. Existing diffusion planners can model multimodal behaviors from demonstrations, but they often exhibit weak scene consistency and remain poorly aligned with closed-loop objectives; meanwhile, stable online post-training in reactive multi-agent environments remains difficult. We present Multi-ORFT, which couples scene-conditioned diffusion pre-training with stable online reinforcement post-training. In pre-training, the planner uses inter-agent self-attention, cross-attention, and AdaLN-Zero-based scene conditioning to improve scene consistency and road adherence of joint trajectories. In post-training, we formulate a two-level MDP that exposes step-wise reverse-kernel likelihoods for online optimization, and combine dense trajectory-level rewards with variance-gated group-relative policy optimization (VG-GRPO) to stabilize training. On the WOMD closed-loop benchmark, Multi-ORFT reduces collision rate from 2.04% to 1.89% and off-road rate from 1.68% to 1.36%, while increasing average speed from 8.36 to 8.61 m/s relative to the pre-trained planner, and it outperforms strong open-source baselines including SMART-large, SMART-tiny-CLSFT, and VBD on the primary safety and efficiency metrics. These results show that coupling scene-consistent denoising with stable online diffusion-policy optimization improves the reliability of closed-loop cooperative driving.

标题：Multi-ORFT：用于协同驾驶多智能体扩散规划的稳定在线强化微调
闭环协同驾驶需要生成真实多模态多智能体轨迹的规划器，同时提升安全性和交通效率。现有扩散规划器虽能从演示中建模多模态行为，但常存在场景一致性弱、与闭环目标对齐不足的问题；同时，在反应式多智能体环境中稳定在线后训练仍具挑战。本文提出Multi-ORFT，结合场景条件扩散预训练与稳定在线强化后训练。预训练阶段，规划器采用智能体间自注意力、交叉注意力及AdaLN-Zero场景条件化，提升联合轨迹的场景一致性与道路遵循性。后训练阶段，构建两级MDP以暴露逐步反核似然用于在线优化，并结合密集轨迹级奖励与方差门控组相对策略优化（VG-GRPO）稳定训练。在WOMD闭环基准测试中，Multi-ORFT将碰撞率从2.04%降至1.89%，离路率从1.68%降至1.36%，平均速度较预训练规划器提升至8.61 m/s，并在主要安全与效率指标上超越包括SMART-large、SMART-tiny-CLSFT和VBD在内的强开源基线。结果表明，将场景一致去噪与稳定在线扩散策略优化结合，可提升闭环协同驾驶的可靠性。

Motivated by the need for safe and efficient cooperative driving that generates realistic multimodal trajectories, Multi-ORFT combines scene-conditioned diffusion pre-training with stable online reinforcement post-training. The method uses inter-agent self-attention, cross-attention, and AdaLN-Zero for scene consistency during pre-training, then applies a two-level MDP with reverse-kernel likelihoods and variance-gated group-relative policy optimization (VG-GRPO) for online fine-tuning. Experimental results on the WOMD benchmark show a reduction in collision rate from 2.04% to 1.89% and off-road rate from 1.68% to 1.36%, while increasing average speed to 8.61 m/s, outperforming baselines like SMART-large and VBD in safety and efficiency.
为解决闭合环路协同驾驶中生成安全、高效且一致的多智能体轨迹的难题，Multi-ORFT结合了场景条件扩散预训练与稳定的在线强化学习后训练。预训练阶段利用智能体间自注意力、交叉注意力和AdaLN-Zero实现场景条件化，提升轨迹真实性和道路遵循性。后训练阶段采用两级MDP框架，结合反向核似然和方差门控组相对策略优化（VG-GRPO）以稳定在线适应。在WOMD基准测试中，Multi-ORFT降低了碰撞率和离路率，同时提升了平均速度，并在安全性和效率指标上优于多个开源基线模型。
MASH: Modeling Abstention via Selective Help-Seeking
Authors: Mustafa Omer Gul, Claire Cardie, Tanya Goyal
First: 2025-10-01T17:41:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:08:57+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, with 15 dedicated to citations and appendix. 17 tables and 11 figures. Preprint, under review. Paper updated to reflect new title and results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs cannot reliably recognize their parametric knowledge boundaries and often hallucinate answers to outside-of-boundary questions. In this paper, we introduce MASH (Modeling Abstention via Selective Help-seeking), a training framework that readily extracts abstentions from LLMs. Our key idea is that any external help-seeking by an LLM, i.e. search tool use, can serve as a proxy for abstention if the external help (search) is appropriately penalized while also rewarding answer accuracy. MASH operationalizes this idea using reinforcement learning with a pay-per-search reward. We run experiments on three knowledge-intensive QA datasets. Our results show that MASH substantially improves upon the selective help-seeking performance of prior efficient search approaches; on multi-hop datasets, it improves answer accuracy by 7.6%. Furthermore, MASH demonstrates strong off-the-shelf abstention performance, showcasing behavior competitive with prior abstention methods that additionally require predetermining model knowledge boundaries to construct training data. Overall, we show MASH training effectively aligns search tool use with parametric knowledge, which can be successfully leveraged for making abstention decisions and efficient search tool use
中文标题/摘要
标题：MASH：通过选择性求助建模回避
大语言模型无法可靠识别自身的参数知识边界，常对超出边界的问题产生幻觉回答。本文提出MASH（通过选择性求助建模回避），一种可直接从大语言模型中提取回避行为的训练框架。核心思想是：大语言模型的任何外部求助行为（如使用搜索工具），若适当惩罚外部求助（搜索）并同时奖励答案准确性，即可作为回避行为的代理。MASH通过基于“按搜索付费”奖励机制的强化学习实现该思想。我们在三个知识密集型问答数据集上进行实验，结果表明MASH显著优于先前高效搜索方法的选择性求助性能；在多跳数据集上，其答案准确率提升7.6%。此外，MASH展现出强大的开箱即用的回避性能，其行为表现可与需预先确定模型知识边界以构建训练数据的现有回避方法相媲美。总体而言，我们证明MASH训练能有效将搜索工具使用与参数知识对齐，从而成功用于回避决策和高效搜索工具使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of LLMs to hallucinate when exceeding their knowledge boundaries, this paper proposes MASH, a reinforcement learning framework that treats external help-seeking (e.g., search tool use) as a proxy for abstention, penalizing such behavior while rewarding accurate answers. Experiments on three knowledge-intensive QA datasets demonstrate that MASH significantly outperforms prior efficient search methods, improving answer accuracy by 7.6% on multi-hop tasks, and achieves competitive abstention performance without requiring predefined knowledge boundaries for training data.
为了解决大语言模型在超出知识边界时容易产生幻觉的问题，本文提出MASH框架，通过强化学习将外部求助（如搜索工具使用）视为“拒绝回答”的代理信号，在惩罚求助行为的同时奖励准确答案。在三个知识密集型问答数据集上的实验表明，MASH显著优于现有高效搜索方法，在多跳任务中准确率提升7.6%，且无需预先设定知识边界即可实现与现有拒绝回答方法相当的性能，有效将搜索行为与模型参数知识对齐，提升拒绝决策与搜索工具使用的效率。
Endogenous Information in Routing Games: Memory-Constrained Equilibria, Recall Braess Paradoxes, and Memory Design
Authors: Saad Alqithami
First: 2026-04-13T17:08:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:08:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study routing games in which travelers optimize over routes that are remembered or surfaced, rather than over a fixed exogenous action set. The paper develops a tractable design theory for endogenous recall and then connects it back to an explicit finite-memory micro model. At the micro level, each traveler carries a finite memory state, receives surfaced alternatives, chooses via a logit rule, and updates memory under a policy such as LRU. This yields a stationary Forgetful Wardrop Equilibrium (FWE); existence is proved under mild regularity, and uniqueness follows in a contraction regime for the reduced fixed-point map. The paper's main design layer is a stationary salience model that summarizes persistent memory and interface effects as route-specific weights. Salience-weighted stochastic user equilibrium is the unique minimizer of a strictly convex potential, which yields a clean optimization and implementability theory. In this layer we characterize governed implementability under ratio budgets and affine tying constraints, and derive constructive algorithms on parallel and series-parallel networks. The bridge between layers is exact for last-choice memory (B=1): the micro model is then equivalent to the salience model, so any interior salience vector can be realized by an appropriate surfacing policy. For larger memories, we develop an explicit LRU-to-TTL-to-salience approximation pipeline and add contraction-based bounds that translate surrogate-map error into fixed-point and welfare error. Finally, we define a Recall Braess Paradox, in which improving recall increases equilibrium delay without changing physical capacity, and show that it can arise on every two-terminal network with at least two distinct s-t paths. Targeted experiments support the approximation regime, governed-design predictions, and the computational advantages of the reduced layer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：路由博弈中的内生信息：记忆受限均衡、回忆布雷斯悖论与记忆设计
我们研究旅行者在可回忆或可呈现的路线上进行优化的路由博弈，而非在固定外生行动集中进行优化。本文发展了一种可处理的内生回忆设计理论，并将其与一个显式的有限记忆微观模型相联系。在微观层面，每位旅行者携带有限记忆状态，接收呈现的替代路线，通过Logit规则选择，并按如LRU等策略更新记忆。这产生一个稳态遗忘沃德罗普均衡（FWE）；在温和正则条件下其存在性得证，且在收缩映射条件下其唯一性成立。本文的主要设计层是一个稳态显著性模型，将持久记忆与界面效应概括为路线特定权重。显著性加权随机用户均衡是严格凸势函数的唯一最小化者，从而获得清晰的优化与可实施性理论。在该层我们刻画了在比例预算与仿射绑定约束下的受控可实施性，并推导出在并联与串并联网络上的构造性算法。两层之间的桥梁在最后选择记忆（B=1）时精确成立：微观模型等价于显著性模型，因此任何内部显著性向量均可通过适当呈现策略实现。对于更大记忆容量，我们开发了从LRU到TTL再到显著性的显式近似管道，并加入基于收缩的误差界限，将代理映射误差转化为不动点与福利误差。最后，我们定义了回忆布雷斯悖论——即提升回忆会增加均衡延迟，而物理容量不变，并证明其可在任意具有至少两条不同s-t路径的两端网络中出现。针对性实验支持近似框架、受控设计预测及简化层的计算优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model travelers who adaptively recall and update route choices based on memory constraints, this paper develops a framework for endogenous recall in routing games. It introduces a micro-level model where agents maintain finite memory states, receive surfaced route alternatives, choose via logit rules, and update memory using policies like LRU, leading to a stationary Forgetful Wardrop Equilibrium. The paper then abstracts this into a salience-weighted stochastic user equilibrium model, which is shown to be the unique minimizer of a strictly convex potential, enabling tractable design and implementation. Experimental results confirm that the salience model accurately approximates micro behavior under LRU memory policies, supports governed design under budget and tying constraints, and reveals a Recall Braess Paradox—where enhanced recall increases equilibrium delay without altering physical capacity—on any two-terminal network with multiple paths.
为建模受记忆容量限制的旅行者如何动态回忆和更新路径，本文构建了路由博弈中的内生回忆框架。在微观层面，个体携带有限记忆状态，依据Logit规则选择路径，并按LRU等策略更新记忆，从而达到静止的遗忘沃德罗普均衡。论文进一步抽象为“显著性加权随机用户均衡”模型，该模型是严格凸势函数的唯一最小化点，便于设计与实施。实验表明，显著性模型能准确近似LRU记忆下的行为，支持在预算与绑定约束下的治理设计，并揭示“回忆式布雷萨悖论”——即增强回忆反而增加均衡延误，而网络物理容量未变——这一现象在任意具有多条路径的两点间网络中均可出现。
A Metamorphic Testing Perspective on Knowledge Distillation for Language Models of Code: Does the Student Deeply Mimic the Teacher?
Authors: Md. Abdul Awal, Mrigank Rochan, Chanchal K. Roy
First: 2025-11-07T18:38:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:06:19+00:00
Comments: This paper is a revised version of a manuscript currently under revision at the Journal of Systems and Software
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based language models of code have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of software analytics tasks, but their practical deployment remains limited due to high computational costs, slow inference speeds, and significant environmental impact. To address these challenges, recent research has increasingly explored knowledge distillation as a method for compressing a large language model of code (the teacher) into a smaller model (the student) while maintaining performance. However, the degree to which a student model deeply mimics the predictive behavior and internal representations of its teacher remains largely unexplored, as current accuracy-based evaluation provides only a surface-level view of model quality and often fails to capture more profound discrepancies in behavioral fidelity between the teacher and student models. To address this gap, we empirically show that the student model often fails to deeply mimic the teacher model, resulting in up to 285% greater performance drop under adversarial attacks, which is not captured by traditional accuracy-based evaluation. Therefore, we propose MetaCompress, a metamorphic testing framework that systematically evaluates behavioral fidelity by comparing the outputs of teacher and student models under a set of behavior-preserving metamorphic relations. We evaluate MetaCompress on two widely studied tasks, using compressed versions of popular language models of code, obtained via three different knowledge distillation techniques: Compressor, AVATAR, and MORPH. The results show that MetaCompress identifies up to 62% behavioral discrepancies in student models, underscoring the need for behavioral fidelity evaluation within the knowledge distillation pipeline and establishing MetaCompress as a practical framework for testing compressed language models of code derived through knowledge distillation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从形变测试视角看代码语言模型的知识蒸馏：学生模型是否深度模仿教师模型？
基于Transformer的代码语言模型在多种软件分析任务中取得了最先进性能，但因计算成本高、推理速度慢及环境影响大，其实际部署受限。为应对这些挑战，近期研究越来越多地探索知识蒸馏方法，将大型代码语言模型（教师）压缩为小型模型（学生）并保持性能。然而，学生模型在多大程度上深度模仿教师模型的预测行为和内部表征仍鲜有研究，因当前基于准确率的评估仅提供模型质量的表层视角，常无法捕捉教师与学生模型间更深层次的行为差异。为填补这一空白，我们实证表明，学生模型往往未能深度模仿教师模型，导致在对抗攻击下性能下降高达285%，而传统准确率评估无法捕捉此现象。因此，我们提出MetaCompress，一种形变测试框架，通过比较教师与学生模型在一组行为保持形变关系下的输出，系统评估行为保真度。我们在两个广泛研究的任务上评估MetaCompress，使用三种不同知识蒸馏技术（Compressor、AVATAR、MORPH）压缩流行代码语言模型所得版本。结果表明，MetaCompress可识别学生模型高达62%的行为差异，凸显知识蒸馏流程中行为保真度评估的必要性，并确立MetaCompress作为测试知识蒸馏所得压缩代码语言模型的实用框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether compressed language models of code truly mimic their larger teacher models, this study introduces MetaCompress, a metamorphic testing framework that assesses behavioral fidelity by comparing teacher and student outputs under behavior-preserving transformations. The authors evaluate student models distilled via Compressor, AVATAR, and MORPH on two code-related tasks and find that traditional accuracy metrics fail to capture critical discrepancies: student models often exhibit up to 285% greater performance degradation under adversarial attacks compared to teachers, with MetaCompress detecting up to 62% of such behavioral mismatches.
为评估压缩后的代码语言模型是否真正模仿其教师模型，本文提出MetaCompress框架，通过在行为保持的变换下对比教师与学生模型输出来评估行为一致性。在两个代码相关任务上，针对Compressor、AVATAR和MORPH三种蒸馏方法生成的学生模型进行测试，发现传统准确率指标无法捕捉关键行为差异——学生模型在对抗攻击下的性能下降可达教师模型的285%。MetaCompress成功识别出高达62%的行为差异，证明其在揭示蒸馏模型行为一致性问题上的有效性。
Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition in Videos for Personalized Digital Health Interventions
Authors: Manuela González-González, Soufiane Belharbi, Muhammad Osama Zeeshan, Masoumeh Sharafi, Muhammad Haseeb Aslam, Lorenzo Sia, Nicolas Richet, Marco Pedersoli, Alessandro Lameiras Koerich, Simon L Bacon, Eric Granger
First: 2026-04-13T17:05:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:05:38+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2505.19328
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using behavioural science, health interventions focus on behaviour change by providing a framework to help patients acquire and maintain healthy habits that improve medical outcomes. In-person interventions are costly and difficult to scale, especially in resource-limited regions. Digital health interventions offer a cost-effective approach, potentially supporting independent living and self-management. Automating such interventions, especially through machine learning, has gained considerable attention recently. Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) play a primary role for individuals to delay, avoid, or abandon health interventions. A/H are subtle and conflicting emotions that place a person in a state between positive and negative evaluations of a behaviour, or between acceptance and refusal to engage in it. They manifest as affective inconsistency across modalities or within a modality, such as language, facial, vocal expressions, and body language. While experts can be trained to recognize A/H, integrating them into digital health interventions is costly and less effective. Automatic A/H recognition is therefore critical for the personalization and cost-effectiveness of digital health interventions. Here, we explore the application of deep learning models for A/H recognition in videos, a multi-modal task by nature. In particular, this paper covers three learning setups: supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation for personalization, and zero-shot inference via large language models (LLMs). Our experiments are conducted on the unique and recently published BAH video dataset for A/H recognition. Our results show limited performance, suggesting that more adapted multi-modal models are required for accurate A/H recognition. Better methods for modeling spatio-temporal and multimodal fusion are necessary to leverage conflicts within/across modalities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视频中矛盾与犹豫情绪识别用于个性化数字健康干预
行为科学指导下的健康干预旨在通过提供框架帮助患者养成并维持健康习惯，从而改善医疗结果。面对面干预成本高且难以规模化，尤其在资源匮乏地区。数字健康干预提供了一种成本效益高的方法，可能支持独立生活和自我管理。通过机器学习自动化此类干预近年来备受关注。矛盾与犹豫（A/H）情绪是导致个人推迟、回避或放弃健康干预的主要因素。A/H是微妙且矛盾的情绪，使个体处于对行为的积极与消极评价之间，或处于接受与拒绝参与之间的状态。它们表现为跨模态或单模态（如语言、面部、语音表达和肢体语言）中的情感不一致。尽管专家可被训练识别A/H，但将其整合进数字健康干预成本高且效果不佳。因此，自动识别A/H对数字健康干预的个性化和成本效益至关重要。本文探讨深度学习模型在视频中A/H识别的应用，这是一个天然的多模态任务。具体而言，本文涵盖三种学习设置：监督学习、用于个性化无监督领域自适应，以及通过大型语言模型（LLM）实现零样本推理。实验基于新近发布的BAH视频数据集进行。结果表明性能有限，提示需更适配的多模态模型以准确识别A/H。还需更好的时空建模与多模态融合方法，以利用模态内/模态间冲突。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for automated recognition of ambivalence and hesitancy in video data to improve personalized digital health interventions, which are critical for behavior change but currently lack scalable, cost-effective solutions. The authors propose applying deep learning models to multi-modal video data—combining facial, vocal, and body language cues—to detect subtle emotional conflicts that indicate hesitation. They evaluate three approaches: supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation for personalization, and zero-shot inference using large language models, all tested on the BAH video dataset. Results show limited performance across all methods, indicating that current models fail to adequately capture spatio-temporal dynamics and multimodal conflicts, and that more sophisticated fusion techniques are needed for accurate A/H recognition.
该研究旨在通过视频中的多模态数据自动识别矛盾与犹豫情绪（A/H），以提升个性化数字健康干预的效果，因为此类情绪常导致患者推迟或放弃健康行为，而人工识别成本高昂。作者提出使用深度学习模型，结合面部、语音和肢体语言等模态，评估三种方法：监督学习、无监督领域自适应用于个性化，以及基于大语言模型的零样本推理。在BAH视频数据集上的实验显示，所有方法表现有限，表明当前模型难以有效建模时空动态和模态间冲突，亟需更先进的多模态融合架构。
Universality of first-order methods on random and deterministic matrices
Authors: Nicola Gorini, Chris Jones, Dmitriy Kunisky, Lucas Pesenti
First: 2026-04-13T17:03:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:03:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
General first-order methods (GFOM) are a flexible class of iterative algorithms which update a state vector by matrix-vector multiplications and entrywise nonlinearities. A long line of work has sought to understand the large-n dynamics of GFOM, mostly focusing on "very random" input matrices and the approximate message passing (AMP) special case of GFOM whose state is asymptotically Gaussian. Yet, it has long remained unknown how to construct iterative algorithms that retain this Gaussianity for more structured inputs, or why existing AMP algorithms can be as effective for some deterministic matrices as they are for random matrices. We analyze diagrammatic expansions of GFOM via the limiting traffic distribution of the input matrix, the collection of all limiting values of permutation-invariant polynomials in the matrix entries, to obtain the following results: 1. We calculate the traffic distribution for the first non-trivial deterministic matrices, including (minor variants of) the Walsh-Hadamard and discrete sine and cosine transform matrices. This determines the limiting dynamics of GFOM on these inputs, resolving parts of longstanding conjectures of Marinari, Parisi, and Ritort (1994). 2. We design a new AMP iteration which unifies several previous AMP variants and generalizes to new input types, whose limiting dynamics are Gaussian conditional on some latent random variables. The asymptotic dynamics hold for a large and natural class of traffic distributions (encompassing both random and deterministic input matrices) and the algorithm's analysis gives a simple combinatorial interpretation of the Onsager correction, answering questions posed recently by Wang, Zhong, and Fan (2022).
中文标题/摘要
标题：一阶方法在随机与确定性矩阵上的普适性
广义一阶方法（GFOM）是一类灵活的迭代算法，通过矩阵-向量乘法和逐元素非线性操作更新状态向量。长期以来，研究者致力于理解GFOM在大n规模下的动态行为，主要聚焦于“非常随机”的输入矩阵及GFOM的近似消息传递（AMP）特例，其状态在渐近下呈高斯分布。然而，长期以来人们尚不清楚如何构造迭代算法，使其在更结构化的输入下仍保持高斯性，或为何现有AMP算法对某些确定性矩阵的有效性可与随机矩阵相当。 我们通过输入矩阵的极限交通分布（即所有在矩阵元素上保持置换不变的多项式的极限值集合）分析GFOM的图示展开，得出以下结果： 1. 我们计算了若干首个非平凡确定性矩阵（包括沃尔什-哈达玛及离散正弦和余弦变换矩阵的若干变体）的交通分布，从而确定了GFOM在这些输入上的极限动态，部分解决了Marinari、Parisi和Ritort（1994）提出的长期猜想。 2. 我们设计了一种新的AMP迭代，统一了若干先前AMP变体并扩展至新输入类型，其极限动态在某些潜在随机变量条件下呈高斯分布。该算法的渐近动态适用于一大类自然的交通分布（涵盖随机与确定性输入矩阵），其分析给出了昂萨格修正的简单组合解释，回应了Wang、Zhong和Fan（2022）近期提出的问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how first-order methods behave on structured deterministic matrices rather than just random ones, this work develops a diagrammatic analysis of general first-order methods using the limiting traffic distribution of input matrices. The authors compute traffic distributions for key deterministic matrices like Walsh-Hadamard and discrete sine/cosine transforms, revealing their asymptotic dynamics and resolving long-standing conjectures. They also introduce a new AMP algorithm that unifies prior variants and maintains Gaussian limiting behavior under latent randomness, applicable to a broad class of traffic distributions including both random and deterministic inputs, and provide a combinatorial interpretation of the Onsager correction.
为理解通用一阶迭代算法在结构化确定性矩阵上的行为——这类矩阵常不满足随机矩阵理论中的高斯性假设——本文通过输入矩阵的极限交通分布，采用图示展开方法刻画一阶方法的渐近动力学。作者计算了包括沃尔什-哈达玛及离散正弦/余弦变换矩阵等关键确定性矩阵的交通分布，部分解决了Marinari等人1994年的长期猜想。同时，他们提出一种统一的AMP算法，其极限动力学在某些潜变量下保持高斯性，适用于包含随机与确定性矩阵的广泛交通分布类，并给出了昂萨格修正的组合解释。
Multi-Modal Learning meets Genetic Programming: Analyzing Alignment in Latent Space Optimization
Authors: Benjamin Léger, Kazem Meidani, Christian Gagné
First: 2026-04-09T14:55:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T17:02:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover mathematical expressions from data, a task traditionally tackled using Genetic Programming (GP) through combinatorial search over symbolic structures. Latent Space Optimization (LSO) methods use neural encoders to map symbolic expressions into continuous spaces, transforming the combinatorial search into continuous optimization. SNIP (Meidani et al., 2024), a contrastive pre-training model inspired by CLIP, advances LSO by introducing a multi-modal approach: aligning symbolic and numeric encoders in a shared latent space to learn the phenotype-genotype mapping, enabling optimization in the numeric space to implicitly guide symbolic search. However, this relies on fine-grained cross-modal alignment, whereas literature on similar models like CLIP reveals that such an alignment is typically coarse-grained. In this paper, we investigate whether SNIP delivers on its promise of effective bi-modal optimization for SR. Our experiments show that: (1) cross-modal alignment does not improve during optimization, even as fitness increases, and (2) the alignment learned by SNIP is too coarse to efficiently conduct principled search in the symbolic space. These findings reveal that while multi-modal LSO holds significant potential for SR, effective alignment-guided optimization remains unrealized in practice, highlighting fine-grained alignment as a critical direction for future work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态学习与遗传编程的结合：潜空间优化中的对齐分析
符号回归（SR）旨在从数据中发现数学表达式，传统上通过遗传编程（GP）在符号结构上进行组合搜索来解决。潜空间优化（LSO）方法使用神经编码器将符号表达式映射到连续空间，将组合搜索转化为连续优化。SNIP（Meidani 等，2024）是一种受 CLIP 启发的对比预训练模型，通过引入多模态方法推进 LSO：在共享的潜空间中对齐符号与数值编码器，学习表型-基因型映射，从而在数值空间中优化以隐式引导符号搜索。然而，这依赖于细粒度的跨模态对齐，而类似 CLIP 的文献表明此类对齐通常是粗粒度的。本文研究 SNIP 是否实现了其在 SR 中有效双模优化的承诺。实验表明：（1）即使适应度提升，跨模态对齐在优化过程中并未改善；（2）SNIP 学习到的对齐过于粗粒度，无法高效在符号空间中进行原则性搜索。这些发现表明，尽管多模态 LSO 对 SR 具有巨大潜力，但基于对齐的优化在实践中尚未实现，凸显细粒度对齐是未来工作的关键方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of multi-modal learning to enhance symbolic regression via latent space optimization, this paper examines whether SNIP—a contrastive pre-training model that aligns symbolic and numeric encoders—can effectively guide symbolic search through fine-grained cross-modal alignment. The study evaluates SNIP’s performance in a latent space setting, where symbolic expressions are encoded and optimized numerically, and finds that despite increasing fitness, cross-modal alignment does not improve during optimization. Furthermore, the alignment achieved is too coarse to support principled symbolic search, indicating that while multi-modal LSO offers promise, current alignment mechanisms fail to deliver practical optimization benefits.
本文旨在探究多模态学习是否能有效提升符号回归中的潜在空间优化效果，重点评估SNIP模型中符号与数值编码器跨模态对齐策略的实际效能。实验方法通过对比优化过程中对齐质量与适应度提升情况，发现尽管适应度持续上升，跨模态对齐并未随之改善，且对齐精度粗糙，无法支持符号空间中的有效搜索。结果表明，尽管多模态潜在空间优化具有潜力，当前对齐机制尚未实现有效的引导优化，精细对齐仍是未来关键研究方向。
Exploring the Grassroots Understanding and Practices of Collective Memory Co-Contribution in a University Community
Authors: Zeyu Huang, Xinyi Cao, Yue Deng, Junze Li, Kangyu Yuan, Xiaojuan Ma
Venue: Proc. ACM Hum.-Comput. Interact. 10, 1, Article GROUP009 (March 2026), 30 pages
First: 2025-12-09T16:39:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:59:35+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 6 figures, to be published in the journal Proceedings of the ACM on Human-Computer Interaction (PACMHCI 2026, Volume 10, Issue 1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collective memory -- community members' interconnected memories and impressions of the group -- is essential to the community's culture and identity. Its development requires members' continuous participatory contribution and sensemaking. However, existing works mainly adopt a holistic sociological perspective to analyze well-developed collective memory, less focusing on member-level conceptualization of this possession or what the co-contribution practices can be. Therefore, this work alternatively adopts the latter perspective and probes such interpretative and interactional patterns with two mobile systems. With one being a locative narrative and exploration system condensed from existing literature's design frameworks, and the other being a conventional online forum representing current practices, they served as the anchors of observation for our two-week, mixed-methods field study (n=38) on a university campus. A core debate we have identified was to retrospectively contemplate or document the presence as a history for the future. This also subsequently impacted the narrative focuses, expectations of collective memory constituents, and the ways participants seek inspiration from the group. We further extracted design considerations that could better embrace the diverse conceptualizations of collective memory and bond different community members together. Lastly, revisiting and reflecting on our design, we provided extra insights on designing devoted locative narrative experiences for community-driven UGC platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索大学社区中集体记忆共构的基层理解与实践
集体记忆——社区成员对群体的相互关联的记忆与印象——是社区文化和身份的核心。其发展需要成员持续的参与性贡献与意义建构。然而，现有研究主要采用整体社会学视角分析成熟的集体记忆，较少关注成员层面对此类记忆的个体概念化或共构实践的具体形式。因此，本研究转而采用后者视角，通过两个移动系统探究此类解释与互动模式：其一是基于现有文献设计框架浓缩的定位叙事与探索系统；其二是代表当前实践的常规在线论坛。这两个系统作为我们为期两周、混合方法田野研究（n=38）的观察锚点，研究地点为大学校园。我们识别出的核心争议在于：回顾性地反思或记录“存在”作为历史，以服务于未来。这进一步影响了叙事焦点、集体记忆构成要素的预期，以及参与者从群体中寻求灵感的方式。我们进一步提炼了能更好包容集体记忆多样概念化并凝聚不同社区成员的设计考量。最后，通过回顾与反思我们的设计，我们为面向社区驱动的用户生成内容平台设计专注的定位叙事体验提供了额外见解。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how university community members conceptually understand and practically contribute to collective memory, addressing a gap in existing research that primarily focuses on macro-level sociological analysis rather than individual-level interpretation and interaction. Using two mobile systems — a locative narrative system based on prior design frameworks and a conventional online forum — the researchers conducted a two-week mixed-methods field study with 38 participants. The study revealed that participants often retrospectively framed their presence as historical documentation for future reference, which influenced their narrative priorities, expectations of collective memory, and how they drew inspiration from the group. The findings also led to design recommendations for fostering inclusive collective memory experiences and enhancing community cohesion through user-generated content platforms.
本研究探讨大学社区成员如何个体化理解并参与集体记忆的共建，弥补现有研究多从整体社会学视角分析成熟集体记忆的不足。通过两个移动系统——一个基于既有设计框架的定位叙事工具和一个常规在线论坛——研究人员对38名参与者进行了为期两周的混合方法田野实验。研究发现，参与者常通过回顾性反思将过去在场记录为未来意义的历史遗产，从而影响其叙事焦点、集体记忆构成的期待以及从群体中汲取灵感的方式。研究进一步提炼出支持多元集体记忆认知与促进成员联结的设计原则，并为面向用户生成内容平台的定位叙事体验设计提供启示。
The Devil is in the Details -- From OCR for Old Church Slavonic to Purely Visual Stemma Reconstruction
Authors: Armin Hoenen
First: 2026-04-13T16:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:58:57+00:00
Comments: International conference at Valamo monastery, Finnland, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The age of artificial intelligence has brought many new possibilities and pitfalls in many fields and tasks. The devil is in the details, and those come to the fore when building new pipelines and executing small practical experiments. OCR and stemmatology are no exception. The current investigation starts comparing a range of OCR-systems, from classical over machine learning to LLMs, for roughly 6,000 characters of late handwritten church slavonic manuscripts from the 18th century. Focussing on basic letter correctness, more than 10 CS OCR-systems among which 2 LLMs (GPT5 and Gemini3-flash) are being compared. Then, post-processing via LLMs is assessed and finally, different agentic OCR architectures (specialized post-processing agents, an agentic pipeline and RAG) are tested. With new technology elaborated, experiments suggest, church slavonic CER for basic letters may reach as low as 2-3% but elaborated diacritics could still present a problem. How well OCR can prime stemmatology as a downstream task is the entry point to the second part of the article which introduces a new stemmatic method based solely on image processing. Here, a pipeline of automated visual glyph extraction, clustering and pairwise statistical comparison leading to a distance matrix and ultimately a stemma, is being presented and applied to two small corpora, one for the church slavonic Gospel of Mark from the 14th to 16th centuries, one for the Roman de la Rose in French from the 14th and 15th centuries. Basic functioning of the method can be demonstrated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：魔鬼在细节中——从古教会斯拉夫语OCR到纯视觉文本关系重建
人工智能时代为诸多领域和任务带来了新机遇与新陷阱。细节决定成败，尤其在构建新流程和执行小型实践实验时尤为明显。OCR与文本关系学也不例外。本研究首先比较了多种OCR系统（从经典方法到机器学习再到大语言模型），针对约6000个字符的18世纪晚期手写教会斯拉夫语文本。聚焦基本字母准确性，比较了10多种CS OCR系统（其中包含2个大语言模型：GPT5和Gemini3-flash）。随后评估了大语言模型的后处理效果，并测试了不同代理式OCR架构（专用后处理代理、代理式流程和RAG）。实验表明，教会斯拉夫语基本字母的字符错误率（CER）可低至2-3%，但复杂变音符号仍存挑战。OCR如何为下游文本关系学任务提供支持，是本文第二部分的切入点，该部分介绍了一种仅基于图像处理的全新文本关系方法。该方法通过自动化视觉字符提取、聚类及成对统计比较，生成距离矩阵并最终推导出文本关系图，应用于两个小型语料库：其一是14至16世纪的教会斯拉夫语《马可福音》，其二是14至15世纪的法语《玫瑰传奇》。该方法的基本功能已可验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve OCR accuracy for historical Church Slavonic manuscripts and to explore purely visual stemma reconstruction without textual reliance, the study compares over ten OCR systems—including two LLMs (GPT5 and Gemini3-flash)—on 6,000 characters from 18th-century handwritten texts, achieving letter-level error rates as low as 2-3% for basic characters but struggling with diacritics. It then evaluates LLM-based post-processing and agentic architectures, before introducing a novel visual stemma method that extracts glyphs, clusters them, and computes pairwise distances to generate a stemma, successfully applied to two corpora: Church Slavonic Gospel of Mark and French Roman de la Rose, demonstrating basic functionality without OCR dependency.
为提升18世纪手写古教会斯拉夫语文本的OCR准确性，研究对比了十余种OCR系统（含两款大语言模型GPT5与Gemini3-flash），在6000字符样本上发现基础字母错误率低至2-3%，但变音符号仍存挑战；随后评估了大模型后处理及代理架构（专用代理、流水线、RAG）以优化结果。第二部分提出纯视觉谱系重建方法，通过自动字符提取、聚类与统计比较生成距离矩阵并推导谱系，成功应用于两组文本：14至16世纪教会斯拉夫语《马可福音》及14至15世纪法语《玫瑰传奇》，初步验证了方法可行性。
VFEFL: Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning against Malicious Clients via Verifiable Functional Encryption
Authors: Nina Cai, Jinguang Han, Weizhi Meng
First: 2025-06-15T13:38:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:57:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning is a promising distributed learning paradigm that enables collaborative model training without exposing local client data, thereby protecting data privacy. However, it also brings new threats and challenges. The advancement of model inversion attacks has rendered the plaintext transmission of local models insecure, while the distributed nature of federated learning makes it particularly vulnerable to attacks raised by malicious clients. To protect data privacy and prevent malicious client attacks, this paper proposes a privacy-preserving Federated Learning framework based on Verifiable Functional Encryption (VFEFL), without a non-colluding dual-server assumption or additional trusted third-party. Specifically, we propose a novel Cross-Ciphertext Decentralized Verifiable Functional Encryption (CC-DVFE) scheme that enables the verification of specific relationships over multi-dimensional ciphertexts. This scheme is formally treated, in terms of definition, security model and security proof. Furthermore, based on the proposed CC-DVFE scheme, we design a privacy-preserving federated learning framework that incorporates a novel robust aggregation rule to detect malicious clients, enabling the effective training of high-accuracy models under adversarial settings. Finally, we provide the formal analysis and empirical evaluation of VFEFL. The results demonstrate that our approach achieves the desired privacy protection, robustness, verifiability and fidelity, while eliminating the reliance on non-colluding dual-server assumption or trusted third parties required by most existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VFEFL：基于可验证功能加密的隐私保护联邦学习框架，抵御恶意客户端攻击
联邦学习是一种有前景的分布式学习范式，能够在不暴露本地客户端数据的前提下实现协作模型训练，从而保护数据隐私。然而，它也带来了新的威胁和挑战。随着模型反演攻击技术的发展，本地模型的明文传输变得不安全，而联邦学习的分布式特性使其特别容易受到恶意客户端发起的攻击。为保护数据隐私并防范恶意客户端攻击，本文提出了一种基于可验证功能加密（VFEFL）的隐私保护联邦学习框架，无需非共谋双服务器假设或额外可信第三方。具体而言，我们提出了一种新颖的跨密文去中心化可验证功能加密（CC-DVFE）方案，支持对多维密文上的特定关系进行验证。该方案在定义、安全模型和安全证明方面进行了形式化处理。此外，基于所提出的CC-DVFE方案，我们设计了一种隐私保护联邦学习框架，结合了一种新颖的鲁棒聚合规则以检测恶意客户端，从而在对抗性环境下有效训练高精度模型。最后，我们对VFEFL进行了形式化分析和实证评估。结果表明，我们的方法实现了预期的隐私保护、鲁棒性、可验证性和保真度，同时消除了大多数现有方法所依赖的非共谋双服务器假设或可信第三方。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of federated learning to model inversion attacks and malicious clients, this paper introduces VFEFL, a privacy-preserving federated learning framework that eliminates reliance on non-colluding dual servers or trusted third parties. It employs a novel Cross-Ciphertext Decentralized Verifiable Functional Encryption (CC-DVFE) scheme to verify functional relationships over multi-dimensional ciphertexts, enabling secure and verifiable aggregation. The framework further integrates a robust aggregation rule to detect and mitigate malicious client behavior, allowing high-accuracy model training even under adversarial conditions. Experimental results confirm that VFEFL achieves strong privacy, robustness, verifiability, and model fidelity without requiring additional trusted infrastructure.
为应对联邦学习中模型反演攻击和恶意客户端的威胁，本文提出VFEFL框架，无需非合谋双服务器或可信第三方即可实现隐私保护。该框架基于创新的跨密文去中心化可验证功能加密（CC-DVFE）方案，支持对多维密文间关系的验证，从而实现安全且可验证的模型聚合。实验表明，VFEFL在对抗环境下能有效保护隐私、精准识别恶意客户端，并保持高模型精度，同时不牺牲计算效率或数据保真度。
Evaluating Cooperation in LLM Social Groups through Elected Leadership
Authors: Ryan Faulkner, Anushka Deshpande, David Guzman Piedrahita, Joel Z. Leibo, Zhijing Jin
First: 2026-04-13T16:57:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:57:11+00:00
Comments: Main text: 11 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Governing common-pool resources requires agents to develop enduring strategies through cooperation and self-governance to avoid collective failure. While foundation models have shown potential for cooperation in these settings, existing multi-agent research provides little insight into whether structured leadership and election mechanisms can improve collective decision making. The lack of such a critical organizational feature ubiquitous in human society presents a significant shortcoming of the current methods. In this work we aim to directly address whether leadership and elections can support improved social welfare and cooperation through multi-agent simulation with LLMs. We present our open-source framework that simulates leadership through elected personas and candidate-driven agendas and carry out an empirical study of LLMs under controlled governance conditions. Our experiments demonstrate that having elected leadership improves social welfare scores by 55.4% and survival time by 128.6% across a range of high performing LLMs. Through the construction of an agent social graph we compute centrality metrics to assess the social influence of leader personas and also analyze rhetorical and cooperative tendencies revealed through a sentiment analysis on leader utterances. This work lays the foundation for further study of election mechanisms in multi-agent systems toward navigating complex social dilemmas.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过选举领导力评估大语言模型社交群体中的合作
管理公共资源需要代理通过合作与自我治理制定持久策略，以避免集体失败。尽管基础模型在这些场景中显示出合作潜力，但现有多智能体研究对结构化领导力和选举机制是否能提升集体决策效果缺乏洞察。当前方法缺乏人类社会普遍存在的关键组织特征，这是其重大缺陷。本研究旨在通过大语言模型的多智能体模拟，直接探讨领导力和选举是否能提升社会福利与合作。我们提出开源框架，通过选举角色和候选人驱动议程模拟领导力，并在受控治理条件下开展LLM实证研究。实验表明，在多种高性能LLM中，选举领导力使社会福利得分提升55.4%，生存时间延长128.6%。通过构建智能体社交图谱，我们计算中心性指标评估领导角色的社会影响力，并通过情感分析领导发言，分析其修辞与合作倾向。本工作为多智能体系统中选举机制研究奠定基础，以应对复杂社会困境。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether elected leadership can enhance cooperation and social welfare in multi-agent systems using large language models, motivated by the absence of structured governance in current AI social simulations. The researchers developed an open-source framework that simulates leadership through elected personas and candidate-driven agendas, enabling controlled experiments under varying governance conditions. Experimental results show that elected leadership improves social welfare by 55.4% and extends group survival time by 128.6% across multiple high-performing LLMs. Additionally, social influence of leaders was quantified via centrality metrics in agent social graphs, and sentiment analysis of leader utterances revealed patterns in rhetorical strategies and cooperative tendencies.
本研究旨在探究选举领导机制能否提升大语言模型在多智能体系统中的合作水平与社会福利，动机源于当前AI社会群体缺乏人类社会普遍存在的组织结构。作者构建了一个开源仿真框架，让智能体选举领导者并遵循候选人议程，从而在可控条件下评估集体决策效果。实验表明，选举领导可使社会福利提升55.4%，群体存活时间延长128.6%。同时，通过构建智能体社交图谱并分析领导者发言的情感倾向，揭示了领导者的社会影响力与合作倾向，为未来研究选举机制在复杂社会困境中的应用奠定基础。
On the Robustness of Watermarking for Autoregressive Image Generation
Authors: Andreas Müller, Denis Lukovnikov, Shingo Kodama, Minh Pham, Anubhav Jain, Jonathan Petit, Niv Cohen, Asja Fischer
First: 2026-04-13T16:56:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:56:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The proliferation of autoregressive (AR) image generators demands reliable detection and attribution of their outputs to mitigate misinformation, and to filter synthetic images from training data to prevent model collapse. To address this need, watermarking techniques, specifically designed for AR models, embed a subtle signal at generation time, enabling downstream verification through a corresponding watermark detector. In this work, we study these schemes and demonstrate their vulnerability to both watermark removal and forgery attacks. We assess existing attacks and further introduce three new attacks: (i) a vector-quantized regeneration removal attack, (ii) adversarial optimization-based attack, and (iii) a frequency injection attack. Our evaluation reveals that removal and forgery attacks can be effective with access to a single watermarked reference image and without access to original model parameters or watermarking secrets. Our findings indicate that existing watermarking schemes for AR image generation do not reliably support synthetic content detection for dataset filtering. Moreover, they enable Watermark Mimicry, whereby authentic images can be manipulated to imitate a generator's watermark and trigger false detection to prevent their inclusion in future model training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自回归图像生成水印鲁棒性研究
自回归（AR）图像生成器的普及要求对其输出进行可靠检测与溯源，以遏制虚假信息，并过滤训练数据中的合成图像以防模型崩溃。为此，我们研究了专为AR模型设计的水印技术，该技术在生成时嵌入细微信号，通过对应水印检测器实现下游验证。本研究揭示了这些方案易受水印移除与伪造攻击的脆弱性。我们评估了现有攻击方法，并提出了三种新攻击：（i）向量量化再生移除攻击，（ii）基于对抗优化的攻击，（iii）频域注入攻击。评估表明，仅需单张水印参考图像，无需原始模型参数或水印密钥，即可有效实施移除与伪造攻击。研究发现，现有AR图像生成水印方案无法可靠支持数据集过滤中的合成内容检测，且存在水印模仿现象——即真实图像可被篡改以模仿生成器水印，从而触发虚假检测，阻止其被纳入未来模型训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect and attribute synthetic images from autoregressive image generators to combat misinformation and prevent model collapse, this study evaluates watermarking schemes designed for AR models. The authors introduce three novel attacks—vector-quantized regeneration removal, adversarial optimization-based forgery, and frequency injection—that effectively remove or mimic watermarks using only a single watermarked reference image, without requiring model parameters or secrets. Experimental results show these attacks successfully evade detection, undermining the reliability of watermarking for dataset filtering and enabling watermark mimicry, where authentic images are altered to falsely trigger watermark detection and exclude themselves from training.
为应对自回归图像生成器输出的检测与溯源需求，以遏制虚假信息并防止模型崩溃，本文评估了专为AR模型设计的水印方案。作者提出了三种新攻击方法——向量量化再生移除攻击、对抗优化伪造攻击和频域注入攻击，仅需一张水印参考图像即可有效移除或伪造水印，无需模型参数或密钥。实验表明，这些攻击可成功绕过检测，削弱水印在数据集过滤中的可靠性，并实现水印模仿，即通过修改真实图像使其触发虚假水印检测，从而避免被纳入模型训练。
Proportoids
Authors: Christian Antić
First: 2022-08-31T00:04:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:54:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analogical proportions are expressions of the form ``$a$ is to $b$ what $c$ is to $d$'' at the core of analogical reasoning, which itself is at the core of artificial intelligence. This paper contributes to the mathematical foundations of analogical proportions in the axiomatic tradition as initiated -- in the tradition of the ancient Greeks -- by Yves Lepage two decades ago. More precisely, we first introduce the name ``proportoid'' for sets endowed with a 4-ary analogical proportion relation satisfying a suitable set of axioms. We then study study different kinds of proportion-preserving mappings and relations and their properties. Formally, we define homomorphisms of proportoids as mappings $\mathsf H$ satisfying $a:b::c:d$ iff $\mathsf Ha: \mathsf Hb:: \mathsf Hc: \mathsf Hd$ for all elements and show that their kernel is a congruence. Moreover, we introduce (proportional) analogies as mappings $\mathsf A$ satisfying $a:b:: \mathsf Aa: \mathsf Ab$ for all elements $a$ and $b$ in the source domain and show how to compute partial analogies. We then introduce a number of useful relations between functions (including homomorphisms and analogies) on proportoids and study their properties. In a broader sense, this paper is a further step towards a mathematical theory of analogical proportions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：比例类
类比比例是类比推理的核心，而类比推理本身又是人工智能的核心。本文在Yves Lepage二十年前开创的公理化传统（源于古希腊传统）基础上，为类比比例的数学基础做出贡献。更具体地说，我们首先为满足适当公理集合的四元类比比例关系的集合引入“比例类”这一名称。接着，我们研究不同类型的保持比例的映射和关系及其性质。形式上，我们定义比例类的同态映射H，满足对所有元素a:b::c:d当且仅当Ha:Hb::Hc:Hd，并证明其核是一个同余关系。此外，我们引入（比例）类比映射A，满足对源域中所有元素a和b，a:b::Aa:Ab，并展示如何计算部分类比。然后，我们介绍若干函数（包括同态和类比）在比例类之间的有用关系并研究其性质。广义而言，本文是迈向类比比例数学理论的进一步一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the foundational role of analogical proportions in artificial intelligence and their axiomatic development since Yves Lepage’s early work, this paper formalizes the concept of 'proportoid'—a set equipped with a 4-ary analogical proportion relation satisfying specific axioms. It introduces homomorphisms preserving proportion structures and demonstrates their kernels form congruences, while also defining analogies as mappings that preserve proportions between pairs of elements. The paper further explores relations among such mappings and their properties, advancing toward a coherent mathematical framework for analogical reasoning.
受类比比例在人工智能基础中的核心作用启发，本文引入“proportoid”概念，指满足特定公理的四元类比比例关系的集合。作者定义了保持比例的同态映射，并证明其核构成同余关系，同时引入类比映射，满足源域中任意元素与其像的比例关系。本文未进行实验，但系统建立了这些映射与关系的理论性质，推动了由Yves Lepage开创的类比比例公理化框架的发展。
SWE-AGILE: A Software Agent Framework for Efficiently Managing Dynamic Reasoning Context
Authors: Shuquan Lian, Juncheng Liu, Yazhe Chen, Yuhong Chen, Hui Li
First: 2026-04-13T16:52:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:52:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prior representative ReAct-style approaches in autonomous Software Engineering (SWE) typically lack the explicit System-2 reasoning required for deep analysis and handling complex edge cases. While recent reasoning models demonstrate the potential of extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT), applying them to the multi-turn SWE task creates a fundamental dilemma: retaining full reasoning history leads to context explosion and ``Lost-in-the-Middle'' degradation, while discarding it would force the agent to redundantly re-reason at every step. To address these challenges, we propose SWE-AGILE, a novel software agent framework designed to bridge the gap between reasoning depth, efficiency, and context constraints. SWE-AGILE introduces a Dynamic Reasoning Context strategy, maintaining a ``sliding window'' of detailed reasoning for immediate continuity to prevent redundant re-analyzing, while compressing historical reasoning content into concise Reasoning Digests. Empirically, SWE-AGILE sets a new standard for 7B-8B models on SWE-Bench-Verified using only 2.2k trajectories and 896 tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/KDEGroup/SWE-AGILE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SWE-AGILE：一种用于高效管理动态推理上下文的软件代理框架
此前在自主软件工程（SWE）中代表性的ReAct风格方法通常缺乏进行深度分析和处理复杂边缘情况所需的显式系统2推理。尽管近期推理模型展示了扩展思维链（CoT）的潜力，但将其应用于多轮SWE任务时，会面临根本性困境：保留完整推理历史会导致上下文爆炸和“中间迷失”退化，而丢弃历史则迫使代理在每一步都冗余地重新推理。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了SWE-AGILE，一种新颖的软件代理框架，旨在弥合推理深度、效率与上下文约束之间的差距。SWE-AGILE引入动态推理上下文策略，通过维护一个“滑动窗口”以保持详细推理的即时连续性，避免冗余重分析，同时将历史推理内容压缩为简洁的推理摘要。实证表明，SWE-AGILE仅用2200条轨迹和896个任务，就在SWE-Bench-Verified上为7B-8B模型设定了新标准。代码见https://github.com/KDEGroup/SWE-AGILE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for deeper, more efficient reasoning in autonomous software engineering, SWE-AGILE introduces a dynamic context management framework that balances reasoning depth with computational efficiency. It employs a sliding window to retain recent detailed reasoning steps for continuity while compressing older reasoning into concise digests to avoid context explosion and redundant re-analysis. Experimental results show that SWE-AGILE achieves state-of-the-art performance on SWE-Bench-Verified using only 2.2k trajectories and 896 tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness with 7B-8B models.
为解决现有ReAct风格软件工程代理在处理复杂边缘案例时缺乏深度系统2推理的问题，SWE-AGILE提出动态推理上下文机制，在保持推理深度的同时控制上下文开销。其通过滑动窗口保留近期详细推理以确保连续性，同时将历史推理压缩为简明摘要，避免冗余重分析和上下文爆炸。实验表明，SWE-AGILE仅用2.2k轨迹和896任务即在SWE-Bench-Verified上达到当前最优性能，显著提升多轮软件工程任务的效率与推理能力。
El Agente Estructural: An Artificially Intelligent Molecular Editor
Authors: Changhyeok Choi, Yunheng Zou, Marcel Müller, Han Hao, Yeonghun Kang, Juan B. Pérez-Sánchez, Ignacio Gustin, Hanyong Xu, Andrew Wang, Mohammad Ghazi Vakili, Chris Crebolder, Alán Aspuru-Guzik, Varinia Bernales
First: 2026-02-04T18:38:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:51:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present El Agente Estructural, a multimodal, natural-language-driven geometry-generation and manipulation agent for autonomous chemistry and molecular modelling. Unlike molecular generation or editing via generative models, Estructural mimics how human experts directly manipulate molecular systems in three dimensions by integrating a comprehensive set of domain-informed tools and vision-language models. This design enables precise control over atomic or functional group replacements, atomic connectivity, and stereochemistry without the need to rebuild extensive core molecular frameworks. Through a series of representative case studies, we demonstrate that Estructural enables chemically meaningful geometry manipulation across a wide range of real-world scenarios. These include site-selective functionalization, ligand binding, ligand exchange, stereochemically controlled structure construction, isomer interconversion, fragment-level structural analysis, image-guided generation of structures from schematic reaction mechanisms, and mechanism-driven geometry generation and modification. These examples illustrate how multimodal reasoning, when combined with specialized geometry-aware tools, supports interactive and context-aware molecular modelling beyond structure generation. Looking forward, the integration of Estructural into El Agente Quntur, an autonomous multi-agent quantum chemistry platform, enhances its capabilities by adding sophisticated tools for the generation and editing of three-dimensional structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构代理：一种人工智能分子编辑器
我们提出了结构代理，这是一种多模态、自然语言驱动的几何生成与操作代理，用于自主化学和分子建模。与通过生成模型进行分子生成或编辑不同，结构代理通过整合一套全面的领域知识工具和视觉-语言模型，模拟人类专家在三维空间中直接操作分子系统的方式。该设计无需重建复杂的分子骨架，即可实现对原子或官能团替换、原子连接性及立体化学的精确控制。通过一系列代表性案例研究，我们证明结构代理能够在多种现实场景中实现具有化学意义的几何操作，包括位点选择性功能化、配体结合与交换、立体化学控制的结构构建、异构体互变、片段级结构分析、基于示意图反应机制的图像引导结构生成，以及机制驱动的几何生成与修改。这些示例表明，当多模态推理与专门的几何感知工具结合时，可支持超越结构生成的交互式和情境感知分子建模。展望未来，将结构代理集成至自主多智能体量子化学平台El Agente Quntur中，可进一步增强其能力，增加用于三维结构生成与编辑的高级工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise, human-like control over molecular geometry without rebuilding entire molecular frameworks, El Agente Estructural is a multimodal, natural-language-driven agent that mimics expert chemists' 3D manipulation techniques by integrating domain-specific tools with vision-language models. It enables accurate atomic or functional group substitutions, connectivity adjustments, and stereochemistry control. Experimental results across diverse chemistry tasks—including site-selective functionalization, ligand binding, stereochemistry-guided synthesis, and image-based structure generation—demonstrate its ability to perform chemically meaningful geometry manipulations with high fidelity and contextual awareness, showcasing its utility beyond generative models.
为实现无需重建分子骨架的精准分子几何操作，El Agente Estructural 是一个结合领域工具与视觉-语言模型的多模态自然语言驱动智能编辑器，模拟化学专家在三维空间中的直接操作方式。实验表明，该系统在多个任务中表现优异，包括位点选择性功能化、配体结合与交换、立体化学控制结构构建、异构体转化、片段结构分析、图像引导结构生成及机制驱动几何编辑，展现出高保真、上下文感知的化学意义分子编辑能力。
DeepFleet: Multi-Agent Foundation Models for Mobile Robots
Authors: Ameya Agaskar, Sriram Siva, William Pickering, Kyle O'Brien, Charles Kekeh, Alexandre Ormiga Galvao Barbosa, Ang Li, Brianna Gallo Sarker, Alicia Chua, Mayur Nemade, Charun Thattai, Jiaming Di, Isaac Iyengar, Ramya Dharoor, Dino Kirouani, Jimmy Erskine, Tamir Hegazy, Scott Niekum, Usman A. Khan, Federico Pecora, Joseph W. Durham
First: 2025-08-12T02:19:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:50:27+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 10 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce DeepFleet, a suite of foundation models designed to support coordination and planning for large-scale mobile robot fleets. These models are trained on fleet movement data, including robot positions, goals, and interactions, from hundreds of thousands of robots in Amazon warehouses worldwide. DeepFleet consists of four architectures that each embody a distinct inductive bias and collectively explore key points in the design space for multi-agent foundation models: the robot-centric (RC) model is an autoregressive decision transformer operating on neighborhoods of individual robots; the robot-floor (RF) model uses a transformer with cross-attention between robots and the warehouse floor; the image-floor (IF) model applies convolutional encoding to a multi-channel image representation of the full fleet; and the graph-floor (GF) model combines temporal attention with graph neural networks for spatial relationships. In this paper, we describe these models and present our evaluation of the impact of these design choices on prediction task performance. We find that the robot-centric and graph-floor models, which both use asynchronous robot state updates and incorporate the localized structure of robot interactions, show the most promise. We also present experiments that show that these two models can make effective use of larger warehouses operation datasets as the models are scaled up.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepFleet：面向移动机器人的多智能体基础模型
我们提出了DeepFleet，一套旨在支持大规模移动机器人编队协调与规划的基础模型。这些模型基于来自全球亚马逊仓库数以十万计机器人运动数据进行训练，数据包括机器人位置、目标及交互信息。DeepFleet包含四种架构，每种架构体现不同的归纳偏置，并共同探索多智能体基础模型设计空间中的关键点：机器人中心（RC）模型是一种在单个机器人邻域上运行的自回归决策变换器；机器人-地板（RF）模型使用机器人与仓库地板间交叉注意力的变换器；图像-地板（IF）模型对整个编队的多通道图像表示应用卷积编码；图-地板（GF）模型结合时间注意力与图神经网络以捕捉空间关系。本文描述了这些模型，并评估了这些设计选择对预测任务性能的影响。我们发现，机器人中心和图-地板模型因采用异步机器人状态更新并融入局部机器人交互结构，表现最为突出。此外，实验表明，随着模型规模扩大，这两种模型能有效利用更大规模的仓库操作数据集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable coordination in large mobile robot fleets, DeepFleet introduces four foundation models trained on real-world warehouse data from hundreds of thousands of robots. Each model employs a distinct architectural bias: robot-centric (autoregressive decision transformer), robot-floor (cross-attention between robots and floor), image-floor (convolutional encoding of fleet images), and graph-floor (temporal attention with graph neural networks). Experimental results show that the robot-centric and graph-floor models, which leverage asynchronous updates and localized interaction structures, outperform others in prediction tasks and scale effectively with larger datasets.
为应对大规模移动机器人编队的协调需求，DeepFleet 提出四类基础模型，基于全球亚马逊仓库中数十万机器人的真实运动数据进行训练。各模型采用不同架构——机器人中心、机器人地板、图像地板和图结构地板——以探索多智能体规划的不同归纳偏置。实验表明，机器人中心与图结构地板模型因采用异步状态更新和局部交互结构，在预测任务中表现最优，且随数据规模扩大而有效提升性能，具备实际部署潜力。
BEM: Training-Free Background Embedding Memory for False-Positive Suppression in Real-Time Fixed-Background Camera
Authors: Junwoo Park, Jangho Lee, Sunho Lim
First: 2026-04-13T16:50:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:50:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Pretrained detectors perform well on benchmarks but often suffer performance degradation in real-world deployments due to distribution gaps between training data and target environments. COCO-like benchmarks emphasize category diversity rather than instance density, causing detectors trained under per-class sparsity to struggle in dense, single- or few-class scenes such as surveillance and traffic monitoring. In fixed-camera environments, the quasi-static background provides a stable, label-free prior that can be exploited at inference to suppress spurious detections. To address the issue, we propose Background Embedding Memory (BEM), a lightweight, training-free, weight-frozen module that can be attached to pretrained detectors during inference. BEM estimates clean background embeddings, maintains a prototype memory, and re-scores detection logits with an inverse-similarity, rank-weighted penalty, effectively reducing false positives while maintaining recall. Empirically, background-frame cosine similarity correlates negatively with object count and positively with Precision-Confidence AUC (P-AUC), motivating its use as a training-free control signal. Across YOLO and RT-DETR families on LLVIP and simulated surveillance streams, BEM consistently reduces false positives while preserving real-time performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leo-Park1214/Background-Embedding-Memory.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：BEM：用于实时固定背景相机中抑制误检的无训练背景嵌入记忆
预训练检测器在基准测试中表现良好，但在实际部署中常因训练数据与目标环境分布差异导致性能下降。COCO类基准侧重类别多样性而非实例密度，导致在监控和交通监控等密集、单类或少类场景中，训练于每类稀疏数据的检测器表现不佳。在固定摄像头环境中，准静态背景提供稳定的无标签先验，可在推理时用于抑制误检。为此，我们提出背景嵌入记忆（BEM），一种轻量级、无训练、权重冻结的模块，可在推理时附加于预训练检测器。BEM估计干净背景嵌入，维护原型记忆，并通过逆相似度、排序加权惩罚重新评分检测得分，有效减少误检同时保持召回率。实验证明，背景帧余弦相似度与目标数量呈负相关，与精度-置信度AUC（P-AUC）呈正相关，支持其作为无训练控制信号的应用。在LLVIP和模拟监控流上，BEM在YOLO和RT-DETR系列中持续减少误检，同时保持实时性能。代码见 https://github.com/Leo-Park1214/Background-Embedding-Memory.git
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation of pretrained detectors in real-world fixed-background scenes due to distribution gaps and per-class sparsity, the paper introduces Background Embedding Memory (BEM), a lightweight, training-free, weight-frozen module that enhances inference by leveraging quasi-static background embeddings. BEM estimates clean background representations, maintains a prototype memory, and applies an inverse-similarity, rank-weighted penalty to detection logits to suppress false positives while preserving recall. Experimental results across YOLO and RT-DETR models on LLVIP and simulated surveillance datasets show consistent false positive reduction without compromising real-time performance, with background-frame cosine similarity empirically correlating negatively with object count and positively with Precision-Confidence AUC.
为解决预训练检测器在真实固定背景场景中因训练与部署环境差异导致的性能下降问题，本文提出BEM模块，该模块无需训练、冻结权重，利用推理时的准静态背景嵌入抑制假阳性。BEM通过估计背景原型并施加逆相似度、排名加权惩罚机制调整检测得分，有效降低假阳性同时保持召回率。在LLVIP和模拟监控数据集上，YOLO与RT-DETR系列模型均验证了BEM在保持实时性能前提下持续降低假阳性，且背景帧余弦相似度与目标密度负相关、与精度-置信度AUC正相关。
Seeing Through the Tool: A Controlled Benchmark for Occlusion Robustness in Foundation Segmentation Models
Authors: Nhan Ho, Luu Le, Thanh-Huy Nguyen, Thien Nguyen, Xiaofeng Liu, Ulas Bagci
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-13T16:46:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:46:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CV4Clinic, CVPR 2026. 10 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Occlusion, where target structures are partially hidden by surgical instruments or overlapping tissues, remains a critical yet underexplored challenge for foundation segmentation models in clinical endoscopy. We introduce OccSAM-Bench, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate SAM-family models under controlled, synthesized surgical occlusion. Our framework simulates two occlusion types (i.e., surgical tool overlay and cutout) across three calibrated severity levels on three public polyp datasets. We propose a novel three-region evaluation protocol that decomposes segmentation performance into full, visible-only, and invisible targets. This metric exposes behaviors that standard amodal evaluation obscures, revealing two distinct model archetypes: Occluder-Aware models (SAM, SAM 2, SAM 3, MedSAM3), which prioritize visible tissue delineation and reject instruments, and Occluder-Agnostic models (MedSAM, MedSAM2), which confidently predict into occluded regions. SAM-Med2D aligns with neither and underperforms across all conditions. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that occlusion robustness is not uniform across architectures, and model selection must be driven by specific clinical intent-whether prioritizing conservative visible-tissue segmentation or the amodal inference of hidden anatomy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：透过工具看穿：用于基础分割模型遮挡鲁棒性评估的受控基准
在临床内镜中，目标结构被手术器械或重叠组织部分遮挡（遮挡）仍是基础分割模型亟待探索的关键挑战。我们提出了OccSAM-Bench基准，旨在在受控合成手术遮挡环境下系统评估SAM家族模型。我们的框架在三个公开的息肉数据集上模拟两种遮挡类型（即手术器械覆盖与镂空）及三个校准严重程度等级。我们提出了一种新颖的三区域评估协议，将分割性能分解为完整、仅可见和不可见目标三部分。该指标揭示了标准无模态评估所掩盖的行为，展现出两种截然不同的模型范式：遮挡感知模型（SAM、SAM 2、SAM 3、MedSAM3）优先分割可见组织并拒绝器械；遮挡无感知模型（MedSAM、MedSAM2）则自信地预测遮挡区域。SAM-Med2D既不匹配上述两类，且在所有条件下表现均不佳。最终，我们的结果表明，不同架构的遮挡鲁棒性并不一致，模型选择必须依据具体临床意图——是优先保守分割可见组织，还是进行无模态推断隐藏解剖结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate foundation segmentation models under realistic surgical occlusion, this study introduces OccSAM-Bench, a controlled benchmark that simulates tool overlay and cutout occlusions across three severity levels on three polyp datasets. It employs a novel three-region evaluation protocol to distinguish performance on full, visible-only, and invisible targets, revealing two distinct model behaviors: Occluder-Aware models prioritize visible tissue and avoid instruments, while Occluder-Agnostic models confidently predict occluded regions. SAM-Med2D underperforms across all conditions, showing no clear alignment with either archetype, and results confirm that occlusion robustness varies significantly by architecture, necessitating model selection based on clinical intent.
为评估基础分割模型在临床内镜中处理遮挡的能力，本研究提出OccSAM-Bench基准，通过在三个公开息肉数据集上模拟手术器械遮挡（三种严重程度），并采用三区域评估协议区分完全可见、部分遮挡和完全隐藏目标的分割表现。实验结果揭示两类模型行为：Occluder-Aware模型（SAM、SAM 2、SAM 3、MedSAM3）优先分割可见组织并拒绝遮挡区域，而Occluder-Agnostic模型（MedSAM、MedSAM2）则自信地预测遮挡区域。SAM-Med2D在所有条件下表现最差，未归入任一类型，表明模型架构的遮挡鲁棒性差异显著，需根据临床需求选择模型。
Physics and causally constrained discrete-time neural models of turbulent dynamical systems
Authors: Fabrizio Falasca, Laure Zanna
First: 2026-02-14T18:43:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:43:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a framework for constructing physics and causally constrained neural models of turbulent dynamical systems from data. We first formulate a finite-time flow map with strict energy-preserving nonlinearities for stable modeling of temporally discrete trajectories. We then impose causal constraints to suppress spurious interactions across degrees of freedom. The resulting neural models accurately capture stationary statistics and responses to both small and large external forcings. We demonstrate the framework on the stochastic Charney-DeVore equations and on a symmetry-broken Lorenz-96 system. The framework is broadly applicable to reduced-order modeling of turbulent dynamical systems from observational data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：湍流动力系统中基于物理与因果约束的离散时间神经模型
我们提出了一种从数据构建湍流动力系统物理与因果约束神经模型的框架。首先，我们构建具有严格能量守恒非线性的有限时间流映射，以稳定建模时序离散轨迹；随后施加因果约束以抑制自由度间的虚假相互作用。所得神经模型能准确捕捉稳态统计特性及对小至大外部强迫的响应。我们在随机Charney-DeVore方程和对称破缺的Lorenz-96系统中验证了该框架，其广泛适用于从观测数据进行湍流动力系统的降阶建模。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model turbulent systems with physical fidelity and causal structure, this work introduces a neural framework that constructs discrete-time models from data by enforcing energy-preserving nonlinearities and causal constraints. The method defines a finite-time flow map that preserves energy and suppresses spurious cross-interactions across system degrees of freedom. Experimental results on stochastic Charney-DeVore equations and a symmetry-broken Lorenz-96 system show that the models accurately reproduce stationary statistics and responses to varying external forcings, validating their utility for reduced-order modeling of turbulent dynamics.
为构建湍流动力系统中物理一致且因果约束的神经模型，该研究提出一种框架，通过有限时间流映射结合能量守恒非线性项与因果约束，避免自由度间虚假交互。方法首先定义能量守恒的离散时间流映射，再施加因果约束确保时间一致性。在随机Charney-DeVore方程与对称破缺Lorenz-96系统上的实验表明，该模型能准确再现稳态统计特性，并对小至大外部扰动做出正确响应，验证其在观测数据驱动的湍流系统降阶建模中的有效性。
A Mamba-Based Multimodal Network for Multiscale Blast-Induced Rapid Structural Damage Assessment
Authors: Wanli Ma, Sivasakthy Selvakumaran, Dain G. Farrimond, Adam A. Dennis, Samuel E. Rigby
First: 2026-04-13T16:43:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:43:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate and rapid structural damage assessment (SDA) is crucial for post-disaster management, helping responders prioritise resources, plan rescues, and support recovery. Traditional field inspections, though precise, are limited by accessibility, safety risks, and time constraints, especially after large explosions. Machine learning with remote sensing has emerged as a scalable solution for rapid SDA, with Mamba-based networks achieving state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods often require extensive training and large datasets, limiting real-world applicability. Moreover, they fail to incorporate key physical characteristics of blast loading for SDA. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Mamba-based multimodal network for rapid SDA that integrates multi-scale blast-loading information with optical remote sensing images. Evaluated on the 2020 Beirut explosion, our method significantly improves performance over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at: https://github.com/IMPACTSquad/Blast-Mamba
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Mamba的多模态网络用于多尺度爆炸引起的快速结构损伤评估
准确且快速的结构损伤评估（SDA）对灾后管理至关重要，有助于救援人员优先分配资源、规划救援并支持恢复。尽管传统现场检查精度高，但受限于可及性、安全风险和时间约束，尤其在大规模爆炸后。结合遥感的机器学习已成为快速SDA的可扩展解决方案，其中基于Mamba的网络已达到最先进性能。然而，这些方法通常需要大量训练和数据集，限制了实际应用。此外，它们未能纳入爆炸载荷的关键物理特性以进行SDA。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于Mamba的多模态网络，用于快速SDA，该网络整合了多尺度爆炸载荷信息与光学遥感图像。在2020年贝鲁特爆炸事件中评估，我们的方法显著优于现有最先进方法。代码可在：https://github.com/IMPACTSquad/Blast-Mamba 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for rapid and accurate structural damage assessment after blast events to support emergency response, this study proposes a Mamba-based multimodal network that fuses multi-scale blast-loading data with optical remote sensing imagery. The method leverages the Mamba architecture’s efficiency in sequence modeling to process heterogeneous inputs and capture spatiotemporal damage patterns. Evaluated on the 2020 Beirut explosion dataset, the model outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in damage classification and localization, demonstrating robustness and scalability without requiring large training datasets or extensive fine-tuning.
为解决传统现场检查效率低、现有机器学习方法依赖大量数据且忽略爆炸物理特性的问题，本文提出一种基于Mamba架构的多模态网络，融合多尺度爆炸载荷信息与光学遥感图像。该方法利用Mamba架构处理长距离依赖的优势，结合爆炸物理特征与视觉数据提升损伤预测能力。在2020年贝鲁特爆炸事件数据集上的实验表明，该模型在准确率和速度上显著优于现有最先进方法，具备良好的泛化能力和实际应用价值。
ACT: Automated CPS Testing for Open-Source Robotic Platforms
Authors: Aditya A. Krishnan, Donghoon Kim, Hokeun Kim
First: 2026-04-13T16:42:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:42:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-source software for cyber-physical systems (CPS) often lacks robust testing involving robotic platforms, resulting in critical errors that remain undetected. This is especially challenging when multiple modules of CPS software are developed by various open-source contributors. To address this gap, we propose Automated CPS Testing (ACT) that performs automated, continuous testing of open-source software with its robotic platforms, integrated with the open-source infrastructure such as GitHub. We implement an ACT prototype and conduct a case study on an open-source CPS with an educational robotic platform to demonstrate its capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACT：面向开源机器人平台的自动化CPS测试
开源软件在涉及机器人平台的测试方面往往缺乏充分的健壮性测试，导致关键错误未被发现。尤其当CPS软件的多个模块由不同开源贡献者开发时，这一问题尤为突出。为解决此问题，我们提出自动化CPS测试（ACT），通过与GitHub等开源基础设施集成，对开源软件及其机器人平台进行自动化、持续测试。我们实现了一个ACT原型，并在一款教育机器人平台的开源CPS上开展案例研究，以展示其能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of robust testing for open-source cyber-physical systems (CPS) involving robotic platforms, which often leads to undetected critical errors due to fragmented development, the paper introduces Automated CPS Testing (ACT), a framework that enables continuous, automated testing integrated with open-source infrastructure like GitHub. The authors implement a prototype and evaluate it through a case study on an educational robotic platform, demonstrating that ACT can effectively detect failures across CPS modules and improve testing coverage without requiring manual intervention.
由于开源的物理信息系统（CPS）缺乏针对机器人平台的充分测试，导致关键错误常被遗漏，且因多模块由不同贡献者开发而更难保障质量，作者提出自动化CPS测试（ACT）框架，实现与GitHub等开源基础设施集成的持续自动化测试。通过在教育机器人平台上的案例研究，ACT原型成功检测了多模块故障，提升了分布式开发环境中的测试覆盖率。
Representations Before Pixels: Semantics-Guided Hierarchical Video Prediction
Authors: Efstathios Karypidis, Spyros Gidaris, Nikos Komodakis
First: 2026-04-13T16:42:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:42:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate future video prediction requires both high visual fidelity and consistent scene semantics, particularly in complex dynamic environments such as autonomous driving. We present Re2Pix, a hierarchical video prediction framework that decomposes forecasting into two stages: semantic representation prediction and representation-guided visual synthesis. Instead of directly predicting future RGB frames, our approach first forecasts future scene structure in the feature space of a frozen vision foundation model, and then conditions a latent diffusion model on these predicted representations to render photorealistic frames. This decomposition enables the model to focus first on scene dynamics and then on appearance generation. A key challenge arises from the train-test mismatch between ground-truth representations available during training and predicted ones used at inference. To address this, we introduce two conditioning strategies, nested dropout and mixed supervision, that improve robustness to imperfect autoregressive predictions. Experiments on challenging driving benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed semantics-first design significantly improves temporal semantic consistency, perceptual quality, and training efficiency compared to strong diffusion baselines. We provide the implementation code at https://github.com/Sta8is/Re2Pix
中文标题/摘要
标题：像素前的表示：语义引导的分层视频预测
准确的未来视频预测需要高视觉保真度和一致的场景语义，特别是在自动驾驶等复杂动态环境中。我们提出了Re2Pix，一种分层视频预测框架，将预测分解为两个阶段：语义表示预测和表示引导的视觉合成。与直接预测未来RGB帧不同，我们的方法首先在冻结的视觉基础模型的特征空间中预测未来场景结构，然后将这些预测表示作为条件输入给潜在扩散模型，生成逼真的帧。这种分解使模型能先关注场景动态，再关注外观生成。一个关键挑战是训练时可用的真实表示与推理时使用的预测表示之间存在训练-测试不匹配。为解决此问题，我们引入了嵌套dropout和混合监督两种条件策略，以提高对不完美自回归预测的鲁棒性。在具有挑战性的驾驶基准测试中，实验表明，所提出的语义优先设计显著优于强扩散基线，在时序语义一致性、感知质量和训练效率方面均有提升。代码已发布在https://github.com/Sta8is/Re2Pix
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for both visual fidelity and semantic consistency in complex dynamic environments like autonomous driving, Re2Pix introduces a hierarchical video prediction framework that first predicts future scene semantics in a frozen vision model’s feature space, then uses these representations to guide a latent diffusion model for photorealistic frame synthesis. To handle the train-test mismatch between ground-truth and predicted representations, it employs nested dropout and mixed supervision to enhance robustness. Experiments on driving benchmarks show that this semantics-first approach significantly improves temporal consistency, perceptual quality, and training efficiency over existing diffusion-based methods.
为提升复杂动态场景（如自动驾驶）中未来视频预测的准确性，Re2Pix提出分层框架：先用冻结的视觉基础模型预测场景语义表示，再以此引导潜在扩散模型生成逼真帧。该两阶段设计优先关注场景结构而非像素细节，提升时序一致性与视觉质量。为应对训练与推理阶段表示不匹配的问题，方法引入嵌套dropout与混合监督策略，增强鲁棒性。在驾驶基准测试中，Re2Pix在语义一致性、视觉保真度和训练效率上显著优于主流扩散基线模型。
Agentic Driving Coach: Robustness and Determinism of Agentic AI-Powered Human-in-the-Loop Cyber-Physical Systems
Authors: Deeksha Prahlad, Daniel Fan, Hokeun Kim
First: 2026-04-13T16:42:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:42:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Foundation models, including large language models (LLMs), are increasingly used for human-in-the-loop (HITL) cyber-physical systems (CPS) because foundation model-based AI agents can potentially interact with both the physical environments and human users. However, the unpredictable behavior of human users and AI agents, in addition to the dynamically changing physical environments, leads to uncontrollable nondeterminism. To address this urgent challenge of enabling agentic AI-powered HITL CPS, we propose a reactor-model-of-computation (MoC)-based approach, realized by the open-source Lingua Franca (LF) framework. We also carry out a concrete case study using the agentic driving coach as an application of HITL CPS. By evaluating the LF-based agentic HITL CPS, we identify practical challenges in reintroducing determinism into such agentic HITL CPS and present pathways to address them.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理式驾驶教练：基于代理AI的人机协同物理系统中的鲁棒性与确定性
基础模型（包括大语言模型LLM）正越来越多地用于人机协同（HITL）的物理系统（CPS），因为基于基础模型的AI代理能够潜在地与物理环境和人类用户交互。然而，人类用户和AI代理的不可预测行为，加上动态变化的物理环境，导致了不可控的非确定性。为应对这一紧迫挑战，我们提出了一种基于反应器模型计算（MoC）的方法，该方法通过开源的Lingua Franca（LF）框架实现。我们还通过代理式驾驶教练作为HITL CPS的应用案例进行了具体研究。通过对基于LF的代理式HITL CPS评估，我们识别了在此类系统中重新引入确定性所面临的实际挑战，并提出了应对路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage unpredictability in human-in-the-loop cyber-physical systems powered by agentic AI, this work proposes a reactor-model-of-computation approach implemented via the Lingua Franca framework to enforce determinism. Through a case study of an agentic driving coach, the study evaluates how well such systems can reintroduce predictability amid dynamic human and environmental inputs. Experimental results reveal significant challenges in achieving robust determinism in agentic HITL CPS, while also identifying practical pathways to mitigate these issues.
为应对由人类行为不可预测性及物理环境动态变化导致的、基于AI代理的人机协同物理系统中的非确定性问题，本文提出基于反应器计算模型的解决方案，并通过开源框架Lingua Franca实现。以智能驾驶教练为案例研究，结果表明此类系统难以恢复确定性，并指出了实际障碍，同时提出了可行的解决路径。
Fairness is Not Flat: Geometric Phase Transitions Against Shortcut Learning
Authors: Nicolas Rodriguez-Alvarez, Fernando Rodriguez-Merino
First: 2026-04-13T16:40:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:40:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep Neural Networks are highly susceptible to shortcut learning, frequently memorizing low-dimensional spurious correlations instead of underlying causal mechanisms. This phenomenon not only degrades out-of-distribution robustness but also induces severe demographic biases in sensitive applications. In this paper, we propose a geometric \textit{a priori} methodology to mitigate shortcut learning. By deploying a zero-hidden-layer ($N=1$) Topological Auditor, we mathematically isolate features that monopolize the gradient without human intervention. We empirically demonstrate a Capacity Phase Transition: once linear shortcuts are pruned, networks are forced to utilize higher geometric capacity ($N \geq 16$) to curve the decision boundary and learn ethical representations. Our approach outperforms L1 Regularization -- which collapses into demographic bias -- and operates at a fraction of the computational cost of post-hoc methods like Just Train Twice (JTT), successfully reducing counterfactual gender vulnerability from 21.18\% to 7.66\%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：公平并非平坦：对抗捷径学习的几何相变
深度神经网络极易受到捷径学习的影响，常记忆低维虚假相关性而非底层因果机制。这一现象不仅降低分布外鲁棒性，还会在敏感应用中引发严重人口偏见。本文提出一种几何先验方法以缓解捷径学习。通过部署零隐藏层（N=1）拓扑审计器，我们无需人工干预即可数学上分离垄断梯度的特征。实证表明存在容量相变：一旦线性捷径被剪枝，网络被迫利用更高几何容量（N≥16）弯曲决策边界并学习伦理表征。该方法优于导致人口偏见的L1正则化，且计算成本仅为事后方法（如Just Train Twice, JTT）的几分之一，成功将反事实性别脆弱性从21.18%降至7.66%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of shortcut learning in deep neural networks that leads to biased and brittle models, this paper introduces a geometric, a priori method using a zero-hidden-layer Topological Auditor to identify and prune gradient-dominant features that encode spurious correlations. The method reveals a Capacity Phase Transition: after removing linear shortcuts, networks must adopt higher geometric capacity (N ≥ 16) to learn complex, ethical decision boundaries. Experimentally, this approach outperforms L1 regularization—which exacerbates demographic bias—and is computationally cheaper than post-hoc methods like JTT, reducing counterfactual gender vulnerability from 21.18% to 7.66%.
为解决深度神经网络因捷径学习导致的模型偏见与脆弱性问题，本文提出一种几何先验方法，通过零隐藏层拓扑审计器自动识别并剪枝主导梯度的特征，从而消除虚假相关性。实验发现，剪枝线性捷径后，网络必须启用更高几何容量（N≥16）以弯曲决策边界，学习更稳健的伦理表征。该方法优于易加剧性别偏见的L1正则化，且计算成本远低于后处理方法如Just Train Twice，将反事实性别脆弱性从21.18%降至7.66%。
DreamKG: A KG-Augmented Conversational System for People Experiencing Homelessness
Authors: Javad M Alizadeh, Genhui Zheng, Chiu C Tan, Yuzhou Chen, Omar Martinez, Philip McCallion, Ying Ding, Chenguang Yang, AnneMarie Tomosky, Huanmei Wu
First: 2026-04-13T16:38:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:38:36+00:00
Comments: This manuscript has been accepted at the 14th IEEE International Conference on Healthcare Informatics (ICHI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People experiencing homelessness (PEH) face substantial barriers to accessing timely, accurate information about community services. DreamKG addresses this through a knowledge graph-augmented conversational system that grounds responses in verified, up-to-date data about Philadelphia organizations, services, locations, and hours. Unlike standard large language models (LLMs) prone to hallucinations, DreamKG combines Neo4j knowledge graphs with structured query understanding to handle location-aware and time-sensitive queries reliably. The system performs spatial reasoning for distance-based recommendations and temporal filtering for operating hours. Preliminary evaluation shows 59% superiority over Google Search AI on relevant queries and 84% rejection of irrelevant queries. This demonstration highlights the potential of hybrid architectures that combines LLM flexibility with knowledge graph reliability to improve service accessibility for vulnerable populations effectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamKG：面向无家可归人群的增强型知识图谱对话系统
无家可归者面临获取社区服务及时准确信息的重大障碍。DreamKG通过一个基于知识图谱的对话系统解决此问题，其回答基于费城相关组织、服务、地点和营业时间的经验证据和最新数据。与易产生幻觉的标准大语言模型不同，DreamKG结合Neo4j知识图谱与结构化查询理解，可靠处理位置感知和时间敏感查询。系统支持基于距离的空间推理推荐和基于营业时间的时序过滤。初步评估显示，在相关查询上优于谷歌搜索AI 59%，对无关查询拒绝率达84%。该演示突显了结合大语言模型灵活性与知识图谱可靠性的混合架构，可有效提升对弱势群体的服务可及性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to provide accurate, location- and time-sensitive information to people experiencing homelessness, DreamKG integrates a Neo4j knowledge graph with large language models to reduce hallucinations and improve reliability. The system uses structured queries to reason spatially and temporally, offering distance-based recommendations and filtering by operating hours. Experimental results show it outperforms Google Search AI by 59% on relevant queries and rejects 84% of irrelevant ones, demonstrating the effectiveness of hybrid architectures for vulnerable populations.
为帮助无家可归者获取准确的地理位置与时间敏感信息，DreamKG结合Neo4j知识图谱与大语言模型，减少幻觉并提升响应可靠性。系统通过结构化查询实现基于距离的推荐和营业时间过滤，所有回答均基于费城的验证数据。实验表明，其在相关查询上比谷歌搜索AI高出59%，且能拒绝84%的无关查询，验证了混合架构在服务弱势群体方面的有效性。
AIRA_2: Overcoming Bottlenecks in AI Research Agents
Authors: Karen Hambardzumyan, Nicolas Baldwin, Edan Toledo, Rishi Hazra, Michael Kuchnik, Bassel Al Omari, Thomas Simon Foster, Anton Protopopov, Jean-Christophe Gagnon-Audet, Ishita Mediratta, Kelvin Niu, Michael Shvartsman, Alisia Lupidi, Alexis Audran-Reiss, Parth Pathak, Tatiana Shavrina, Despoina Magka, Hela Momand, Derek Dunfield, Nicola Cancedda, Pontus Stenetorp, Carole-Jean Wu, Jakob Nicolaus Foerster, Yoram Bachrach, Martin Josifoski
First: 2026-03-27T15:02:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:38:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing research has identified three structural performance bottlenecks in AI research agents: (1) synchronous single-GPU execution constrains sample throughput, limiting the benefit of search; (2) a generalization gap where validation-based selection causes overfitting and performance to degrade over extended search horizons; and (3) the limited capability of fixed, single-turn LLM operators imposes a ceiling on search performance. We introduce AIRA$_2$, which addresses these bottlenecks through three architectural choices: an asynchronous multi-GPU worker pool that increases experiment throughput linearly; a Hidden Consistent Evaluation protocol that delivers a reliable evaluation signal; and ReAct agents that dynamically scope their actions and debug interactively. On MLE-bench-30, AIRA$^{\dagger}_{2}$ achieves a mean Percentile Rank of 81.5% at 24 hours and 83.1% at 72 hours, outperforming the strongest baseline, which achieves 72.7%.
