On AIRS-Bench, AIRA$_2$ exceeds human state-of-the-art on 6 out of 20 diverse research tasks. Ablations confirm that each architectural component is necessary, that performance follows a predictable scaling law that transfers across LLM backbones, and that the "overfitting" reported in prior work was driven by evaluation noise rather than true data memorization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AIRA_2：克服AI研究代理中的瓶颈
现有研究已识别出AI研究代理中的三个结构性能瓶颈：(1) 同步单GPU执行限制了样本吞吐量，削弱了搜索的效益；(2) 验证驱动的选择导致过拟合，且在长期搜索中性能下降；(3) 固定单轮LLM操作符的能力有限，限制了搜索性能上限。我们提出了AIRA_2，通过三个架构选择解决这些瓶颈：异步多GPU工作池线性提升实验吞吐量；隐藏一致评估协议提供可靠评估信号；以及ReAct代理动态调整行动范围并交互式调试。在MLE-bench-30上，AIRA_2在24小时和72小时分别取得81.5%和83.1%的平均百分位排名，优于最强基线（72.7%）。在AIRS-Bench上，AIRA_2在20个多样化研究任务中的6个任务上超越人类当前最佳水平。消融实验验证了每个架构组件的必要性，性能遵循可跨LLM骨干网络迁移的可预测缩放规律，且先前工作报告的“过拟合”源于评估噪声而非真实数据记忆。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by three key performance bottlenecks in AI research agents—low throughput from synchronous single-GPU execution, overfitting due to validation-based selection, and limited capability from fixed LLM operators—AIRA_2 introduces three architectural innovations: an asynchronous multi-GPU worker pool for linear throughput scaling, a Hidden Consistent Evaluation protocol to stabilize performance signals, and ReAct agents for dynamic action scoping and interactive debugging. Experiments on MLE-bench-30 show AIRA_2 achieving 81.5% and 83.1% mean percentile rank at 24 and 72 hours respectively, surpassing the strongest baseline (72.7%). On AIRS-Bench, it outperforms human state-of-the-art on six of twenty tasks. Ablations confirm each component is essential, performance scales predictably across LLMs, and prior "overfitting" was caused by evaluation noise, not memorization.
为解决AI研究代理中的三大瓶颈——同步单GPU执行导致吞吐量低、验证选择引发过拟合、固定单轮LLM操作者能力受限——AIRA_2引入三项架构改进：异步多GPU工作池提升吞吐量、隐藏一致性评估协议减少评估噪声、ReAct代理实现动态行动范围与交互式调试。在MLE-bench-30上，AIRA_2在24小时和72小时分别达到81.5%和83.1%的平均百分位排名，优于最强基线（72.7%）。在AIRS-Bench上，其在20个多样化任务中6项超越人类顶尖水平。消融实验验证各组件不可或缺，性能在不同LLM间可预测扩展，且此前所谓“过拟合”实为评估噪声所致而非真实记忆。
HeartSway: Exploring Biodata as Poetic Traces in Public Space
Authors: Zeyu Huang, Zhifan Guo, Xingyu Li, Xiaojuan Ma, Noura Howell
First: 2026-04-13T16:37:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:37:52+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures, to be published in Proceedings of the 2026 ACM Designing Interactive Systems Conference (DIS '26)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human traces scattered across urban landscapes can signify our everyday lives and societal vibrancy in subtle and poetic forms. In this paper, we explore how designed technology can engage biodata as evocative traces. To this end, we present the design, implementation, and evaluation of HeartSway, an interactive hammock that captures a user's heart rate and micro-movements as traces and replays them as an embodied experience for the next visitor. Through a qualitative field study (N=10), we find that HeartSway evokes feelings of connection, curiosity about prior users, and appreciation for shared human vitality. Our work contributes to understanding anonymous archival biodata as a design material for experiential urban traces. We offer design considerations for intimate asynchronous encounters between strangers in public spaces and for reimagining public amenities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HeartSway：探索生物数据作为公共空间中的诗意痕迹
散布于城市景观中的人类痕迹，以微妙而诗意的方式象征着我们的日常生活与社会活力。本文探讨了如何通过设计技术将生物数据转化为富有感染力的痕迹。为此，我们呈现了HeartSway的设计、实现与评估：这是一种互动吊床，能捕捉用户的心率与微小动作作为痕迹，并将其重放为下一位访客的具身体验。通过一项定性实地研究（N=10），我们发现HeartSway唤起用户对前使用者的连接感、好奇心以及对共享人类活力的欣赏。我们的工作有助于理解匿名存档的生物数据作为一种设计材料，用于体验式城市痕迹。我们为公共空间中陌生人之间亲密的异步相遇以及重新想象公共设施提供了设计考量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to transform anonymous biodata into poetic, shared urban experiences, the authors designed HeartSway, an interactive hammock that captures and replays users' heart rate and micro-movements as embodied traces for subsequent visitors. Through a qualitative field study with ten participants, they found that the installation evoked feelings of connection, curiosity about prior users, and appreciation for collective human vitality, demonstrating how biodata can serve as an intimate yet anonymous medium for public space interaction. The work contributes design principles for asynchronous, embodied encounters between strangers and proposes reimagining public amenities as responsive, affective environments.
为将匿名生物数据转化为城市公共空间中的诗意共享体验，作者设计了HeartSway——一款可捕捉并回放用户心率与微动作的互动吊床，供后续使用者体验。通过对十名参与者进行质性实地研究，发现该装置激发了用户对前使用者的好奇、对集体生命活力的欣赏以及情感联结，证明生物数据可作为城市空间中匿名而亲密的交互媒介。该研究推动了公共空间中异步体感互动的设计实践，并提出重新想象公共设施为体验式档案的新思路。
Exploring Radiologists' Expectations of Explainable Machine Learning Models in Medical Image Analysis
Authors: Sara Ketabi, Matthias W. Wagner, Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner, Greg A. Jamieson, Farzad Khalvati
First: 2026-04-13T16:37:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:37:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In spite of the strong performance of machine learning (ML) models in radiology, they have not been widely accepted by radiologists, limiting clinical integration. A key reason is the lack of explainability, which ensures that model predictions are understandable and verifiable by clinicians. Several methods and tools have been proposed to improve explainability, but most reflect developers' perspectives and lack systematic clinical validation. In this work, we gathered insights from radiologists with varying experience and specialties into explainable ML requirements through a structured questionnaire. They also highlighted key clinical tasks where ML could be most beneficial and how it might be deployed. Based on their input, we propose guidelines for designing and developing explainable ML models in radiology. These guidelines can help researchers develop clinically useful models, facilitating integration into radiology practice as a supportive tool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索放射科医生对医学影像分析中可解释机器学习模型的期望
尽管机器学习（ML）模型在放射学中表现出色，但尚未被放射科医生广泛接受，限制了其临床整合。关键原因在于缺乏可解释性，即确保模型预测结果可被临床医生理解并验证。已有多种方法和工具被提出以提升可解释性，但多数仅反映开发者视角，缺乏系统性的临床验证。本研究通过结构化问卷收集了不同经验与专长的放射科医生对可解释ML的需求见解，他们还指出了ML最能发挥效益的关键临床任务及其部署方式。基于其意见，我们提出放射学领域设计与开发可解释ML模型的指导原则，以帮助研究人员开发具有临床实用价值的模型，促进其作为辅助工具融入放射学实践。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the gap between high-performing machine learning models in radiology and radiologists' reluctance to adopt them due to lack of explainability, this study gathered structured feedback from radiologists across experience levels and specialties via questionnaire. The method focused on identifying clinical tasks where ML could add value and how explainability should be implemented in practice. Results revealed radiologists prioritize transparency, relevance to diagnostic reasoning, and integration into existing workflows, leading to proposed guidelines for designing explainable ML models that align with clinical needs and support radiologists’ decision-making.
鉴于高性能机器学习模型在放射学中的应用受限于临床可解释性不足，本研究通过问卷收集了不同经验与专长放射医师的反馈。方法聚焦于明确其对可解释模型的需求、ML最能发挥作用的临床任务及部署偏好。主要成果是提出一套面向放射医师实际需求的可解释模型设计指南，旨在帮助研究人员开发更贴近临床实践的模型，促进其在放射科工作流程中的有效整合。
Legal2LogicICL: Improving Generalization in Transforming Legal Cases to Logical Formulas via Diverse Few-Shot Learning
Authors: Jieying Xue, Phuong Minh Nguyen, Ha Thanh Nguyen, May Myo Zin, Ken Satoh
First: 2026-04-13T16:36:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:36:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICAIL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This work aims to improve the generalization of logic-based legal reasoning systems by integrating recent advances in NLP with legal-domain adaptive few-shot learning techniques using LLMs. Existing logic-based legal reasoning pipelines typically rely on fine-tuned models to map natural-language legal cases into logical formulas before forwarding them to a symbolic reasoner. However, such approaches are heavily constrained by the scarcity of high-quality annotated training data. To address this limitation, we propose a novel LLM-based legal reasoning framework that enables effective in-context learning through retrieval-augmented generation. Specifically, we introduce Legal2LogicICL, a few-shot retrieval framework that balances diversity and similarity of exemplars at both the latent semantic representation level and the legal text structure level. In addition, our method explicitly accounts for legal structure by mitigating entity-induced retrieval bias in legal texts, where lengthy and highly specific entity mentions often dominate semantic representations and obscure legally meaningful reasoning patterns. Our Legal2LogicICL constructs informative and robust few-shot demonstrations, leading to accurate and stable logical rule generation without requiring additional training. In addition, we construct a new dataset, named Legal2Proleg, which is annotated with alignments between legal cases and PROLEG logical formulas to support the evaluation of legal semantic parsing. Experimental results on both open-source and proprietary LLMs demonstrate that our approach significantly improves accuracy, stability, and generalization in transforming natural-language legal case descriptions into logical representations, highlighting its effectiveness for interpretable and reliable legal reasoning. Our code is available at https://github.com/yingjie7/Legal2LogicICL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Legal2LogicICL：通过多样化的少样本学习提升法律案例转逻辑公式的一般化能力
本工作旨在通过结合自然语言处理领域的最新进展与基于大语言模型的法律领域自适应少样本学习技术，提升基于逻辑的法律推理系统的泛化能力。现有基于逻辑的法律推理流程通常依赖微调模型将自然语言法律案例映射为逻辑公式，再传递给符号推理器。然而，此类方法受限于高质量标注训练数据的稀缺。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于大语言模型的法律推理框架，通过检索增强生成实现有效的上下文学习。具体而言，我们引入Legal2LogicICL，一种少样本检索框架，该框架在潜在语义表示层面和法律文本结构层面兼顾示例的多样性和相似性。此外，我们的方法通过缓解法律文本中实体引发的检索偏差，显式考虑法律结构，其中冗长且高度具体的实体提及常主导语义表示，掩盖法律上重要的推理模式。Legal2LogicICL构建了信息丰富且稳健的少样本演示，无需额外训练即可实现准确且稳定的逻辑规则生成。同时，我们构建了一个新数据集Legal2Proleg，标注了法律案例与PROLEG逻辑公式之间的对齐关系，以支持法律语义解析的评估。在开源和专有大语言模型上的实验结果表明，我们的方法显著提升了将自然语言法律案例描述转化为逻辑表示的准确性、稳定性和泛化能力，凸显其在可解释且可靠的法律推理中的有效性。代码已开源：https://github.com/yingjie7/Legal2LogicICL。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of generalizing legal reasoning systems by improving their ability to convert natural-language legal cases into logical formulas using few-shot learning with large language models. The authors propose Legal2LogicICL, a retrieval-augmented framework that selects diverse yet semantically relevant exemplars to guide in-context learning, while mitigating retrieval bias caused by overly specific legal entities. The method constructs robust few-shot demonstrations that enhance logical rule generation without additional training. Evaluated on both open-source and proprietary LLMs, Legal2LogicICL significantly improves accuracy, stability, and generalization compared to baseline methods, validated using a newly created dataset, Legal2Proleg, which aligns legal cases with PROLEG logical formulas.
该论文旨在提升法律推理系统在自然语言法律案例向逻辑公式转换中的泛化能力，通过改进大语言模型的少样本学习。作者提出Legal2LogicICL框架，结合检索增强生成技术，选择语义相关且结构多样的示例以引导上下文学习，同时缓解法律文本中实体信息过重导致的检索偏差。该方法在语义和法律结构层面平衡示例多样性，生成更准确稳定的逻辑规则，无需额外训练。实验表明，该方法在开源和专有LLM上显著优于基线模型，在准确性、稳定性和泛化能力方面均有提升，且依托新构建的Legal2Proleg数据集（标注法律案例与PROLEG逻辑公式对齐）进行评估。
Integrating Semi-Supervised and Active Learning for Semantic Segmentation
Authors: Wanli Ma, Oktay Karakus, Paul L. Rosin
First: 2025-01-31T15:37:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:32:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a novel active learning approach integrated with an improved semi-supervised learning framework to reduce the cost of manual annotation and enhance model performance. Our proposed approach effectively leverages both the labelled data selected through active learning and the unlabelled data excluded from the selection process. The proposed active learning approach pinpoints areas where the pseudo-labels are likely to be inaccurate. Then, an automatic and efficient pseudo-label auto-refinement (PLAR) module is proposed to correct pixels with potentially erroneous pseudo-labels by comparing their feature representations with those of labelled regions. This approach operates without increasing the labelling budget and is based on the cluster assumption, which states that pixels belonging to the same class should exhibit similar representations in feature space. Furthermore, manual labelling is only applied to the most difficult and uncertain areas in unlabelled data, where insufficient information prevents the PLAR module from making a decision. We evaluated the proposed hybrid semi-supervised active learning framework on two benchmark datasets, one from natural and the other from remote sensing imagery domains. In both cases, it outperformed state-of-the-art methods in the semantic segmentation task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结合半监督学习与主动学习的语义分割方法
本文提出了一种新颖的主动学习方法，与改进的半监督学习框架相结合，以降低人工标注成本并提升模型性能。该方法有效利用了通过主动学习选择的标注数据和未被选中的未标注数据。所提出的主动学习方法能识别伪标签可能不准确的区域，随后提出一种自动高效的伪标签自优化（PLAR）模块，通过比较像素特征表示与标注区域的特征表示，修正潜在错误的伪标签。该方法无需增加标注预算，基于聚类假设（即同一类别的像素在特征空间中应具有相似表示）。此外，人工标注仅应用于未标注数据中最困难、最不确定的区域，这些区域因信息不足导致PLAR模块无法做出判断。我们在两个基准数据集（自然图像与遥感图像）上评估了该混合半监督主动学习框架，结果在语义分割任务中优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of manual annotation in semantic segmentation, this paper proposes a hybrid framework combining semi-supervised and active learning to improve performance while reducing labeling effort. The method selects labeled data via active learning and uses an automatic pseudo-label auto-refinement (PLAR) module to correct potentially erroneous pseudo-labels in unlabeled data by leveraging feature space clustering. Manual labeling is reserved only for the most uncertain regions where PLAR cannot make reliable decisions. Experiments on two benchmark datasets—natural and remote sensing imagery—show that this approach consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in semantic segmentation without increasing the labeling budget.
为降低语义分割中人工标注成本并提升模型性能，本文提出一种融合半监督学习与主动学习的混合框架。该方法通过主动学习选择标注样本，并引入伪标签自动修正（PLAR）模块，利用特征空间中的聚类假设，自动修正未标注数据中可能错误的伪标签。人工标注仅用于PLAR无法处理的高不确定性区域。在自然图像与遥感图像两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不增加标注预算的前提下，显著优于现有最先进方法。
LARY: A Latent Action Representation Yielding Benchmark for Generalizable Vision-to-Action Alignment
Authors: Dujun Nie, Fengjiao Chen, Qi Lv, Jun Kuang, Xiaoyu Li, Xuezhi Cao, Xunliang Cai
First: 2026-04-13T16:30:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:30:35+00:00
Comments: Project: https://meituan-longcat.github.io/LARYBench Code: https://github.com/meituan-longcat/LARYBench Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/meituan-longcat/LARYBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
While the shortage of explicit action data limits Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, human action videos offer a scalable yet unlabeled data source. A critical challenge in utilizing large-scale human video datasets lies in transforming visual signals into ontology-independent representations, known as latent actions. However, the capacity of latent action representation to derive robust control from visual observations has yet to be rigorously evaluated. We introduce the Latent Action Representation Yielding (LARY) Benchmark, a unified framework for evaluating latent action representations on both high-level semantic actions (what to do) and low-level robotic control (how to do). The comprehensively curated dataset encompasses over one million videos (1,000 hours) spanning 151 action categories, alongside 620K image pairs and 595K motion trajectories across diverse embodiments and environments. Our experiments reveal two crucial insights: (i) General visual foundation models, trained without any action supervision, consistently outperform specialized embodied latent action models. (ii) Latent-based visual space is fundamentally better aligned to physical action space than pixel-based space. These results suggest that general visual representations inherently encode action-relevant knowledge for physical control, and that semantic-level abstraction serves as a fundamentally more effective pathway from vision to action than pixel-level reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LARY：一种生成通用视觉-动作对齐基准的潜在动作表示
尽管显式动作数据稀缺限制了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的发展，但人类动作视频提供了一种可扩展且无需标注的数据来源。在利用大规模人类视频数据集时，关键挑战在于将视觉信号转化为与本体无关的潜在动作表示。然而，潜在动作表示从视觉观测中推导稳健控制的能力尚未经过严格评估。我们提出了潜在动作表示生成（LARY）基准，这是一个统一框架，用于评估潜在动作表示在高层语义动作（做什么）和低层机器人控制（如何做）两个层面的表现。该数据集全面收录了超过一百万视频（1000小时），涵盖151个动作类别，同时包含62万张图像对和59.5万条运动轨迹，覆盖多种具身形态与环境。实验揭示两个关键发现：（i）未经任何动作监督训练的通用视觉基础模型，持续优于专门的具身潜在动作模型；（ii）基于潜在表示的视觉空间比基于像素的空间更本质地与物理动作空间对齐。这些结果表明，通用视觉表示天然编码了物理控制相关的动作知识，且语义层面的抽象比像素层面的重建是视觉到动作更根本有效的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of labeled action data for Vision-Language-Action models, this work leverages large-scale unlabeled human action videos to evaluate latent action representations. It introduces the LARY Benchmark, a unified framework assessing both semantic action understanding and robotic control, using a dataset of over one million videos, 620K image pairs, and 595K motion trajectories across 151 action categories. Experiments show that general visual foundation models, trained without action supervision, outperform specialized embodied models, and that latent visual representations are more aligned with physical action space than pixel-based representations, indicating that semantic abstraction is more effective for vision-to-action alignment than pixel reconstruction.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏标注动作数据的问题，该研究利用大规模未标注的人类动作视频，构建LARY基准测试，评估从语义动作理解到机器人控制的潜在动作表示能力。该数据集包含超过一百万视频、62万图像对和59.5万运动轨迹，覆盖151类动作。实验表明，未经动作监督训练的通用视觉基础模型性能优于专用具身模型，且基于潜在表示的视觉空间比像素空间更贴近物理动作空间，说明语义抽象比像素重建更有效实现视觉到动作的对齐。
Please Make it Sound like Human: Encoder-Decoder vs. Decoder-Only Transformers for AI-to-Human Text Style Transfer
Authors: Utsav Paneru
First: 2026-04-13T16:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:30:15+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-generated text has become common in academic and professional writing, prompting research into detection methods. Less studied is the reverse: systematically rewriting AI-generated prose to read as genuinely human-authored. We build a parallel corpus of 25,140 paired AI-input and human-reference text chunks, identify 11 measurable stylistic markers separating the two registers, and fine-tune three models: BART-base, BART-large, and Mistral-7B-Instruct with QLoRA. BART-large achieves the highest reference similarity -- BERTScore F1 of 0.924, ROUGE-L of 0.566, and chrF++ of 55.92 -- with 17x fewer parameters than Mistral-7B. We show that Mistral-7B's higher marker shift score reflects overshoot rather than accuracy, and argue that shift accuracy is a meaningful blind spot in current style transfer evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：请使其听起来像人类：AI到人类文本风格迁移中的编码器-解码器与仅解码器Transformer的比较
AI生成文本在学术和专业写作中已变得普遍，促使研究者探索检测方法。较少被研究的是其逆向过程：系统性地改写AI生成的散文，使其读起来像真正的人类创作。我们构建了一个包含25,140对AI输入与人类参考文本片段的平行语料库，识别出11个可测量的风格标记，区分两种文体，并对三个模型（BART-base、BART-large和Mistral-7B-Instruct）进行QLoRA微调。BART-large在参考相似性上表现最佳——BERTScore F1为0.924，ROUGE-L为0.566，chrF++为55.92——其参数量仅为Mistral-7B的1/17。我们表明，Mistral-7B更高的标记偏移得分反映的是过度调整而非准确性，并认为偏移准确性是当前风格迁移评估中的一个有意义的盲点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make AI-generated text appear more human-like, this study investigates style transfer methods using encoder-decoder and decoder-only transformers. The researchers constructed a parallel corpus of 25,140 AI-generated-to-human-reference text pairs and identified 11 stylistic markers distinguishing the two. They fine-tuned BART-base, BART-large, and Mistral-7B-Instruct with QLoRA, finding that BART-large achieved the highest similarity to human text (BERTScore F1 0.924, ROUGE-L 0.566, chrF++ 55.92) while using 17x fewer parameters than Mistral-7B. Crucially, they observed that Mistral-7B’s higher stylistic shift score was due to overshooting rather than improved accuracy, highlighting a gap in current evaluation metrics.
为使AI生成文本在学术或专业写作中更像人类撰写，本研究探索了编码器-解码器与解码器-only Transformer的风格迁移方法。研究者构建了25,140对AI生成文本与人类参考文本的平行语料库，并识别出11个区分两类文本的风格标记。他们对BART-base、BART-large和Mistral-7B-Instruct进行QLoRA微调，发现BART-large在人类文本相似度上表现最优（BERTScore F1 0.924，ROUGE-L 0.566，chrF++ 55.92），参数量仅为Mistral-7B的1/17。重要的是，研究指出Mistral-7B更高的风格迁移得分反映的是过度调整而非准确度提升，揭示当前评估体系中对“风格迁移准确性”这一盲点的忽视。
Unfolding 3D Gaussian Splatting via Iterative Gaussian Synopsis
Authors: Yuqin Lu, Yang Zhou, Yihua Dai, Guiqing Li, Shengfeng He
First: 2026-04-13T16:28:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:28:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a state-of-the-art framework for real-time, high-fidelity novel view synthesis. However, its substantial storage requirements and inherently unstructured representation pose challenges for deployment in streaming and resource-constrained environments. Existing Level-of-Detail (LOD) strategies, particularly those based on bottom-up construction, often introduce redundancy or lead to fidelity degradation. To overcome these limitations, we propose Iterative Gaussian Synopsis, a novel framework for compact and progressive rendering through a top-down "unfolding" scheme. Our approach begins with a full-resolution 3DGS model and iteratively derives coarser LODs using an adaptive, learnable mask-based pruning mechanism. This process constructs a multi-level hierarchy that preserves visual quality while improving efficiency. We integrate hierarchical spatial grids, which capture the global scene structure, with a shared Anchor Codebook that models localized details. This combination produces a compact yet expressive feature representation, designed to minimize redundancy and support efficient, level-specific adaptation. The unfolding mechanism promotes inter-layer reusability and requires only minimal data overhead for progressive refinement. Experiments show that our method maintains high rendering quality across all LODs while achieving substantial storage reduction. These results demonstrate the practicality and scalability of our approach for real-time 3DGS rendering in bandwidth- and memory-constrained scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过迭代高斯概要实现3D高斯点绘的展开
3D高斯点绘（3DGS）已成为实时、高保真新视角合成的前沿框架。然而，其庞大的存储需求和固有的非结构化表示对流媒体和资源受限环境的部署构成挑战。现有细节层次（LOD）策略，特别是基于自下而上构建的方法，常引入冗余或导致保真度下降。为克服这些限制，我们提出迭代高斯概要，一种通过自上而下“展开”方案实现紧凑且渐进式渲染的新型框架。我们的方法从全分辨率3DGS模型开始，通过自适应、可学习的掩码剪枝机制迭代推导更粗略的LOD。该过程构建多层级结构，在保持视觉质量的同时提升效率。我们结合层次化空间网格（捕捉全局场景结构）与共享锚点码本（建模局部细节），生成紧凑且表达力强的特征表示，旨在减少冗余并支持高效、层级特定的自适应。展开机制促进层间复用，仅需极小数据开销即可实现渐进式优化。实验表明，我们的方法在所有LOD层级均保持高质量渲染，同时实现显著存储缩减。结果证明，该方法在带宽和内存受限场景下具备实用性和可扩展性，适用于实时3DGS渲染。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage demands and unstructured nature of 3D Gaussian Splatting, this work introduces Iterative Gaussian Synopsis, a top-down unfolding method that progressively generates compact Level-of-Detail representations. Starting from a full-resolution 3DGS model, it applies adaptive mask-based pruning to derive coarser levels, while preserving visual fidelity through hierarchical spatial grids and a shared Anchor Codebook that jointly encode global structure and local detail. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach achieves significant storage reduction without compromising rendering quality across all LODs, making it suitable for real-time deployment in memory- and bandwidth-constrained environments.
为解决3D高斯点云存储开销大、结构不规则的问题，本文提出迭代高斯概要（Iterative Gaussian Synopsis）方法，通过自顶向下展开策略，利用可学习掩码动态裁剪高斯点，逐层生成粗化层级。该方法结合层次化空间网格捕捉全局结构与共享锚点码本建模局部细节，实现紧凑且可复用的特征表示。实验表明，该方法在大幅降低存储开销的同时，各层级渲染质量保持高位，适用于内存与带宽受限场景下的实时渲染。
Dual-Control Frequency-Aware Diffusion Model for Depth-Dependent Optical Microrobot Microscopy Image Generation
Authors: Lan Wei, Zongcai Tan, Kangyi Lu, Jian-Qing Zheng, Dandan Zhang
First: 2026-04-13T16:24:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:24:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optical microrobots actuated by optical tweezers (OT) are important for cell manipulation and microscale assembly, but their autonomous operation depends on accurate 3D perception. Developing such perception systems is challenging because large-scale, high-quality microscopy datasets are scarce, owing to complex fabrication processes and labor-intensive annotation. Although generative AI offers a promising route for data augmentation, existing generative adversarial network (GAN)-based methods struggle to reproduce key optical characteristics, particularly depth-dependent diffraction and defocus effects. To address this limitation, we propose Du-FreqNet, a dual-control, frequency-aware diffusion model for physically consistent microscopy image synthesis. The framework features two independent ControlNet branches to encode microrobot 3D point clouds and depth-specific mesh layers, respectively. We introduce an adaptive frequency-domain loss that dynamically reweights high- and low-frequency components based on the distance to the focal plane. By leveraging differentiable FFT-based supervision, Du-FreqNet captures physically meaningful frequency distributions often missed by pixel-space methods. Trained on a limited dataset (e.g., 80 images per pose), our model achieves controllable, depth-dependent image synthesis, improving SSIM by 20.7% over baselines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Du-FreqNet generalizes effectively to unseen poses and significantly enhances downstream tasks, including 3D pose and depth estimation, thereby facilitating robust closed-loop control in microrobotic systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向深度依赖光学微机器人显微图像生成的双控频域扩散模型
由光镊驱动的光学微机器人在细胞操作和微尺度组装中至关重要，但其自主运行依赖于精确的三维感知。开发此类感知系统具有挑战性，因为大规模高质量显微图像数据集稀缺，这归因于复杂的制造工艺和耗时的标注。尽管生成式人工智能为数据增强提供了有前景的途径，但现有的基于生成对抗网络（GAN）的方法难以再现关键的光学特性，特别是与深度相关的衍射和失焦效应。为解决这一局限，我们提出Du-FreqNet，一种面向物理一致显微图像合成的双控频域扩散模型。该框架包含两个独立的ControlNet分支，分别编码微机器人的三维点云和深度特定的网格层。我们引入一种自适应频域损失，根据与焦平面的距离动态调整高频和低频成分的权重。通过可微分FFT监督，Du-FreqNet捕捉到像素空间方法常忽略的物理意义频域分布。在有限数据集（如每姿态80张图像）上训练后，我们的模型实现了可控的深度依赖图像合成，较基线方法SSIM提升20.7%。大量实验表明，Du-FreqNet能有效泛化至未见姿态，并显著增强下游任务（如三维姿态与深度估计），从而促进微机器人系统的鲁棒闭环控制。
Summary / 总结
To enable autonomous operation of optical microrobots, accurate 3D perception from microscopy images is essential but hindered by scarce, high-quality datasets. Du-FreqNet, a dual-control, frequency-aware diffusion model, addresses this by using two ControlNet branches to encode 3D microrobot geometry and depth-specific mesh layers, while an adaptive frequency-domain loss dynamically weights high- and low-frequency components based on depth relative to the focal plane. Trained on a small dataset (80 images per pose), it generates physically consistent images that capture depth-dependent optical effects missed by pixel-space methods, improving SSIM by 20.7% over baselines and enhancing downstream 3D pose and depth estimation, thus supporting robust closed-loop microrobotic control.
为实现光学微机器人自主操作所需的精确三维感知，现有高质显微图像数据稀缺，Du-FreqNet提出双控制、频域感知扩散模型，通过两个ControlNet分支分别编码微机器人3D点云与深度相关网格层，并引入自适应频域损失动态调整高频与低频成分权重。在仅80张图像/姿态的小数据集上训练，该模型生成具有物理一致性的深度依赖图像，SSIM较基线提升20.7%，显著增强下游3D姿态与深度估计，助力微机器人闭环控制系统的鲁棒性。
Towards Brain MRI Foundation Models for the Clinic: Findings from the FOMO25 Challenge
Authors: Asbjørn Munk, Stefano Cerri, Vardan Nersesjan, Christian Hedeager Krag, Jakob Ambsdorf, Pablo Rocamora García, Julia Machnio, Peirong Liu, Suhyun Ahn, Nasrin Akbari, Yasmina Al Khalil, Kimberly Amador, Sina Amirrajab, Tal Arbel, Meritxell Bach Cuadra, Ujjwal Baid, Bhakti Baheti, Jaume Banus, Kamil Barbierik, Christoph Brune, Yansong Bu, Baptiste Callard, Yuhan Chen, Cornelius Crijnen, Corentin Dancette, Peter Drotar, Prasad Dutande, Nils D. Gordaliza, Sam Hashemi, Tobias Heimann, Fucang Jia, Jiexin Jiang, Emily Kaczmarek, Chris Kang, Seung Kwan Kang, Mohammad Khazaei, Julien Khlaut, Petros Koutsouvelis, Jae Sung Lee, Yuchong Li, Mengye Lyu, Mingchen Ma, Anant Madabhushi, Klaus H. Maier-Hein, Pierre Manceron, Andrés Martínez Mora, Moona Mazher, Felix Meister, Nataliia Molchanova, Steven A. Niederer, Leonard Nürnberg, Jinah Park, Abdul Qayyum, Jonas Richiardi, Antoine Saporta, Branislav Setlak, Ning Shen, Justin Szeto, Constantin Ulrich, Puru Vaish, Vibujithan Vigneshwaran, Leroy Volmer, Zihao Wang, Siqi Wei, Anthony Winder, Jelmer M. Wolterink, Maxence Wynen, Chang Yang, Si Young Yie, Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi, Akshay Pai, Espen Jimenez Solem, Sebastian Nørgaard Llambias, Mikael Boesen, Michael Eriksen Benros, Juan Eugenio Iglesias, Mads Nielsen
First: 2026-04-13T16:23:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:23:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical deployment of automated brain MRI analysis faces a fundamental challenge: clinical data is heterogeneous and noisy, and high-quality labels are prohibitively costly to obtain. Self-supervised learning (SSL) can address this by leveraging the vast amounts of unlabeled data produced in clinical workflows to train robust \textit{foundation models} that adapt out-of-domain with minimal supervision. However, the development of foundation models for brain MRI has been limited by small pretraining datasets and in-domain benchmarking focused on high-quality, research-grade data. To address this gap, we organized the FOMO25 challenge as a satellite event at MICCAI 2025. FOMO25 provided participants with a large pretraining dataset, FOMO60K, and evaluated models on data sourced directly from clinical workflows in few-shot and out-of-domain settings. Tasks covered infarct classification, meningioma segmentation, and brain age regression, and considered both models trained on FOMO60K (method track) and any data (open track). Nineteen foundation models from sixteen teams were evaluated using a standardized containerized pipeline. Results show that (a) self-supervised pretraining improves generalization on clinical data under domain shift, with the strongest models trained \textit{out-of-domain} surpassing supervised baselines trained \textit{in-domain}. (b) No single pretraining objective benefits all tasks: MAE favors segmentation, hybrid reconstruction-contrastive objectives favor classification, and (c) strong performance was achieved by small pretrained models, and improvements from scaling model size and training duration did not yield reliable benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向临床的脑部MRI基础模型：FOMO25挑战赛成果
自动化脑部MRI分析在临床部署中面临根本性挑战：临床数据异质且噪声大，高质量标注获取成本极高。自监督学习（SSL）可通过利用临床工作流中大量未标注数据，训练出鲁棒的\textit{基础模型}，使其在少量监督下实现跨域适应。然而，脑部MRI基础模型的发展受限于小规模预训练数据集及聚焦高质量研究级数据的域内基准测试。为弥合此差距，我们在MICCAI 2025会议期间组织了FOMO25挑战赛。FOMO25为参赛者提供大规模预训练数据集FOMO60K，并在临床工作流真实数据上进行少样本和跨域评估。任务涵盖脑梗死分类、脑膜瘤分割和脑龄回归，同时评估基于FOMO60K训练的模型（方法赛道）和任何数据训练的模型（开放赛道）。十六支团队提交的十九个基础模型通过标准化容器化流程进行评估。结果表明：(a) 自监督预训练在域偏移下显著提升临床数据泛化能力，最强模型在\textit{跨域}训练下超越\textit{域内}训练的监督基线；(b) 无单一预训练目标适用于所有任务：MAE更利于分割，混合重建-对比目标更利于分类；(c) 小型预训练模型已取得强性能，扩大模型规模和训练时长并未带来稳定收益。

Motivated by the challenge of deploying automated brain MRI analysis in clinical settings due to data heterogeneity and label scarcity, the paper introduces the FOMO25 challenge to evaluate self-supervised foundation models trained on a large unlabeled dataset (FOMO60K) and tested on real clinical data under few-shot and out-of-domain conditions. 为应对临床环境中脑MRI自动化分析因数据异质性和标注成本高昂而面临的挑战，本研究采用自监督学习方法，利用大量未标注数据训练基础模型。FOMO25挑战赛提供FOMO60K预训练数据集，并在真实临床数据上评估模型在少样本和跨域场景下的表现，涵盖梗死分类、脑膜瘤分割和脑龄回归等任务。结果表明，自监督预训练能提升跨域泛化能力，跨域训练模型优于域内监督基线；不同任务对预训练目标需求不同——MAE适合分割，混合重建-对比目标更适合分类；且小规模模型已能取得优异表现，扩大模型规模或训练时长并未带来稳定收益。
Training-Free Multi-User Generative Semantic Communications via Null-Space Diffusion Sampling
Authors: Eleonora Grassucci, Jinho Choi, Jihong Park, Riccardo F. Gramaccioni, Giordano Cicchetti, Danilo Comminiello
First: 2024-05-16T07:43:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:23:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted in IEEE Access
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, novel communication strategies have emerged to face the challenges that the increased number of connected devices and the higher quality of transmitted information are posing. Among them, semantic communication obtained promising results especially when combined with state-of-the-art deep generative models, such as large language or diffusion models, able to regenerate content from extremely compressed semantic information. However, most of these approaches focus on single-user scenarios processing the received content at the receiver on top of conventional communication systems. In this paper, we propose to go beyond these methods by developing a novel generative semantic communication framework tailored for multi-user scenarios. This system assigns the channel to users knowing that the lost information can be filled in with a diffusion model at the receivers. Under this innovative perspective, OFDMA systems should not aim to transmit the largest part of information, but solely the bits necessary to the generative model to semantically regenerate the missing ones. The thorough experimental evaluation shows the capabilities of the novel diffusion model and the effectiveness of the proposed framework, leading towards a GenAI-based next generation of communications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于零空间扩散采样的无训练多用户生成语义通信
近年来，为应对连接设备数量增加和传输信息质量提升带来的挑战，出现了新的通信策略。其中，语义通信在与最先进的深度生成模型（如大型语言模型或扩散模型）结合时表现尤为突出，这些模型能够从极压缩的语义信息中再生内容。然而，大多数现有方法聚焦于单用户场景，仅在传统通信系统基础上处理接收端内容。本文提出超越这些方法的新型生成语义通信框架，专为多用户场景设计。该系统将信道分配给用户，同时假设接收端可通过扩散模型填补丢失信息。在此创新视角下，OFDMA系统无需传输最大信息量，仅需传输生成模型再生缺失内容所必需的比特。全面的实验评估验证了新型扩散模型的能力及所提框架的有效性，推动了基于生成人工智能的下一代通信发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient communication in multi-user environments with high-quality semantic content, this paper introduces a training-free generative semantic communication framework using null-space diffusion sampling. The method leverages diffusion models at receivers to regenerate lost information, allowing OFDMA systems to transmit only minimal semantic bits rather than full data. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach significantly improves communication efficiency and fidelity without requiring training, validating its potential for GenAI-driven next-generation communications.
为应对多用户环境下高语义质量通信的挑战，本文提出一种无需训练的生成式语义通信框架，利用扩散模型在接收端重建丢失信息。该方法根据生成模型所需最小语义比特分配信道资源，将传输重点从原始数据转向关键语义线索。实验结果表明，该方法相较传统方法显著提升通信效率与语义保真度，验证了其在下一代生成式人工智能通信系统中的应用潜力。
AffordSim: A Scalable Data Generator and Benchmark for Affordance-Aware Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Mingyang Li, Haofan Xu, Haowen Sun, Xinzhe Chen, Sihua Ren, Liqi Huang, Xinyang Sui, Chenyang Miao, Qiongjie Cui, Zeyang Liu, Xingyu Chen, Xuguang Lan
First: 2026-04-13T16:21:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:21:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulation-based data generation has become a dominant paradigm for training robotic manipulation policies, yet existing platforms do not incorporate object affordance information into trajectory generation. As a result, tasks requiring precise interaction with specific functional regions--grasping a mug by its handle, pouring from a cup's rim, or hanging a mug on a hook--cannot be automatically generated with semantically correct trajectories. We introduce AffordSim, the first simulation framework that integrates open-vocabulary 3D affordance prediction into the manipulation data generation pipeline. AffordSim uses our VoxAfford model, an open-vocabulary 3D affordance detector that enhances MLLM output tokens with multi-scale geometric features, to predict affordance maps on object point clouds, guiding grasp pose estimation toward task-relevant functional regions. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim with cross-embodiment support (Franka FR3, Panda, UR5e, Kinova), VLM-powered task generation, and novel domain randomization using DA3-based 3D Gaussian reconstruction from real photographs, AffordSim enables automated, scalable generation of affordance-aware manipulation data. We establish a benchmark of 50 tasks across 7 categories (grasping, placing, stacking, pushing/pulling, pouring, mug hanging, long-horizon composite) and evaluate 4 imitation learning baselines (BC, Diffusion Policy, ACT, Pi 0.5). Our results reveal that while grasping is largely solved (53-93% success), affordance-demanding tasks such as pouring into narrow containers (1-43%) and mug hanging (0-47%) remain significantly more challenging for current imitation learning methods, highlighting the need for affordance-aware data generation. Zero-shot sim-to-real experiments on a real Franka FR3 validate the transferability of the generated data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AffordSim：面向感知功能区域的机器人操作可扩展数据生成与基准
基于仿真的数据生成已成为训练机器人操作策略的主流范式，但现有平台在轨迹生成中未整合物体功能区域信息。因此，需要精确交互特定功能区域的任务——如握住杯子把手、从杯口倾倒、将杯子挂在钩子上——无法自动生成语义正确的轨迹。我们提出了AffordSim，首个将开放词汇3D功能区域预测集成到操作数据生成流程中的仿真框架。AffordSim采用VoxAfford模型，该模型是一种开放词汇3D功能区域检测器，通过多尺度几何特征增强MLLM输出标记，预测物体点云上的功能区域图，引导抓取位姿估计朝向任务相关功能区域。基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建，支持跨具身（Franka FR3、Panda、UR5e、Kinova），结合VLM驱动的任务生成，以及基于DA3的3D高斯重建从真实照片实现新颖域随机化，AffordSim可实现自动化、可扩展的功能区域感知操作数据生成。我们建立了涵盖7类（抓取、放置、堆叠、推/拉、倾倒、挂杯、长时复合）共50项任务的基准，并评估4种模仿学习基线（BC、扩散策略、ACT、Pi 0.5）。结果表明，抓取任务已基本解决（成功率53-93%），而对功能区域依赖性强的任务如倾倒至窄容器（1-43%）和挂杯（0-47%）仍显著更具挑战性，凸显了功能区域感知数据生成的必要性。在真实Franka FR3机器人上的零样本仿真到现实实验验证了生成数据的可迁移性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of semantically grounded trajectory generation in robotic manipulation simulation, AffordSim introduces a scalable framework that integrates open-vocabulary 3D affordance prediction into data generation. It leverages the VoxAfford model to predict functional regions on object point clouds, guiding grasp and manipulation poses toward task-relevant areas. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim with support for multiple robotic arms and VLM-driven task generation, it employs DA3-based domain randomization for realistic data synthesis. Evaluated across 50 tasks in seven categories using four imitation learning baselines, AffordSim shows high success in grasping (53–93%) but reveals significant performance gaps in affordance-sensitive tasks like pouring (1–43%) and mug hanging (0–47%), underscoring the need for affordance-aware training data. 为解决机器人操作仿真中缺乏语义引导轨迹生成的问题，AffordSim首次将开放词汇3D功能区域预测集成到数据生成流程中。它利用VoxAfford模型预测物体点云上的功能区域，引导抓取与操作姿态朝向任务相关区域。该框架基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim，支持多种机械臂、VLM驱动的任务生成，并采用DA3基于的3D高斯重建实现域随机化。在7类共50项任务上评估4种模仿学习基线，结果显示抓取任务成功率高（53-93%），但对功能区域敏感的任务如倒液（1-43%）和挂杯（0-47%）表现显著不足，凸显了功能区域感知数据的重要性。在真实Franka FR3机器人上的零样本仿真到现实实验验证了生成数据的可迁移性。
MSTN: A Lightweight and Fast Model for General TimeSeries Analysis
Authors: Sumit S Shevtekar, Chandresh K Maurya
First: 2025-11-25T18:09:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:21:07+00:00
Comments: 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world time series often exhibit strong non-stationarity, complex nonlinear dynamics, and behavior expressed across multiple temporal scales, from rapid local fluctuations to slow-evolving long-range trends. However, many contemporary architectures impose rigid, fixed-scale structural priors -- such as patch-based tokenization, predefined receptive fields, or frozen backbone encoders -- which can over-regularize temporal dynamics and limit adaptability to abrupt high-magnitude events. To handle this, we introduce the Multi-scale Temporal Network (MSTN), a hybrid neural architecture grounded in an Early Temporal Aggregation principle. MSTN integrates three complementary components: (i) a multi-scale convolutional encoder that captures fine-grained local structure; (ii) a sequence modeling module that learns long-range dependencies through either recurrent or attention-based mechanisms; and (iii) a self-gated fusion stage incorporating squeeze-excitation and a single dense layer to dynamically reweight and fuse multi-scale representations. This design enables MSTN to flexibly model temporal patterns spanning milliseconds to extended horizons, while avoiding the computational burden typically associated with long-context models. Across extensive benchmarks covering imputation, long term forecasting, short term forecasting, classification, and cross-dataset generalization, MSTN achieves state-of-the-art performance, establishing new best results on 33 of 40 datasets, while remaining lightweight ($\sim$278,520 params for MSTN-BiLSTM and $\sim$950,776 $\approx$ 1M for MSTN-Transformer) and suitable for low-latency inference ($<$1 sec, often in milliseconds), resource-constrained deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MSTN：一种轻量且快速的通用时间序列分析模型
现实世界的时间序列常表现出强非平稳性、复杂的非线性动态，以及跨越多个时间尺度的行为，从快速局部波动到缓慢演化的长程趋势。然而，许多现代架构施加了刚性、固定尺度的结构先验——如基于块的标记化、预定义感受野或冻结的骨干编码器——这可能过度正则化时间动态并限制对突发高幅事件的适应性。为解决此问题，我们提出了多尺度时序网络（MSTN），一种基于早期时序聚合原则的混合神经架构。MSTN整合了三个互补组件：(i) 多尺度卷积编码器，捕捉细粒度局部结构；(ii) 序列建模模块，通过循环或注意力机制学习长程依赖；(iii) 自门控融合阶段，结合挤压激励与单层稠密层，动态重加权并融合多尺度表示。该设计使MSTN能够灵活建模从毫秒到长时程的时间模式，同时避免了长上下文模型通常伴随的计算负担。在涵盖缺失值填补、长期预测、短期预测、分类及跨数据集泛化等广泛基准测试中，MSTN取得最先进性能，在40个数据集中33个创下新最佳结果，同时保持轻量（MSTN-BiLSTM约278,520参数，MSTN-Transformer约950,776≈1M参数）且适合低延迟推理（<1秒，常为毫秒级）及资源受限部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle non-stationary, multi-scale time series without rigid structural priors, MSTN introduces a hybrid architecture based on Early Temporal Aggregation. It combines a multi-scale convolutional encoder for fine-grained local patterns, a sequence modeling module (either recurrent or attention-based) for long-range dependencies, and a self-gated fusion stage that dynamically reweights features. Experiments across 40 datasets show MSTN achieves state-of-the-art results on 33 of them, while remaining lightweight (278K–1M parameters) and fast (<1 second inference), making it suitable for real-time and resource-constrained applications.
为应对现实世界中非平稳、多尺度时间序列数据，MSTN提出一种基于早期时序聚合的混合架构。该架构融合多尺度卷积编码器捕捉局部结构、序列建模模块（循环或注意力机制）学习长程依赖，以及自门控融合层动态整合多尺度特征。在40个数据集的广泛实验中，MSTN在其中33个上达到最先进性能，同时保持轻量（27.8万至100万参数）和快速（<1秒推理），适用于实时与资源受限场景。
NetworkNet: A Deep Neural Network Approach for Random Networks with Sparse Nodal Attributes and Complex Nodal Heterogeneity
Authors: Zhaoyu Xing, Xiufan Yu
First: 2026-04-13T16:19:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:19:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Heterogeneous network data with rich nodal information become increasingly prevalent across multidisciplinary research, yet accurately modeling complex nodal heterogeneity and simultaneously selecting influential nodal attributes remains an open challenge. This problem is central to many applications in economics and sociology, when both nodal heterogeneity and high-dimensional individual characteristics highly affect network formation. We propose a statistically grounded, unified deep neural network approach for modeling nodal heterogeneity in random networks with high-dimensional nodal attributes, namely ``NetworkNet''. A key innovation of NetworkNet lies in a tailored neural architecture that explicitly parameterizes attribute-driven heterogeneity, and at the same time, embeds a scalable attribute selection mechanism. NetworkNet consistently estimates two types of latent heterogeneity functions, i.e., nodal expansiveness and popularity, while simultaneously performing data-driven attribute selection to extract influential nodal attributes. By unifying classical statistical network modeling with deep learning, NetworkNet delivers the expressive power of DNNs with methodological interpretability, algorithmic scalability, and statistical rigor with a non-asymptotic approximation error bound. Empirically, simulations demonstrate strong performance in both heterogeneity estimation and high-dimensional attribute selection. We further apply NetworkNet to a large-scale author-citation network among statisticians, revealing new insights into the dynamic evolution of research fields and scholarly impact.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NetworkNet：用于具有稀疏节点属性和复杂节点异质性的随机网络的深度神经网络方法
随着多学科研究中异质网络数据及其丰富的节点信息日益普及，准确建模复杂的节点异质性并同时选择有影响力的节点属性仍是一个开放性挑战。这一问题在经济学和社会学的许多应用中至关重要，因为节点异质性和高维个体特征高度影响网络形成。我们提出了一种基于统计学的统一深度神经网络方法——“NetworkNet”，用于建模具有高维节点属性的随机网络中的节点异质性。NetworkNet 的关键创新在于一种定制化的神经架构，该架构明确参数化属性驱动的异质性，同时嵌入可扩展的属性选择机制。NetworkNet 一致估计两种类型的潜在异质性函数，即节点扩展性和流行度，同时进行数据驱动的属性选择以提取有影响力的节点属性。通过将经典统计网络建模与深度学习统一，NetworkNet 在保持方法可解释性、算法可扩展性和统计严谨性的同时，具备深度神经网络的表达能力，并具有非渐近近似误差界。实证研究表明，模拟实验在异质性估计和高维属性选择方面表现优异。我们进一步将 NetworkNet 应用于统计学家之间的大规模作者引文网络，揭示了研究领域动态演化和学术影响力的新见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model complex nodal heterogeneity and select influential attributes in random networks with sparse, high-dimensional node data, the authors propose NetworkNet, a deep neural network approach that explicitly parameterizes attribute-driven heterogeneity while embedding scalable attribute selection. The method estimates two latent heterogeneity functions—nodal expansiveness and popularity—simultaneously with attribute selection, unifying statistical network modeling with deep learning. Empirical results from simulations show strong performance in both heterogeneity estimation and attribute selection, and application to a large-scale author-citation network reveals insights into research field evolution and scholarly impact.
为应对随机网络中稀疏节点属性与复杂节点异质性建模及关键属性选择的挑战，NetworkNet提出一种深度神经网络框架，显式参数化属性驱动的异质性并嵌入可扩展的属性选择机制。该方法同时估计节点扩展性与流行度两种潜在函数，并进行数据驱动的属性筛选。仿真结果表明其在异质性估计与高维属性选择上表现优异，应用于统计学家引文网络后，揭示了研究领域演化与学术影响力的新洞见。
FPBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Multimodal Large Language Models for Fingerprint Analysis
Authors: Ekta Gavas, Sudipta Banerjee, Chinmay Hegde, Nasir Memon
First: 2025-12-19T21:23:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:17:11+00:00
Comments: Revised version with additional experiments and code release
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) are capable of performing complex data analysis, visual question answering, generation, and reasoning tasks. However, their ability to analyze biometric data is relatively underexplored. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of MLLMs in understanding fine structural and textural details present in fingerprint images. To this end, we design a comprehensive benchmark, FPBench, to evaluate 20 MLLMs (open-source and proprietary models) across 7 real and synthetic datasets on a suite of 8 biometric and forensic tasks (e.g., pattern analysis, fingerprint verification, real versus synthetic classification, etc.) using zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting strategies. We further fine-tune vision and language encoders on a subset of open-source MLLMs to demonstrate domain adaptation. FPBench is a novel benchmark designed as a first step towards developing foundation models in fingerprints. Our findings indicate fine-tuning of vision and language encoders improves the performance by 7%-39%. Our codes are available at https://github.com/Ektagavas/FPBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FPBench：用于指纹分析的多模态大语言模型综合基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够执行复杂的数据分析、视觉问答、生成和推理任务。然而，它们对生物识别数据的分析能力尚待探索。本研究探讨了MLLMs在理解指纹图像中精细结构和纹理细节方面的有效性。为此，我们设计了综合基准FPBench，评估20个MLLMs（开源与专有模型）在7个真实与合成数据集上，针对8项生物识别与法证任务（如模式分析、指纹验证、真实与合成分类等）的表现，采用零样本和思维链提示策略。我们进一步在部分开源MLLMs上微调视觉与语言编码器，以展示领域自适应能力。FPBench是首个面向指纹领域基础模型开发的基准。我们的研究发现，微调视觉与语言编码器可提升性能7%-39%。代码已发布于https://github.com/Ektagavas/FPBench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored potential of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in biometric analysis, this study introduces FPBench, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating 20 MLLMs across 7 real and synthetic fingerprint datasets using 8 forensic tasks. The evaluation employs zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting, and further experiments fine-tune vision and language encoders on open-source models to demonstrate domain adaptation. Results show that such fine-tuning improves performance by 7% to 39% across tasks, highlighting the effectiveness of targeted adaptation for fingerprint analysis.
为探索多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在指纹分析中的潜力，本研究构建了FPBench基准，对20个开源与商业模型在7组真实与合成指纹数据集上进行8项法医任务评估，采用零样本与思维链提示策略，并对部分开源模型进行视觉与语言编码器微调以实现领域适配。实验表明，微调可使模型性能提升7%至39%，验证了针对性适配对指纹分析的有效性。
VLMaterial: Vision-Language Model-Based Camera-Radar Fusion for Physics-Grounded Material Identification
Authors: Jiangyou Zhu, He Chen
First: 2026-04-13T16:16:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:16:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate material recognition is a fundamental capability for intelligent perception systems to interact safely and effectively with the physical world. For instance, distinguishing visually similar objects like glass and plastic cups is critical for safety but challenging for vision-based methods due to specular reflections, transparency, and visual deception. While millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers robust material sensing regardless of lighting, existing camera-radar fusion methods are limited to closed-set categories and lack semantic interpretability. In this paper, we introduce VLMaterial, a training-free framework that fuses vision-language models (VLMs) with domain-specific radar knowledge for physics-grounded material identification. First, we propose a dual-pipeline architecture: an optical pipeline uses the segment anything model and VLM for material candidate proposals, while an electromagnetic characterization pipeline extracts the intrinsic dielectric constant from radar signals via an effective peak reflection cell area (PRCA) method and weighted vector synthesis. Second, we employ a context-augmented generation (CAG) strategy to equip the VLM with radar-specific physical knowledge, enabling it to interpret electromagnetic parameters as stable references. Third, an adaptive fusion mechanism is introduced to intelligently integrate outputs from both sensors by resolving cross-modal conflicts based on uncertainty estimation. We evaluated VLMaterial in over 120 real-world experiments involving 41 diverse everyday objects and 4 typical visually deceptive counterfeits across varying environments. Experimental results demonstrate that VLMaterial achieves a recognition accuracy of 96.08%, delivering performance on par with state-of-the-art closed-set benchmarks while eliminating the need for extensive task-specific data collection and training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLMaterial：基于视觉-语言模型的相机-雷达融合物理接地材料识别
准确的材料识别是智能感知系统安全有效与物理世界交互的基础能力。例如，区分视觉上相似的物体（如玻璃杯和塑料杯）对安全至关重要，但基于视觉的方法因镜面反射、透明性和视觉欺骗而面临挑战。虽然毫米波（mmWave）雷达在任何光照条件下均能提供稳健的材料感知，但现有相机-雷达融合方法仅限于封闭集类别且缺乏语义可解释性。本文提出VLMaterial，一种无需训练的框架，通过融合视觉-语言模型（VLM）与领域特定的雷达知识实现物理接地的材料识别。首先，我们提出双管道架构：光学管道利用Segment Anything模型和VLM生成材料候选；电磁表征管道通过有效峰值反射单元面积（PRCA）方法和加权向量合成从雷达信号中提取介电常数。其次，我们采用上下文增强生成（CAG）策略，使VLM具备雷达特定的物理知识，从而将电磁参数作为稳定参考进行解释。第三，引入自适应融合机制，通过不确定性估计智能整合双传感器输出，解决跨模态冲突。我们在超过120次真实世界实验中评估VLMaterial，涵盖41种日常物体和4种视觉欺骗性仿制品，环境各异。实验结果表明，VLMaterial识别准确率达96.08%，性能媲美当前最先进的封闭集基准，同时无需大量任务特定数据收集与训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately identifying visually similar materials like glass and plastic using only vision, which is hindered by reflections and transparency, and by the limitations of existing camera-radar fusion methods that rely on closed-set categories and lack semantic grounding, VLMaterial introduces a training-free framework that combines vision-language models with radar-based electromagnetic properties. It employs a dual-pipeline architecture: one using a segment anything model and VLM to propose material candidates from visual input, and another extracting dielectric constants from radar signals via a peak reflection cell area method and weighted synthesis. A context-augmented generation strategy enriches the VLM with radar physics knowledge, enabling it to interpret electromagnetic parameters as stable references, while an adaptive fusion mechanism resolves cross-modal conflicts based on uncertainty. Evaluated across 120 real-world experiments with 41 diverse objects and 4 deceptive counterfeits, VLMaterial achieved 96.08% accuracy, matching state-of-the-art closed-set performance without requiring task-specific training data.
为解决仅依赖视觉识别材料时因反光、透明性导致的视觉欺骗问题，同时克服现有相机-雷达融合方法仅支持封闭类别且缺乏语义可解释性的局限，VLMaterial提出一种无需训练的框架，结合视觉语言模型与雷达电磁特性。该方法采用双管道架构：视觉管道利用分割任何模型和VLM生成材料候选，电磁管道通过PRCA方法与加权合成提取雷达信号中的介电常数。通过上下文增强生成策略，使VLM能将电磁参数作为稳定参考，结合不确定性估计的自适应融合机制解决跨模态冲突。在120次真实世界实验中，涵盖41种日常物品与4种视觉欺骗仿制品，VLMaterial达到96.08%识别准确率，媲美当前最先进封闭集基准，且无需特定任务训练数据。
UNIGEOCLIP: Unified Geospatial Contrastive Learning
Authors: Guillaume Astruc, Eduard Trulls, Jan Hosang, Loic Landrieu, Paul-Edouard Sarlin
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-13T16:14:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:14:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The growing availability of co-located geospatial data spanning aerial imagery, street-level views, elevation models, text, and geographic coordinates offers a unique opportunity for multimodal representation learning. We introduce UNIGEOCLIP, a massively multimodal contrastive framework to jointly align five complementary geospatial modalities in a single unified embedding space. Unlike prior approaches that fuse modalities or rely on a central pivot representation, our method performs all-to-all contrastive alignment, enabling seamless comparison, retrieval, and reasoning across arbitrary combinations of modalities. We further propose a scaled latitude-longitude encoder that improves spatial representation by capturing multi-scale geographic structure. Extensive experiments across downstream geospatial tasks demonstrate that UNIGEOCLIP consistently outperforms single-modality contrastive models and coordinate-only baselines, highlighting the benefits of holistic multimodal geospatial alignment. A reference implementation is available at https://gastruc.github.io/unigeoclip.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UNIGEOCLIP：统一地理空间对比学习
随着航拍影像、街景视图、高程模型、文本和地理坐标等共存地理空间数据的日益丰富，为多模态表征学习提供了独特机遇。我们提出UNIGEOCLIP，一种大规模多模态对比框架，可在单一统一嵌入空间中联合对齐五种互补的地理空间模态。与以往融合模态或依赖中心枢纽表征的方法不同，我们的方法执行全对全对比对齐，支持任意模态组合间的无缝比较、检索与推理。我们进一步提出一种缩放经纬度编码器，通过捕捉多尺度地理结构提升空间表征能力。在多个下游地理空间任务上的广泛实验表明，UNIGEOCLIP持续优于单模态对比模型和仅坐标基线方法，凸显了整体多模态地理空间对齐的优势。参考实现见https://gastruc.github.io/unigeoclip。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the increasing availability of diverse geospatial data types, UNIGEOCLIP introduces a unified contrastive learning framework that aligns five modalities— aerial imagery, street views, elevation models, text, and coordinates—into a single embedding space. Instead of fusing modalities or using a central pivot, it employs all-to-all contrastive alignment to enable flexible cross-modal comparison and reasoning. The method also incorporates a scaled latitude-longitude encoder to enhance spatial representation through multi-scale geographic modeling. Experiments across multiple geospatial tasks show that UNIGEOCLIP consistently outperforms single-modality contrastive models and coordinate-only baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of holistic multimodal alignment.
鉴于多源地理空间数据日益丰富，UNIGEOCLIP提出一种统一对比学习框架，将航拍图像、街景、高程模型、文本与地理坐标五种模态对齐至单一嵌入空间。不同于传统融合或中心锚点方法，该框架采用全对全对比学习，支持任意模态组合的灵活比较与推理。其改进的经纬度编码器通过多尺度地理结构建模提升空间表征能力。在多个地理空间下游任务中，实验表明其性能持续优于单模态对比模型和仅坐标基线，验证了整体多模态对齐的有效性。
Playing Along: Learning a Double-Agent Defender for Belief Steering via Theory of Mind
Authors: Hanqi Xiao, Vaidehi Patil, Zaid Khan, Hyunji Lee, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Mohit Bansal
First: 2026-04-13T16:14:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:14:41+00:00
Comments: First two authors contributed equally. Code: https://github.com/The-Inscrutable-X/AIDoubleAgentDefenders
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) become the engine behind conversational systems, their ability to reason about the intentions and states of their dialogue partners (i.e., form and use a theory-of-mind, or ToM) becomes increasingly critical for safe interaction with potentially adversarial partners. We propose a novel privacy-themed ToM challenge, ToM for Steering Beliefs (ToM-SB), in which a defender must act as a Double Agent to steer the beliefs of an attacker with partial prior knowledge within a shared universe. To succeed on ToM-SB, the defender must engage with and form a ToM of the attacker, with a goal of fooling the attacker into believing they have succeeded in extracting sensitive information. We find that strong frontier models like Gemini3-Pro and GPT-5.4 struggle on ToM-SB, often failing to fool attackers in hard scenarios with partial attacker prior knowledge, even when prompted to reason about the attacker's beliefs (ToM prompting). To close this gap, we train models on ToM-SB to act as AI Double Agents using reinforcement learning, testing both fooling and ToM rewards. Notably, we find a bidirectionally emergent relationship between ToM and attacker-fooling: rewarding fooling success alone improves ToM, and rewarding ToM alone improves fooling. Across four attackers with different strengths, six defender methods, and both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation, we find that gains in ToM and attacker-fooling are well-correlated, highlighting belief modeling as a key driver of success on ToM-SB. AI Double Agents that combine both ToM and fooling rewards yield the strongest fooling and ToM performance, outperforming Gemini3-Pro and GPT-5.4 with ToM prompting on hard scenarios. We also show that ToM-SB and AI Double Agents can be extended to stronger attackers, demonstrating generalization to OOD settings and the upgradability of our task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协同参与：通过心智理论学习双面特工防御者以引导信念
随着大型语言模型（LLM）成为对话系统的引擎，其对对话伙伴意图和状态的推理能力（即形成并运用心智理论，ToM）对于与潜在对抗性伙伴安全交互变得日益关键。我们提出一个新颖的隐私主题ToM挑战——ToM用于信念引导（ToM-SB），其中防御者必须扮演双面特工，在共享宇宙中引导拥有部分先验知识的攻击者信念。要在ToM-SB中成功，防御者必须与攻击者互动并形成其心智理论，目标是使攻击者误以为已成功提取敏感信息。我们发现，即使是前沿模型如Gemini3-Pro和GPT-5.4在ToM-SB上也表现不佳，即使在提示其推理攻击者信念（ToM提示）的情况下，仍常无法在具有部分攻击者先验知识的困难场景中欺骗攻击者。为弥合这一差距，我们通过强化学习训练模型在ToM-SB上扮演AI双面特工，同时测试欺骗和ToM奖励。值得注意的是，我们发现ToM与攻击者欺骗之间存在双向涌现关系：仅奖励欺骗成功可提升ToM，仅奖励ToM亦可提升欺骗效果。在四种不同强度攻击者、六种防御方法以及分布内和分布外（OOD）评估中，我们发现ToM提升与攻击者欺骗能力提升高度相关，凸显信念建模是ToM-SB成功的关键驱动因素。结合ToM与欺骗奖励的AI双面特工模型在欺骗与ToM表现上均最强，优于在困难场景中经ToM提示的Gemini3-Pro和GPT-5.4。我们还表明，ToM-SB与AI双面特工可扩展至更强攻击者，证明其在OOD设置中的泛化能力及任务可升级性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large language models to reason about dialogue partners’ intentions for safe interaction, this work introduces ToM-SB—a privacy-focused challenge where a defender must act as a double agent to mislead an attacker by steering their beliefs. The method trains models via reinforcement learning using combined ToM and fooling rewards, revealing a bidirectional improvement between belief modeling and deception success. Experiments across multiple attacker types and evaluation settings show that models trained with both rewards outperform frontier models like Gemini3-Pro and GPT-5.4, especially in hard, partially informed scenarios, and demonstrate generalization to out-of-distribution attackers.
为提升大语言模型在对话中对对手意图的推理能力以保障安全交互，本文提出ToM-SB挑战，要求防御者扮演双面间谍，引导攻击者误以为已成功窃取敏感信息。方法通过强化学习结合ToM与欺骗奖励训练模型，发现二者存在双向促进关系。实验表明，在多种攻击者强度和防御方法下，联合奖励训练的模型在分布内与分布外场景均显著优于Gemini3-Pro和GPT-5.4，尤其在困难场景表现突出，证明信念建模是关键，且该方法可扩展至更强攻击者，具备泛化能力。
Beyond LLMs, Sparse Distributed Memory, and Neuromorphics <A Hyper-Dimensional SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl" for Ultra-High Speed, Ultra-Low Power, and Low Cost>
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-04-13T16:13:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:13:17+00:00
Comments: 55 pages, 4 figure, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports an unexpected finding: in a deterministic hyperdimensional computing (HDC) architecture based on Galois-field algebra, a path-dependent semantic selection mechanism emerges, equivalent to spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), with magnitude predictable a priori by a closed-form expression matching large-scale measurements. This addresses limitations of modern AI including catastrophic forgetting, learning stagnation, and the Binding Problem at an algebraic level. We propose VaCoAl (Vague Coincident Algorithm) and its Python implementation PyVaCoAl, combining ultra-high-dimensional memory with deterministic logic. Rooted in Sparse Distributed Memory, it resolves orthogonalisation and retrieval in high-dimensional binary spaces via Galois-field diffusion, enabling low-load deployment. VaCoAl is a memory-centric architecture prioritising retrieval and association, enabling reversible composition while preserving element independence and supporting compositional generalisation with a transparent reliability metric (CR score). We evaluated multi-hop reasoning on about 470k mentor-student relations from Wikidata, tracing up to 57 generations (over 25.5M paths). Using HDC bundling and unbinding with CR-based denoising, we quantify concept propagation over DAGs. Results show a reinterpretation of the Newton-Leibniz dispute and a phase transition from sparse convergence to a post-Leibniz "superhighway", from which structural indicators emerge supporting a Kuhnian paradigm shift. Collision-tolerance mechanisms further induce path-based pruning that favors direct paths, yielding emergent semantic selection equivalent to STDP. VaCoAl thus defines a third paradigm, HDC-AI, complementing LLMs with reversible multi-hop reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越大语言模型、稀疏分布式记忆与神经形态计算：一种用于超高速、超低功耗和低成本的高维SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl"
本文报告了一个意外发现：在基于伽罗瓦域代数的确定性高维计算（HDC）架构中，出现了一种路径依赖的语义选择机制，等价于突触可塑性（STDP），其幅度可通过与大规模测量结果匹配的闭式表达式预先预测。这在代数层面解决了现代AI的若干局限，包括灾难性遗忘、学习停滞和绑定问题。我们提出了VaCoAl（模糊同时算法）及其Python实现PyVaCoAl，结合超高维记忆与确定性逻辑。基于稀疏分布式记忆，它通过伽罗瓦域扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索问题，实现低负载部署。VaCoAl是一种以记忆为中心的架构，优先考虑检索与关联，支持可逆组合，同时保持元素独立性，并通过透明的可靠性度量（CR分数）支持组合泛化。我们在约47万条维基数据中的导师-学生关系上评估了多跳推理，追踪最多57代（超过2550万条路径）。通过HDC捆绑与解绑结合CR基去噪，我们量化了DAG上的概念传播。结果表明，对牛顿-莱布尼茨争议的重新诠释，并揭示了从稀疏收敛到“后莱布尼茨超级高速公路”的相变，其中涌现出支持库恩范式转变的结构指标。碰撞容忍机制进一步诱导路径剪枝，偏好直接路径，从而产生等价于STDP的语义选择。VaCoAl因此定义了第三种范式——HDC-AI，与大语言模型互补，支持可逆多跳推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations in modern AI such as catastrophic forgetting and the binding problem, this work introduces VaCoAl, a hyper-dimensional computing architecture based on Galois-field algebra and sparse distributed memory, enabling deterministic, reversible multi-hop reasoning. VaCoAl combines ultra-high-dimensional binary memory with algebraic diffusion to resolve orthogonality and retrieval, supporting compositional generalisation via a CR reliability metric. Evaluated on 470k Wikidata mentor-student relations across 57 generations (25.5M paths), it demonstrated emergent semantic selection matching spike-timing-dependent plasticity, with path-dependent pruning favoring direct routes. Experimental results revealed a phase transition from sparse convergence to a 'superhighway' state, suggesting structural shifts akin to a Kuhnian paradigm shift, while maintaining low power, high speed, and low cost.
为解决现代AI中的灾难性遗忘、学习停滞和绑定问题，本文提出VaCoAl架构，基于稀疏分布式记忆与伽罗瓦域代数，实现确定性、可逆且低负载的高维推理。该架构通过代数扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索问题，并借助CR分数可靠性指标支持组合泛化。在47万维基数据导师-学生关系上进行57代（2550万路径）多跳推理实验，结果展现出类似STDP的路径依赖语义选择机制，路径修剪机制偏好直接路径。实验揭示概念传播从稀疏收敛向“超级高速公路”状态的相变，暗示AI推理范式可能发生库恩意义上的范式转变。
Why Do Large Language Models Generate Harmful Content?
Authors: Rajesh Ganguli, Raha Moraffah
First: 2026-04-13T16:11:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:11:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to generate harmful content. However, the underlying causes of such behavior remain under explored. We propose a causal mediation analysis-based approach to identify the causal factors responsible for harmful generation. Our method performs a multi-granular analysis across model layers, modules (MLP and attention blocks), and individual neurons. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs indicate that harmful generation arises in the later layers of the model, results primarily from failures in MLP blocks rather than attention blocks, and is associated with neurons that act as a gating mechanism for harmful generation. The results indicate that the early layers in the model are used for a contextual understanding of harmfulness in a prompt, which is then propagated through the model, to generate harmfulness in the late layers, as well as a signal indicating harmfulness through MLP blocks. This is then further propagated to the last layer of the model, specifically to a sparse set of neurons, which receives the signal and determines the generation of harmful content accordingly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型为何会产生有害内容？
大型语言模型（LLMs）已被证明会生成有害内容，但其背后的原因尚未被充分探索。我们提出一种基于因果中介分析的方法，以识别导致有害生成的因果因素。该方法在模型层、模块（MLP和注意力块）及单个神经元层面进行多粒度分析。在当前最先进的LLMs上进行的大量实验表明，有害生成主要发生在模型后期层，主要源于MLP模块而非注意力模块的失效，并与充当有害生成闸门机制的神经元相关。结果表明，模型早期层用于理解提示中的有害性上下文，该信息随后通过模型传播至后期层，生成有害内容，同时通过MLP模块传递有害性信号，最终传播至模型最后一层，特别是稀疏的一组神经元，接收该信号并据此决定生成有害内容。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why large language models generate harmful content by applying causal mediation analysis across model layers, modules, and neurons. The motivation stems from the lack of understanding about the causal mechanisms behind such behavior. The method identifies where and how harmful signals propagate through the model, revealing that harmful generation primarily originates in later layers, driven by failures in MLP blocks rather than attention blocks, and is mediated by a sparse set of neurons acting as gatekeepers. Experimental results across state-of-the-art LLMs show that early layers encode contextual understanding of harmful prompts, which is then amplified through MLP blocks and ultimately determined by specific neurons in the final layer.
本研究通过因果中介分析方法，探究大语言模型生成有害内容的深层原因。研究动机在于当前对有害输出的因果机制缺乏系统理解。方法上，该研究在模型层、模块（MLP与注意力块）及单个神经元层面进行多粒度分析，发现有害生成主要发生在模型后期层，由MLP模块的失效主导而非注意力模块，且与一组稀疏神经元相关，这些神经元作为有害生成的“门控机制”。实验表明，早期层负责识别提示中的有害语境，该信号经MLP模块放大后传递至最终层，由特定神经元决定输出是否有害。
Hidden Failures in Robustness: Why Supervised Uncertainty Quantification Needs Better Evaluation
Authors: Joe Stacey, Hadas Orgad, Kentaro Inui, Benjamin Heinzerling, Nafise Sadat Moosavi
First: 2026-04-13T16:11:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:11:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work has shown that the hidden states of large language models contain signals useful for uncertainty estimation and hallucination detection, motivating a growing interest in efficient probe-based approaches. Yet it remains unclear how robust existing methods are, and which probe designs provide uncertainty estimates that are reliable under distribution shift. We present a systematic study of supervised uncertainty probes across models, tasks, and OOD settings, training over 2,000 probes while varying the representation layer, feature type, and token aggregation strategy. Our evaluation highlights poor robustness in current methods, particularly in the case of long-form generations. We also find that probe robustness is driven less by architecture and more by the probe inputs. Middle-layer representations generalise more reliably than final-layer hidden states, and aggregating across response tokens is consistently more robust than relying on single-token features. These differences are often largely invisible in-distribution but become more important under distribution shift. Informed by our evaluation, we explore a simple hybrid back-off strategy for improving robustness, arguing that better evaluation is a prerequisite for building more robust probes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒性中的隐藏失效：为何监督式不确定性量化需要更好的评估
近期研究表明，大语言模型的隐藏状态包含可用于不确定性估计和幻觉检测的信号，从而推动了高效探针方法的研究兴趣。然而，现有方法的鲁棒性尚不明确，哪些探针设计能在分布偏移下提供可靠的不确定性估计仍待探索。我们系统性地研究了不同模型、任务和OOD设置下的监督式不确定性探针，训练了2000多个探针，同时变化表示层、特征类型和标记聚合策略。评估结果表明，当前方法鲁棒性较差，尤其在长文本生成场景中。我们发现，探针的鲁棒性更多取决于输入而非架构；中间层表示比最终层隐藏状态更可靠，跨响应标记聚合比依赖单标记特征更鲁棒。这些差异在分布内场景中往往不明显，但在分布偏移下变得更为重要。基于评估结果，我们探索了一种简单的混合回退策略以提升鲁棒性，并认为更好的评估是构建更鲁棒探针的前提。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of hidden states in large language models for uncertainty estimation, this study systematically evaluates supervised uncertainty probes across multiple models, tasks, and out-of-distribution settings, training over 2,000 probes with varied layer, feature, and aggregation choices. Results reveal poor robustness in current methods, especially for long-form generations, and show that probe reliability is more influenced by input representation and token aggregation than model architecture. Middle-layer representations and token-aggregated features consistently outperform final-layer states and single-token features, with these differences becoming critical under distribution shift. The authors propose a simple hybrid back-off strategy to improve robustness, emphasizing that better evaluation is essential for developing reliable uncertainty probes.
鉴于大语言模型隐藏状态在不确定性估计中的潜力，本研究系统评估了监督不确定性探针在不同模型、任务和分布外场景下的表现，训练了2000多个探针，调整了层、特征和聚合策略。结果表明当前方法鲁棒性差，尤其在长文本生成中表现不佳，中间层表示和跨token聚合特征显著优于最终层隐藏状态和单token特征。这些差异在分布内场景中常不明显，但在分布外场景中至关重要，作者据此提出简单混合回退策略，并强调改进评估是构建更可靠探针的前提。
Towards Autonomous Mechanistic Reasoning in Virtual Cells
Authors: Yunhui Jang, Lu Zhu, Jake Fawkes, Alisandra Kaye Denton, Dominique Beaini, Emmanuel Noutahi
First: 2026-04-13T16:10:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:10:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have recently gained significant attention as a promising approach to accelerate scientific discovery. However, their application in open-ended scientific domains such as biology remains limited, primarily due to the lack of factually grounded and actionable explanations. To address this, we introduce a structured explanation formalism for virtual cells that represents biological reasoning as mechanistic action graphs, enabling systematic verification and falsification. Building upon this, we propose VCR-Agent, a multi-agent framework that integrates biologically grounded knowledge retrieval with a verifier-based filtering approach to generate and validate mechanistic reasoning autonomously. Using this framework, we release VC-TRACES dataset, which consists of verified mechanistic explanations derived from the Tahoe-100M atlas. Empirically, we demonstrate that training with these explanations improves factual precision and provides a more effective supervision signal for downstream gene expression prediction. These results underscore the importance of reliable mechanistic reasoning for virtual cells, achieved through the synergy of multi-agent and rigorous verification.

为解决生物领域缺乏事实依据的推理问题，该研究提出将生物机制形式化为动作图谱，以支持系统性验证。其核心方法VCR-Agent是一个多智能体框架，结合知识检索与验证过滤机制，实现机制推理的自主生成与验证。实验基于新发布的VC-TRACES数据集（源自Tahoe-100M图谱），结果表明，使用经验证的机制解释训练模型可显著提升事实准确性，并有效改善下游基因表达预测性能。
XD-MAP: Cross-Modal Domain Adaptation via Semantic Parametric Maps for Scalable Training Data Generation
Authors: Frank Bieder, Hendrik Königshof, Haohao Hu, Fabian Immel, Yinzhe Shen, Jan-Hendrik Pauls, Christoph Stiller
First: 2026-01-20T21:00:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:09:40+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables, accepted at CVPRW
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Until open-world foundation models match the performance of specialized approaches, deep learning systems remain dependent on task- and sensor-specific data availability. To bridge the gap between available datasets and deployment domains, domain adaptation strategies are widely used. In this work, we propose XD-MAP, a novel approach to transfer sensor-specific knowledge from an image dataset to LiDAR, an entirely different sensing domain. Our method leverages detections on camera images to create a semantic parametric map. The map elements are modeled to produce pseudo labels in the target domain without any manual annotation effort. Unlike previous domain transfer approaches, our method does not require direct overlap between sensors and enables extending the angular perception range from a front-view camera to a full 360° view. On our large-scale road feature dataset, XD-MAP outperforms single shot baseline approaches by +19.5 mIoU for 2D semantic segmentation, +19.5 PQth for 2D panoptic segmentation, and +32.3 mIoU in 3D semantic segmentation. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach achieving strong performance on LiDAR data without any manual labeling.

在开放世界基础模型达到专用方法性能之前，深度学习系统仍依赖于任务和传感器特定的数据可用性。为弥合现有数据集与部署域之间的差距，域自适应策略被广泛应用。本文提出XD-MAP，一种新颖方法，可将图像数据集中的传感器特定知识迁移到完全不同的感知域——激光雷达。我们的方法利用摄像头图像上的检测结果生成语义参数化地图，地图元素被建模以在目标域中生成伪标签，无需任何人工标注。与以往域迁移方法不同，我们的方法无需传感器直接重叠，且可将视角从前视摄像头扩展至全360°。在大规模道路特征数据集上，XD-MAP在2D语义分割中比单次采样基线方法提升+19.5 mIoU，在2D全景分割中提升+19.5 PQth，在3D语义分割中提升+32.3 mIoU。结果表明，我们的方法无需人工标注即可在激光雷达数据上实现优异性能。

To address the challenge of limited sensor-specific data for deploying deep learning systems, this work introduces XD-MAP, a method that transfers knowledge from image-based datasets to LiDAR data without manual annotation. It constructs a semantic parametric map using camera detections to generate pseudo labels in the LiDAR domain, enabling domain adaptation without requiring sensor overlap or manual labeling. GPU Acceleration of Sparse Fully Homomorphic Encrypted DNNs
Authors: Lara D'Agata, Carlos Agulló-Domingo, Óscar Vera-López, Kaustubh Shivdikar, Ardhi W. B. Yudha, Ferhat Yaman, David Kaeli, José L. Abellán, Ian Colbert, José Cano
First: 2026-04-13T16:09:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:09:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 6th Workshop on Machine Learning and Systems (EuroMLSys) co-located with EuroSys '26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) has recently attracted significant attention as both a cryptographic primitive and a systems challenge. Given the latest advances in accelerated computing, FHE presents a promising opportunity for progress, with applications ranging from machine learning to information security. We target the most computationally intensive operation in deep neural networks from a hardware perspective, matrix multiplication (matmul), and adapt it for execution on AMD GPUs. We propose a new optimized method that improves the runtime and complexity of ciphertext matmul by using FIDESlib, a recent open-source FHE library designed specifically for GPUs. By exploiting sparsity in both operands, our sparse matmul implementation outperforms its CPU counterpart by up to $3.0\times$ and reduces the time complexity from cubic to semi-linear, demonstrating an improvement over existing FHE matmul implementations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GPU加速稀疏全同态加密深度神经网络
全同态加密（FHE）近期作为密码学原语和系统挑战备受关注。鉴于加速计算的最新进展，FHE为机器学习到信息安全等多个领域提供了广阔前景。我们从硬件角度聚焦深度神经网络中最耗时的操作——矩阵乘法（matmul），并将其适配于AMD GPU执行。我们提出一种新优化方法，利用专为GPU设计的开源FHE库FIDESlib，提升密文矩阵乘法的运行时间和复杂度。通过利用两个操作数的稀疏性，我们的稀疏矩阵乘法实现相比CPU版本最高提升3.0倍，并将时间复杂度从立方级降至半线性级，优于现有FHE矩阵乘法实现。
