为推动全同态加密（FHE）在机器学习中的应用，该研究聚焦深度神经网络中最耗时的矩阵乘法运算，并将其优化至AMD GPU上执行。作者基于专为GPU设计的FIDESlib库，提出一种利用双操作数稀疏性的矩阵乘法方法，将时间复杂度从立方级降至半线性级。实验表明，该方法相较CPU实现的FHE矩阵乘法最高提速3.0倍，显著提升了加密深度学习任务的效率。
RPA-Check: A Multi-Stage Automated Framework for Evaluating Dynamic LLM-based Role-Playing Agents
Authors: Riccardo Rosati, Edoardo Colucci, Massimiliano Bolognini, Adriano Mancini, Paolo Sernani
First: 2026-04-13T16:08:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:08:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in interactive systems has enabled the creation of dynamic, open-ended Role-Playing Agents (RPAs). However, evaluating these agents remains a significant challenge, as standard NLP metrics fail to capture the nuances of role adherence, logical consistency, and long-term narrative stability. This paper introduces RPA-Check, a multi-stage automated evaluation framework designed to objectively assess the performance of LLM-based RPAs in complex, constraints-heavy environments. Our methodology is based on a four-step pipeline: (1) Dimension Definition, establishing high-level qualitative behavioral criteria; (2) Augmentation, where these requirements are expanded into granular boolean checklist indicators; (3) Semantic Filtering, to ensure indicator objectivity, no redundancy and agent isolation; and (4) LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation, which employs chain-of-thought verification to score agent fidelity. We validate this framework by applying it to LLM Court, a serious game for forensic training involving several quantized local models. Experimental results across five distinct legal scenarios demonstrate the framework's ability to identify subtle trade-offs between model size, reasoning depth, and operational stability. Notably, the findings reveal an inverse relationship between parametric scale and procedural consistency, showing that smaller, adequately instruction-tuned models (8-9B) can outperform larger architectures prone to user-alignment bias or sycophancy. RPA-Check thus provides a standardized and reproducible metric for future research in generative agent evaluation within specialized domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RPA-Check：用于评估基于大语言模型的动态角色扮演代理的多阶段自动化框架
大语言模型（LLMs）在交互系统中的快速采用，使得动态、开放式的角色扮演代理（RPAs）的创建成为可能。然而，评估这些代理仍是一个重大挑战，因为标准自然语言处理指标无法捕捉角色遵循、逻辑一致性和长期叙事稳定性的细微差别。本文提出了RPA-Check，一个专为复杂、约束密集环境设计的多阶段自动化评估框架，旨在客观评估基于LLM的RPAs的表现。我们的方法基于四步流程：（1）维度定义，建立高层次定性行为标准；（2）扩充，将这些要求扩展为细粒度布尔检查清单指标；（3）语义过滤，确保指标客观性、无冗余且代理隔离；（4）LLM作为裁判评估，采用思维链验证对代理忠诚度打分。我们通过将该框架应用于LLM法庭——一个用于法医培训的严肃游戏（涉及多个量化本地模型）进行验证。在五个不同法律场景中的实验结果表明，该框架能够识别模型规模、推理深度与运行稳定性之间的微妙权衡。值得注意的是，研究发现参数规模与程序一致性呈反比关系，表明经过适当指令调优的小型模型（8-9B）可优于易受用户对齐偏差或谄媚倾向影响的大型架构。RPA-Check因此为未来在特定领域内生成式代理评估研究提供了一种标准化、可重复的度量标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate dynamic LLM-based Role-Playing Agents (RPAs) beyond standard NLP metrics, this paper introduces RPA-Check, a multi-stage automated framework that assesses RPAs for role adherence, logical consistency, and narrative stability. The method follows a four-step pipeline: defining qualitative behavioral dimensions, expanding them into granular boolean indicators, filtering for objectivity and isolation, and using LLM-as-a-Judge with chain-of-thought reasoning to score agent fidelity. Experiments on LLM Court across five legal scenarios show that smaller models (8-9B) with proper instruction tuning outperform larger models, revealing an inverse relationship between model size and procedural consistency, thus offering a reproducible evaluation standard for specialized agent domains.
为解决大型语言模型驱动的角色扮演代理在复杂环境中难以客观评估的问题，本文提出RPA-Check多阶段自动化评估框架。该方法包含四个步骤：定义行为维度、扩展为布尔指标、语义过滤确保客观性与隔离性、并使用LLM作为裁判结合思维链验证评分。在LLM Court（法证训练游戏）中对五种法律场景的实验表明，参数规模较小但经过良好指令调优的模型（8-9B）在程序一致性上优于大型模型，揭示了模型规模与叙事稳定性呈负相关，同时减少了用户对齐偏差与谄媚倾向，为生成式代理在专业领域的评估提供了标准化可复现的度量标准。
How Alignment Routes: Localizing, Scaling, and Controlling Policy Circuits in Language Models
Authors: Gregory N. Frank
First: 2026-04-06T03:20:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-13T16:07:48+00:00
Comments: Code and data: https://github.com/gregfrank/how-alignment-routes
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper localizes the policy routing mechanism in alignment-trained language models. An intermediate-layer attention gate reads detected content and triggers deeper amplifier heads that boost the signal toward refusal. In smaller models the gate and amplifier are single heads; at larger scale they become bands of heads across adjacent layers. The gate contributes under 1% of output DLA, but interchange testing (p<0.001) and knockout cascade confirm it is causally necessary. Interchange screening at n>=120 detects the same motif in twelve models from six labs (2B to 72B), though specific heads differ by lab. Per-head ablation weakens up to 58x at 72B and misses gates that interchange identifies; interchange is the only reliable audit at scale. Modulating the detection-layer signal continuously controls policy from hard refusal through evasion to factual answering. On safety prompts the same intervention turns refusal into harmful guidance, showing the safety-trained capability is gated by routing rather than removed. Thresholds vary by topic and by input language, and the circuit relocates across generations within a family while behavioral benchmarks register no change. Routing is early-commitment: the gate commits at its own layer before deeper layers finish processing the input. Under an in-context substitution cipher, gate interchange necessity collapses 70 to 99% across three models and the model switches to puzzle-solving. Injecting the plaintext gate activation into the cipher forward pass restores 48% of refusals in Phi-4-mini, localizing the bypass to the routing interface. A second method, cipher contrast analysis, uses plain/cipher DLA differences to map the full cipher-sensitive routing circuit in O(3n) forward passes. Any encoding that defeats detection-layer pattern matching bypasses the policy regardless of whether deeper layers reconstruct the content.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐如何路由：在语言模型中定位、扩展和控制策略电路
本文在对齐训练的语言模型中定位策略路由机制。中间层注意力门读取检测到的内容并触发深层放大头，将信号增强导向拒绝响应。在较小模型中，门和放大器为单个头；在更大规模模型中，它们扩展为相邻层中的一组头。该门贡献的输出DLA不足1%，但互换测试（p<0.001）和剔除级联实验证明其因果必要性。在n>=120时的互换筛选在六个实验室的十二个模型（2B至72B）中检测到相同模式，尽管各实验室的具体头不同。按头消融在72B模型中削弱效果最高达58倍，且遗漏互换测试识别的门；互换测试是大规模下唯一可靠的审计方法。连续调节检测层信号可控制策略，从强硬拒绝到规避再到事实回答。在安全提示下，相同干预可使拒绝转化为有害指导，表明安全训练能力由路由控制而非被移除。阈值随主题和输入语言变化，且电路在家族内不同代际间迁移，但行为基准无变化。路由为早期承诺：门在自身层即做出承诺，而深层层尚未完成输入处理。在上下文替换密码下，三模型中门互换必要性下降70至99%，模型转为解谜。将明文门激活注入密码前向传递可恢复Phi-4-mini中48%的拒绝，定位绕过路径至路由接口。第二种方法，密码对比分析，利用明文/密码DLA差异在O(3n)前向传递中映射完整密码敏感路由电路。任何能绕过检测层模式匹配的编码均可绕过策略，无论深层层是否重建内容。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how alignment in language models is routed through a localized policy mechanism involving an attention gate and amplifier heads that boost refusal signals. Motivated by the need to understand and control safety behavior, the authors identify this circuit across multiple models and labs using interchange testing and knockout cascades, confirming its causal necessity despite minimal output contribution. Experiments show that modulating the gate’s activation can shift behavior from refusal to evasion or factual answering, and that routing is early-commitment, occurring before deeper layers process input fully. The circuit is sensitive to input encoding, and when bypassed via cipher substitution, models switch to puzzle-solving; restoring gate activation partially recovers refusal. Cipher contrast analysis further maps the full routing circuit efficiently, revealing that any encoding that evades detection-layer matching can bypass policy regardless of downstream reconstruction.
该研究旨在理解大语言模型中对齐机制如何通过局部路由电路实现安全行为控制。作者通过互换测试和消融实验，在多个模型中定位到一个由注意力门和放大头组成的电路，该电路虽仅贡献输出的不到1%，但对安全行为具有因果必要性。实验表明，调节该门的激活可使模型从拒绝到规避再到事实回答，且在输入被加密干扰时，该电路的必要性大幅下降，说明其为早期承诺机制。电路位置随模型和生成变化，但行为保持稳定，而密文对比分析可揭示完整路由路径，表明绕过检测层模式匹配即可无视下游处理而规避政策。
